# 《饥荒联机版》游戏玩法系统（中文详尽版）

> **原文对应**：`02-gameplay-systems.md`（英文原报告，共 4168 行）
> **源码根目录**：`C:\Users\35916\.qwenworkcn\workspace\mv38nal4gdn29zna\dst-analysis\scripts\scripts\`
> **本文所有路径均相对该根目录。**
> **本版说明**：完整译制 + 深度扩写。扩写部分以 `> **【深度扩写】**` 标注，并在末尾追加 `【移植指引】` 与 `【实现清单】` 两章。
> **术语依据**：`00-注释规范与术语表.md`。

---

## 概述 / TL;DR

《饥荒联机版》（Don't Starve Together, DST）的玩法层可以用一句话概括：**所有生存压力最终都汇入一次 `health:DoDelta(负值)` 调用，而除生命值外的一切数值条都只是"生成伤害的方式"加上"非致命的副作用"。**

十个要点：

1. **四条数值条，一个终点。** 生命值（health）是唯一的*终局*数值。饥饿度（hunger）、理智值（sanity）、体温（temperature）、湿度（moisture）都只是不同的"扣血途径"，外加非致命后果（影子生物刷出、屏幕特效、工具打滑）。
2. **万物皆 `DoDelta`。** 生命、饥饿、理智、湿度甚至护甲耐久都暴露 `DoDelta` / `SetPercent` / `GetPercent`。学会一个等于学会五个。
3. **是速率，不是计时器。** 各数值条在 1 秒周期（`hunger`）或每帧（`sanity`、`temperature`、`moisture`）的 `OnUpdate(dt)` 中推进。组件先从世界算出*速率*，再做 `速率 × dt` 积分。这让暂停、快进、离线追赶（`LongUpdate`）几乎免费。
4. **`SourceModifierList` 是模组生态的骨架。** 游戏里几乎所有"×倍率"都是一个带来源追踪、能自动清理的修饰器袋（`util/sourcemodifierlist.lua`），折叠方式是 `multiply` / `additive` / `boolean`。**先抄这个模式。**
5. **标签就是查询语言。** `sanityaura`、`HASHEATER`、`prototyper`、`edible_MEAT`、`playerghost`、`heavy`、`_combat`。空间查询是 `TheSim:FindEntities(x,y,z,radius, mustTags, cantTags)`，玩法规则是 `inst:HasTag(...)`。游戏里**没有**"这是不是食物"的类继承体系。
6. **事件解耦系统。** 组件 `PushEvent("healthdelta" | "hungerdelta" | "sanitydelta" | "temperaturedelta" | "moisturedelta" | "oneat" | "equip" | "attacked" | "death")`，任何人都能订阅。`healthtrigger.lua` 是一个 41 行的通用阈值穿越响应器，完全构建在 `healthdelta` 之上。
7. **护甲是百分比吸收 + 共享耐久池**，计算发生在 `inventory:ApplyDamage`，**不在** `combat.lua` 或 `health.lua`。
8. **制作是科技向量比较。** 配方声明 `{SCIENCE=2}`；原型机声明 `{SCIENCE=2, MAGIC=1}`；`CanPrototypeRecipe` 是逐轴 `<=`。解锁是永久的（名字进列表），原型制造是位置性的（必须在机器 4 单位内）。
9. **烹饪是"标签加权约束匹配 + 优先级分层 + 加权随机"**，不是查表。`wetgoop` 是一条 `test = function() return true end`、`priority = -10` 的兜底项。
10. **服务器/客户端通过 `replica` 分离。** 每个玩法组件都有一个 `*_replica.lua` 镜像，只持有可同步的标量。目标判定规则（`IsValidTarget`、`CanBeAlly`、`CanBeAttacked`）放在*副本*里，好让客户端能预测。

## 0. 范围更正（原文开篇声明）

> 任务简报把 `clothing.lua` 和 `beefalo_clothing.lua` 当作服装/装备玩法文件。**它们不是。** 两者都是自动生成的外观皮肤数据库（`-- AUTOGENERATED CODE BY export_accountitems.lua`），只包含 `type` / `skin_tags` / `symbol_overrides` / `rarity` / `release_group` 条目——**没有保暖值、没有护甲、没有槽位**。
>
> 真正的服装玩法在 `components/equippable.lua`、`components/insulator.lua`、`components/waterproofer.lua`、`components/armor.lua`、`equipslotutil.lua`，以及服装预制体（`prefabs/hats.lua`、`prefabs/armor_wood.lua` 等）。同理，也不存在 `components/tameable.lua`；驯服是 `components/domesticatable.lua`。这里明确点出，而不是凭空编造。

## 1. 四条生存数值条——它们如何互相影响

```
                      ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                      │                     WORLD STATE                      │
                      │  TheWorld.state.temperature / israining /            │
                      │  precipitationrate / isday / isnight / season        │
                      └───────┬───────────────┬───────────────┬──────────────┘
                              │               │               │
             GetTemperatureAtXZ(x,z)   precipitationrate   LightWatcher:GetLightValue()
                              │               │               │
                              ▼               ▼               ▼
        ┌─────────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌────────────┐
        │ TEMPERATURE │◀─┤ MOISTURE │  │    SANITY    │  │   HUNGER   │
        │ temp.lua    │  │ moist.lua│  │  sanity.lua  │  │ hunger.lua │
        │ OnUpdate(dt)│  │OnUpdt(dt)│  │  Recalc(dt)  │  │  DoDec(dt)  │
        └──────┬──────┘  └────┬─────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └─────┬──────┘
               │              │               │                │
   current<0 ──┤              │  ─────────────┤                │ current<=0
   current>70 ─┤              │               │                │
               │         GetMoisturePenalty   │                │
               │         = -Lerp(0,30,wet%)   │                │
               │◀─────────────┘               │                │
               │              │               │                │
               │              │  inSine easing│                │
               │              └──────────────▶│                │
               │         MOISTURE_SANITY_     │                │
               │         PENALTY_MAX          │                │
               │                              │                │
               ▼                              ▼                ▼
   health:DoDelta(-1.25*dt,"cold")   sanity < 15% ──────▶ health:DoDelta(-hurtrate*dt,
   health:DoDelta(-1.25*dt,"hot")         │                        "hunger")
               │                          ▼                        │
               │              shadowcreaturespawner.lua            │
               │              population target by sanity%:        │
               │              >50%→0, >10%→1, else→2               │
               │                          │                        │
               │                          ▼                        │
               │              SpawnPrefab("terrorbeak"             │
               │                 or "crawlinghorror")              │
               │                          │                        │
               │                          ▼                        │
               └───────────────▶  combat:GetAttacked  ◀────────────┘
                                          │
                                          ▼
                                ┌───────────────────┐
                                │      HEALTH       │
                                │   health.lua      │
                                │ DoDelta → SetVal  │
                                │ ≤0 → "death"      │
                                └─────────┬─────────┘
                                          │
                       ┌──────────────────┴───────────────────┐
                       ▼                                      ▼
              non-player: ErodeAway                  player: "playerghost"
              after destroytime (2s)                 needs "reviver" item
                                                     → DeltaPenalty(0.25)
```

**跨数值条耦合关系（逐条落实）：**

| 源 | 目标 | 机制 | 代码位置 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 湿度 | 体温 | 全身湿透时环境温度最多再降 30 °C | `temperature.lua:GetMoisturePenalty()` |
| 湿度 | 理智 | 带缓动的持续流失，上限 `MOISTURE_SANITY_PENALTY_MAX = -100/(day_time*6)` | `sanity.lua:Recalc()` 第 526 行 |
| 潮湿 | 取暖效率 | 湿着烤火，取暖效率差 25% | `WET_HEAT_FACTOR_PENALTY = 0.75` |
| 潮湿 | 电击 | `ELECTRIC_WET_DAMAGE_MULT × target:GetWetMultiplier()` | `combat.lua:DoAttack()` 第 1184 行 |
| 潮湿 | 易腐品 | `PERISH_WET_MULT = 1.3` | `perishable.lua` 第 108 行 |
| 潮湿 | 装备 | 每件湿装备 −0.1 风度；湿度 ≥4/5 格时工具打滑 | `equippable.lua:GetDapperness`、`player_common.lua:DropWetTool` |
| 饥饿=0 | 生命 | `hurtrate = 150/120 = 1.25 hp/s` | `hunger.lua:DoDec`、`STARVE_KILL_TIME = 120` |
| 体温<0 / >70 | 生命 | `hurtrate = WILSON_HEALTH/FREEZING_KILL_TIME = 1.25 hp/s` | `temperature.lua:OnUpdate()` 第 517-523 行 |
| 理智<15% | 战斗 | 影子生物刷出并攻击 | `shadowcreaturespawner.lua` |
| 生命 | 战斗 | `panic_thresh` → `combat._ispanic` 封锁目标选择 | `health.lua:onpercent`、`combat_replica.lua:CanTarget` |
| 生命 | 制作 | `MAX_HEALTH` 类材料是永久 0–75% 惩罚 | `builder.lua:RemoveIngredients` |

**基准数值**（`tuning.lua`，`Tune()` 局部变量，第 18-42 行）：

```lua
local seg_time = 30                       -- 每个"节段"30 秒
local total_day_time = seg_time*16        -- 480 秒 = 一整天 8 分钟
local day_segs, dusk_segs, night_segs = 10, 4, 2

local wilson_attack = 34                  -- 玩家徒手伤害
local wilson_health = 150
local wilson_hunger = 150                 -- "胃容量"
local wilson_sanity = 200
local calories_per_day = 75
local wilson_attack_period = 0.4

local multiplayer_armor_durability_modifier = 0.7
local multiplayer_armor_absorption_modifier = 1
```

`MAXITEMSLOTS = 15`（`constants.lua:650`），`EQUIPSLOTS = {HANDS, HEAD, BODY, BEARD}`（`constants.lua:652`）。

> **【深度扩写】** 上面这张"日/黄昏/夜"的节段划分是全部数值的地基，所有"每天"的换算都由它派生。展开见 §E-1《节段（SEG_TIME）制数值体系：为什么所有常量都写成 `100/(day_time*N)`》。

## 2. 生命值 —— `components/health.lua`（665 行）

### 2.1 状态字段

```lua
-- components/health.lua:62
local Health = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.inst = inst
    self.maxhealth = 100
    self.minhealth = 0
    self.currenthealth = self.maxhealth
    self.invincible = false
    ...
    self.penalty = 0
    self.disable_penalty = not TUNING.HEALTH_PENALTY_ENABLED

    self.absorb = 0 -- DEPRECATED, please use externalabsorbmodifiers instead
    self.playerabsorb = 0 -- DEPRECATED, please use externalabsorbmodifiers instead

    self.externalabsorbmodifiers = SourceModifierList(inst, 0, SourceModifierList.additive)
    self.externalreductionmodifiers = SourceModifierList(inst, 0, SourceModifierList.additive)

    self.destroytime = nil
    self.canmurder = true
    self.canheal = true

    self.nonlethal_temperature = TUNING.NONLETHAL_TEMPERATURE
    self.nonlethal_hunger = TUNING.NONLETHAL_HUNGER
    self.nonlethal_pct = TUNING.NONLETHAL_PERCENT
end, nil, { maxhealth = onmaxhealth, currenthealth = oncurrenthealth, ... })
```

注意 `Class(...)` 的第三个参数：**属性设置器表**。给 `self.currenthealth = x` 赋值会透明地触发 `oncurrenthealth`，后者把值推到 `self.inst.replica.health` 做网络同步。**一张表换来免费的网络脏标记同步。**

### 2.2 `DoDelta` —— 伤害/治疗管线

```lua
-- components/health.lua:613
function Health:DoDelta(amount, overtime, cause, ignore_invincible, afflicter, ignore_absorb)
    local old_percent = self:GetPercent()

    if old_percent <= self.nonlethal_pct and
        (((cause == "cold" or cause == "hot") and self.nonlethal_temperature) or
        (cause == "hunger" and self.nonlethal_hunger)) then
        return 0
    end

    if self.redirect ~= nil and self.redirect(self.inst, amount, overtime, cause, ignore_invincible, afflicter, ignore_absorb) then
        return 0
    elseif not ignore_invincible and (self:IsInvincible() or self.inst.is_teleporting) then
        return 0
    elseif amount < 0 and not ignore_absorb then
        amount = amount * math.clamp(1 - (self.playerabsorb ~= 0 and afflicter ~= nil and afflicter:HasTag("player") and self.playerabsorb + self.absorb or self.absorb), 0, 1) * math.max(1 - self.externalabsorbmodifiers:Get(), 0)
        amount = afflicter ~= nil and math.min(0, amount + self.externalreductionmodifiers:Get()) or amount
    end

    if self.deltamodifierfn ~= nil then
        amount = self.deltamodifierfn(self.inst, amount, overtime, cause, ignore_invincible, afflicter, ignore_absorb)
    end

    if self.maxdamagetakenperhit ~= nil and amount < self.maxdamagetakenperhit and not self._ignore_maxdamagetakenperhit then
        amount = self.maxdamagetakenperhit
    end

    self:SetVal(self.currenthealth + amount, cause, afflicter)

    self.inst:PushEvent("healthdelta", { oldpercent = old_percent, newpercent = self:GetPercent(), overtime = overtime, cause = cause, afflicter = afflicter, amount = amount })

    if self.ondelta ~= nil then
        self.ondelta(self.inst, old_percent, self:GetPercent(), overtime, cause, afflicter, amount)
    end
    return amount
end
```

**有序的七个阶段，值得逐字抄走：**

1. **非致死地板** —— 环境原因（`cold` / `hot` / `hunger`）在 `NONLETHAL_PERCENT = 0.2` 以下可以被设为不致命。普通模式下以 `false` 出厂（实测 `tuning.lua:6834-6837`：`NONLETHAL_TEMPERATURE = false`、`NONLETHAL_HUNGER = false`、`NONLETHAL_DARKNESS = false`、`NONLETHAL_PERCENT = 0.2`）；由游戏模式翻转。
2. **`redirect` 钩子** —— 一个函数指针，可以吞掉或改道整个 delta（用于骑乘、格挡）。
3. **无敌判定** —— `self.invincible or inst.sg:HasStateTag("temp_invincible")`，外加 `is_teleporting`。
4. **百分比吸收** —— 已废弃的扁平 `absorb` / `playerabsorb` 字段 *加上* 现代的 `externalabsorbmodifiers`（additive 型 `SourceModifierList`），然后再套一个**扁平**的 `externalreductionmodifiers`，以 `math.min(0, amount + reduction)` 应用，**保证它永远不会变成治疗**。
5. **`deltamodifierfn`** —— 最终的任意改写。
6. **`maxdamagetakenperhit`** —— 一个伤害**下限**（注意 `SetMaxDamageTakenPerHit` 里的符号翻转：它对正输入取负）。
7. **`SetVal`** —— 钳制、死亡检测、事件广播。

`DoDelta` 返回**已结算的**伤害值。`combat.lua` 依赖这个返回值：

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:653
damageresolved = self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-damage, nil, cause ~= nil and (cause.nameoverride or cause.prefab) or "NIL", nil, cause)
damageresolved = damageresolved ~= nil and -damageresolved or damage
```

### 2.3 `SetVal` —— 死亡在这里被检测，只此一次

```lua
-- components/health.lua:565
function Health:SetVal(val, cause, afflicter)
    local old_health = self.currenthealth
    local max_health = self:GetMaxWithPenalty()
    local min_health = math.min(self.minhealth or 0, max_health)

    self.inst:PushEvent("pre_health_setval", {val=val, old_health=old_health})

    if val > max_health then val = max_health end

    if val <= min_health then
        self.currenthealth = min_health
        self.inst:PushEvent("minhealth", { cause = cause, afflicter = afflicter })
    else
        self.currenthealth = val
    end

    if old_health > 0 and self.currenthealth <= 0 then
        self.causeofdeath = afflicter or nil
        local is_corpsing = CanEntityBecomeCorpse(self.inst)
        TheWorld:PushEvent("entity_death", { inst = self.inst, cause = cause, afflicter = afflicter, corpsing = is_corpsing })
        self.inst:PushEvent("death", { cause = cause, afflicter = afflicter, corpsing = is_corpsing })
        self.is_corpsing = is_corpsing
        ...
        if self:CanFadeOut() then
            self.inst:AddTag("NOCLICK")
            self.inst.persists = false
            self.inst.erode_task = self.inst:DoTaskInTime(self.destroytime or 2, ErodeAway)
        elseif self.is_corpsing then ...
        end
    end
end
```

两个设计要点：

- **世界事件先于实体事件触发，是刻意的** —— 源码注释写明*"the entity event may invalidate itself"*（带 `nofadeout` 的物品会在 `death` 里调 `:Remove()`）。
- **`minhealth`** 让一个实体可以被"打不死"，而不需要进入无敌状态；它推 `"minhealth"` 而不是 `"death"`。`combat.lua:ShouldAggro` 拒绝让 NPC 去仇恨这种目标，除非目标带 `hostile` 标签或是玩家。

### 2.4 生命惩罚（上限生命缩水）

```lua
-- components/health.lua:460
function Health:SetPenalty(penalty)
    if not self.disable_penalty then
        --Penalty should never be less than 0% or ever above 75%.
        self.penalty = math.clamp(penalty, 0, TUNING.MAXIMUM_HEALTH_PENALTY)
    end
end

function Health:GetMaxWithPenalty()
    return self.maxhealth - self.maxhealth * self.penalty
end
```

`MAXIMUM_HEALTH_PENALTY = 0.75`（实测 `tuning.lua:2161`）。这就是 DST 的复活代价：你复活后血条会**永久缩水**，直到有东西把它去掉（`components/maxhealer.lua` 负责 `DeltaPenalty(self.healamount)`）。相关的 `REVIVE_HEALTH_PENALTY = 0.25` 在 `tuning.lua:2169`。

### 2.5 两种回血 API

```lua
-- components/health.lua:328  （简单、单来源、向后兼容）
function Health:StartRegen(amount, period, interruptcurrentregen) ... end

-- components/health.lua:353  （推荐：按来源打键，来源移除时自动清理）
function Health:AddRegenSource(source, amount, period, key)
    ...
    self.regensources[source] = { tasks = { [key] = { task = self.inst:DoPeriodicTask(period, DoRegenFromSource, nil, self, amount, key), ... } } }
    if EntityScript.is_instance(source) then
        self.regensources[source].onremove = function(source) ... end
        self.inst:ListenForEvent("onremove", self.regensources[source].onremove, source)
    end
end
```

`DoRegenFromSource` 会把 `"regen_"..key` 作为 `cause` 传下去，让下游监听者能归因回血来源。第 327 行的注释写得很直白：*"Consider using Health:AddRegenSource, specially for players."*

### 2.6 火焰伤害 —— 一个带速率上限的子系统

```lua
-- components/health.lua:194
function Health:DoFireDamage(amount, doer, instant)
    local mult = self:GetFireDamageScale()
    ...
    --We're going to take damage at this point, so make sure it's not over the cap/second
    if self.firedamagecaptimer <= time then
        self.firedamageinlastsecond = 0
        self.firedamagecaptimer = time + 1
    end
    if self.firedamageinlastsecond + amount > TUNING.MAX_FIRE_DAMAGE_PER_SECOND then
        amount = TUNING.MAX_FIRE_DAMAGE_PER_SECOND - self.firedamageinlastsecond
    end
    self:DoDelta(-amount * mult, false, doer ~= nil and (doer.nameoverride or doer.prefab) or "fire", nil, doer)
```

`MAX_FIRE_DAMAGE_PER_SECOND = 120`。此外还有 `fire_timestart` 宽限期，以及一个 `takingfiredamagelow` 状态，用于在 `GetFireDamageScale()` 为 0 时驱动廉价 VFX 版的"微火"（`PushEvent("changefiredamage", {low = ...})`）。

### 2.7 `components/healthtrigger.lua` —— 41 行，纯模式

```lua
function HealthTrigger:AddTrigger(amount, fn)
    self.triggers[amount] = fn
end

function HealthTrigger:OnHealthDelta(data)
    local totrigger = {}
    for k, v in pairs(self.triggers) do
        if (data.oldpercent > k and data.newpercent <= k) or
           (data.oldpercent < k and data.newpercent >= k) then
            table.insert(totrigger, k)
        end
    end
    if #totrigger > 0 then
        table.sort(totrigger, data.oldpercent > data.newpercent and descending or nil)
        for i, v in ipairs(totrigger) do self.triggers[v](self.inst) end
    end
end
```

双向阈值穿越，并做排序，使得当生命值一帧内暴跌穿过多个阈值时，按**高→低**顺序触发。除 `healthdelta` 的载荷外，与任何东西零耦合。

### 2.8 复活

```lua
-- prefabs/player_common.lua:341
local function OnGetItem(inst, giver, item)
    if item ~= nil and item:HasTag("reviver") and inst:HasTag("playerghost") then
        local dohealthpenalty = not item:HasTag("noreviverhealthpenalty")
        ...
        item:PushEvent("usereviver", { user = giver })
        giver.hasRevivedPlayer = true
        item:Remove()
        inst:PushEvent("respawnfromghost", { source = item, user = giver })
        if dohealthpenalty then
            inst.components.health:DeltaPenalty(TUNING.REVIVE_HEALTH_PENALTY)   -- 0.25
        end
        giver.components.sanity:DoDelta(TUNING.REVIVE_OTHER_SANITY_BONUS)
```

而鬼魂只能接受复活道具：

```lua
-- prefabs/player_common.lua:333
local function ShouldAcceptItem(inst, item)
    if inst:HasTag("playerghost") then
        return item:HasTag("reviver") and inst:IsOnPassablePoint()
    else
        return item.components.inventoryitem ~= nil
    end
end
```

## 3. 护甲 —— `components/armor.lua` + `inventory:ApplyDamage`

### 3.1 护甲物品本体

```lua
-- components/armor.lua:39
function Armor:InitCondition(amount, absorb_percent)
    self.condition = amount
    self.absorb_percent = absorb_percent
    self.maxcondition = amount
end

function Armor:GetAbsorption(attacker, weapon)
    return self:CanResist(attacker, weapon) and self.absorb_percent or nil
end

function Armor:TakeDamage(damage_amount)
    damage_amount = damage_amount * self.conditionlossmultipliers:Get()
    self:SetCondition(self.condition - damage_amount)
    if self.ontakedamage ~= nil then self.ontakedamage(self.inst, damage_amount) end
    self.inst:PushEvent("armordamaged", damage_amount)
end
```

`CanResist` 实现的是**伤害类型门控**：`self.tags` 是一个列表；若攻击者或武器命中其中任一标签，护甲生效；否则 `GetAbsorption` 返回 `nil`，护甲完全不做事。`AddWeakness(tag, bonus_damage)` 是反向操作——被带标签的攻击者额外造成固定耐久伤害。

真实数值（`tuning.lua:1814`，配合 `multiplayer_armor_durability_modifier = 0.7`、`wilson_health = 150`）：

| 物品 | 耐久 | 吸收率 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 草甲 | 150×1.5×0.7 = **157.5** | **0.60** | |
| 木甲 | 150×3×0.7 = **315** | **0.80** | |
| 橄榄球头盔 | **315** | **0.80** | 头部槽——与木甲搭配 |
| 大理石甲 | 150×7×0.7 = **735** | **0.95** | `ARMORMARBLE_SLOW = 0.7` 减速惩罚 |
| 石化甲（Thulecite） | 150×12×0.7 = **1260** | **0.90** | |
| 蜂帽 | 150×10×0.7 = **1050** | **0.80** | |
| 暗夜护甲 | 150×5×0.7 = **525** | **0.95** | 改为烧理智：`ARMOR_SANITY_DMG_AS_SANITY = 0.10` |

### 3.2 吸收数学 —— 这是大多数人搞错的部分

```lua
-- components/inventory.lua:408
function Inventory:ApplyDamage(damage, attacker, weapon, spdamage)
    local absorbers = {}
    local damagetypemult = 1
    for slotid = 1, EquipSlot.Count() do
        local slotname = EquipSlot.FromID(slotid)
        local v = self.equipslots[slotname]
        if v then
            if v.components.resistance ~= nil and v.components.resistance:HasResistance(attacker, weapon)
               and v.components.resistance:ShouldResistDamage() then
                v.components.resistance:ResistDamage(damage, attacker)
                return 0, nil                       -- 完全免疫短路
            elseif v.components.armor ~= nil then
                absorbers[v.components.armor] = v.components.armor:GetAbsorption(attacker, weapon)
            end
            if v.components.damagetyperesist ~= nil then
                damagetypemult = damagetypemult * v.components.damagetyperesist:GetResist(attacker, weapon)
            end
        end
    end

    damage = damage * damagetypemult

    local absorbed_percent = 0
    local total_absorption = 0
    for armor, amt in pairs(absorbers) do
        absorbed_percent = math.max(amt, absorbed_percent)   -- 取 MAX，不求和
        total_absorption = total_absorption + amt            -- 求和，用于拆分耐久
    end

    local absorbed_damage = damage * absorbed_percent
    local leftover_damage = damage - absorbed_damage

    local armor_damage = {}
    if total_absorption > 0 then
        for armor, amt in pairs(absorbers) do
            armor_damage[armor] = absorbed_damage * amt / total_absorption + armor:GetBonusDamage(attacker, weapon)
        end
    end
    ...
    for armor, dmg in pairs(armor_damage) do armor:TakeDamage(dmg) end
    return leftover_damage, spdamage
end
```

规则是：**减伤取所有已装备护甲中吸收率的最大值；耐久消耗按各自的吸收率占比分摊。** 所以木甲（0.8）+ 橄榄球头盔（0.8）给你 80% 减伤（**不是** 96%），并且两件各承担一半被吸收的伤害。这是对"护甲叠加吗？"这个问题的一个优雅回答——**减伤意义上不叠加，耐久意义上叠加。**

`resistance` 短路（返回 `0, nil`）是"对该伤害类型完全免疫"的情形，并且它在护甲**之前**检查。

```lua
-- components/armor.lua:3
local function PercentChanged(inst, data)
    ...
    if data.percent <= 0 and inst.components.inventoryitem ~= nil and inst.components.inventoryitem.owner ~= nil then
        inst.components.inventoryitem.owner:PushEvent("armorbroke", { armor = inst })
    end
end
```

## 4. 饥饿度 —— `components/hunger.lua`（210 行）

整个 tick 就这么点：

```lua
-- components/hunger.lua:4
local UPDATE_PERIOD = 1
...
local function OnTaskTick(inst, self) self:DoDec(UPDATE_PERIOD) end

local Hunger = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.max = 100
    self.current = self.max
    self.hungerrate = 1
    self.hurtrate = 1
    self.overridestarvefn = nil
    self.burning = true
    self.burnrate = 1 -- DEPRECATED, please use burnratemodifiers instead.
    self.burnratemodifiers = SourceModifierList(self.inst)
    self.updatetask = self.inst:DoPeriodicTask(UPDATE_PERIOD, OnTaskTick, nil, self)
end, nil, { max = onmax, current = oncurrent })
```

### 4.1 速率数学

```lua
-- components/hunger.lua:146
function Hunger:DoDec(dt, ignore_damage)
    if self:IsPaused() then return end
    local old = self.current
    if self.current > 0 then
        self:DoDelta(-self.hungerrate * dt * self.burnrate * self.burnratemodifiers:Get(), true)
    elseif not ignore_damage then
        if self.overridestarvefn ~= nil then
            self.overridestarvefn(self.inst, dt)
        else
            self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-self.hurtrate * dt, true, "hunger")
        end
    end
end
```

有效消耗 = `hungerrate × burnrate × burnratemodifiers:Get()`，单位每秒。

玩家接线（`prefabs/player_common.lua:2777`）：

```lua
inst.components.hunger:SetRate(TUNING.WILSON_HUNGER_RATE)                        -- 75/480 = 0.15625 /s
inst.components.hunger:SetKillRate(TUNING.WILSON_HEALTH / TUNING.STARVE_KILL_TIME) -- 150/120 = 1.25 hp/s
```

所以：**150 饥饿 ÷ 0.15625 = 960 秒 = 2 个整天**从饱到饿，然后再 **120 秒**饿死。注意 `SetMax(150)` 而组件默认是 `max = 100` —— **百分比永远相对 `self.max` 计算**。

通过 grep `SetRate` 找到的角色/实体变体：

| 角色/实体 | 速率 | 来源 |
|---|---|---|
| Wilson（基准） | `75/480` = 0.156/s | `player_common.lua:2777` |
| Warly | `× TUNING.WARLY_HUNGER_RATE_MODIFIER` = 1.2 | `prefabs/warly.lua:33` |
| Wormwood | `× mult`（动态） | `prefabs/wormwood.lua:414` |
| Beefalo | `(75×4)/480` = 0.625/s | `TUNING.BEEFALO_HUNGER_RATE` |
| 月鱼人（Merm King） | `200/(480×8)` | `TUNING.MERM_KING_HUNGER_RATE` |
| Woby | `50/(480×2.5)` | `WOBY_BIG/SMALL_HUNGER_RATE` |
| 岩浆虫（Lavae） | `50/480` | `LAVAE_HUNGER_RATE` |
| 企鹅 / 小鸟 | `HUNGER / STARVE_TIME` | `prefabs/penguin.lua:273` |

Wolfgang 旧的 `WOLFGANG_HUNGER_RATE_MULT_MIGHTY = 3` 等已被标注 `-- depricated`；现代 Wolfgang 改用 `components/mightiness.lua`，它设置的是 `combat.externaldamagemultipliers`（虚弱 0.75 / 正常 nil / 强壮 2），而不是饥饿速率。

`LongUpdate(dt)` 调用 `DoDec(dt, true)` —— **`ignore_damage = true`**，所以离线时间会消耗数值条但**永远不会杀死你**。一条有意为之的仁慈规则，一行代码，值得抄。

### 4.2 `DoDelta` 语义

```lua
-- components/hunger.lua:128
function Hunger:DoDelta(delta, overtime, ignore_invincible)
    if self.redirect ~= nil then self.redirect(self.inst, delta, overtime); return end
    if not ignore_invincible and self.inst.components.health and self.inst.components.health:IsInvincible()
       or self.inst.is_teleporting then return end
    self:SetCurrent(self.current + delta, overtime)
end
```

饥饿度**会被生命值无敌状态暂停** —— 一个上帝模式的玩家不会变饿。`SetCurrent` 把值钳制到 `[0, max]`，推 `hungerdelta`（载荷 `{oldpercent, newpercent, overtime, delta}`），并对 `startstarving` / `stopstarving` 做边缘触发。

### 4.3 进食 —— `components/eater.lua:230`

```lua
function Eater:Eat(food, feeder)
    feeder = feeder or self.inst
    if self:PrefersToEat(food) then
        local stack_mult = self.eatwholestack and food.components.edible:GetStackMultiplier() or 1
        local base_mult = self.inst.components.foodmemory ~= nil and self.inst.components.foodmemory:GetFoodMultiplier(food.prefab) or 1

        local health_delta, hunger_delta, sanity_delta = 0, 0, 0

        if self.inst.components.health ~= nil and
            (food.components.edible.healthvalue >= 0 or self:DoFoodEffects(food)) then
            health_delta = food.components.edible:GetHealth(self.inst) * base_mult * self.healthabsorption
        end
        if self.inst.components.hunger ~= nil then
            hunger_delta = food.components.edible:GetHunger(self.inst) * base_mult * self.hungerabsorption
        end
        if self.inst.components.sanity ~= nil and
            (food.components.edible.sanityvalue >= 0 or self:DoFoodEffects(food)) then
            sanity_delta = food.components.edible:GetSanity(self.inst) * base_mult * self.sanityabsorption
        end

        if self.custom_stats_mod_fn ~= nil then
            health_delta, hunger_delta, sanity_delta = self.custom_stats_mod_fn(self.inst, health_delta, hunger_delta, sanity_delta, food, feeder)
        end

        if health_delta ~= 0 then self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(health_delta * stack_mult, nil, food.prefab) end
        if hunger_delta ~= 0 then self.inst.components.hunger:DoDelta(hunger_delta * stack_mult) end
        if sanity_delta ~= 0 then self.inst.components.sanity:DoDelta(sanity_delta * stack_mult) end
        ...
        self.inst:PushEvent("oneat", { food = food, feeder = feeder })
        if self.oneatfn ~= nil then self.oneatfn(self.inst, food, feeder) end
        food.components.edible:OnEaten(self.inst)
        food.components.edible:HandleEatRemove(self.eatwholestack)
        self.lasteattime = GetTime()
        if self.inst.components.foodmemory ~= nil and not food:HasTag("potion") then
            self.inst.components.foodmemory:RememberFood(food.prefab)
        end
        return true
    end
end
```

三个可分离的关注点值得注意：

**(a) 两段式饮食检查。** `CanEat`（用于*提供*这个动作）与 `PrefersToEat`（用于真正吞下去）。注释解释了原因：*"special diet characters (e.g. wigfrid) can TRY to eat all foods (they get the actions for it) but upon actually put it in their mouth, they bail and 'spit it out'."*

```lua
function Eater:PrefersToEat(food)
    if food.prefab == "winter_food4" and self.inst:HasTag("player") then
        --V2C: fruitcake hack. see how long this code stays untouched - _-"
        return false
    elseif self.nospoiledfood and (food.components.perishable and food.components.perishable:IsSpoiled()) then
        return false
    elseif self.preferseatingtags ~= nil then
        local preferred = food:HasAnyTag(self.preferseatingtags)   -- Warly：只吃料理过的食物
        if not preferred then return false end
    end
    return self:TestFood(food, self.preferseating)
end
```

**(b) 负面效果门控。** `DoFoodEffects` 决定*副作用*（坏食物的惩罚）是否生效：

```lua
function Eater:DoFoodEffects(food)
    return not ((self.strongstomach and food:HasTag("monstermeat")) or
                (self.eatsrawmeat and food:HasTag("rawmeat")) or
                (self.inst.components.foodaffinity and self.inst.components.foodaffinity:HasPrefabAffinity(food)))
end
```

**(c) `foodmemory` 递减收益。** `components/foodmemory.lua` 记住 `TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME`（480 秒）内吃过的每一种不同食物，返回 `self.mults[min(#mults, count)]`。Warly 的 `SetMultipliers` 让重复吃同一道菜收益递减。注意 `not food:HasTag("potion")` —— 药剂豁免。

### 4.4 食物数值 —— `components/edible.lua`

```lua
-- components/edible.lua:19
self.healthvalue = 10
self.hungervalue = 10
self.sanityvalue = 0
self.foodtype = FOODTYPE.GENERIC
self.secondaryfoodtype = nil
self.degrades_with_spoilage = true
self.temperaturedelta = 0
self.temperatureduration = 0
self.chill = 0      -- .temperatureduration 的百分比 [0,1]
self.stale_hunger = TUNING.STALE_FOOD_HUNGER      -- 0.667
self.stale_health = TUNING.STALE_FOOD_HEALTH      -- 0.333
self.spoiled_hunger = TUNING.SPOILED_FOOD_HUNGER  -- 0.5
self.spoiled_health = TUNING.SPOILED_FOOD_HEALTH  -- 0
self.spice = nil
```

腐败分档（`perishable.lua:onpercent`，`PERISH_FRESH = 0.5`、`PERISH_STALE = 0.2`）：`fresh` ≥ 50%，`stale` 20–50%，`spoiled` < 20%。这些是**实体上的标签**，不是数字，所以任何系统都能便宜地测试它们。

`GetHealth` / `GetHunger` / `GetSanity` 各自依次应用：腐败 → 香料 → 亲和：

```lua
function Edible:GetHunger(eater)
    local hungervalue = self.hungervalue
    local multiplier = 1
    local ignore_spoilage = not self.degrades_with_spoilage or hungervalue < 0
        or (eater ~= nil and eater.components.eater ~= nil and eater.components.eater.ignoresspoilage)

    if eater ~= nil and eater.components.eater ~= nil and eater.components.eater:CanProcessSpoiledItem(self.inst) then
        hungervalue = math.max(0, hungervalue)          -- 耐腐食客
    elseif not ignore_spoilage and self.inst.components.perishable ~= nil then
        if self.inst.components.perishable:IsStale() then
            multiplier = ... .stale_hunger or self.stale_hunger
        elseif self.inst.components.perishable:IsSpoiled() then
            multiplier = ... .spoiled_hunger or self.spoiled_hunger
        end
    end

    if eater ~= nil and eater.components.foodaffinity ~= nil then
        local affinity_bonus = eater.components.foodaffinity:GetAffinity(self.inst)
        if affinity_bonus ~= nil then multiplier = multiplier * affinity_bonus end
    end
    return hungervalue * multiplier
end
```

进食者可以覆盖*食物的*乘数（`eater.components.eater.stale_hunger`）—— Wiggybottom 有 `WICKERBOTTOM_STALE_FOOD_HUNGER = 0.333`，而默认是 0.667，也就是她从腐败食物里获得的**更少**。

**共享的食物数值词汇表**（`tuning.lua`）：

| 生命值 | 数值 | | 热量 | 数值（满值 75/天） |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `HEALING_TINY` | 1 | | `CALORIES_TINY` | 9.375（浆果） |
| `HEALING_SMALL` | 3 | | `CALORIES_SMALL` | 12.5（蔬菜） |
| `HEALING_MEDSMALL` | 8 | | `CALORIES_MEDSMALL` | 18.75 |
| `HEALING_MED` | 20 | | `CALORIES_MED` | 25（肉） |
| `HEALING_MEDLARGE` | 30 | | `CALORIES_LARGE` | 37.5（熟肉） |
| `HEALING_LARGE` | 40 | | `CALORIES_HUGE` | 75（锅） |
| `HEALING_HUGE` | 60 | | `CALORIES_SUPERHUGE` | 150 |

| 理智值 | 数值 | | 腐败时间 | 数值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SANITY_SUPERTINY` | 1 | | `PERISH_FAST` | 6 天（2880 秒） |
| `SANITY_TINY` | 5 | | `PERISH_FASTISH` | 8 天 |
| `SANITY_SMALL` | 10 | | `PERISH_MED` | 10 天 |
| `SANITY_MED` | 15 | | `PERISH_SLOW` | 15 天 |
| `SANITY_MEDLARGE` | 20 | | `PERISH_PRESERVED` | 20 天 |
| `SANITY_LARGE` | 33 | | `PERISH_SUPERSLOW` | 40 天 |
| `SANITY_HUGE` | 50 | | | |

食物同时还是一个**隐式加热器**：`Edible:OnEaten` 调用 `eater.components.temperature:SetTemperatureInBelly(delta*(1-chill)*mult, duration*mult)`。`chill` 会随着热食物放置而向 1 爬升（`AddChill`），并且叠放会稀释它（`DiluteChill`）。

---

## 5. 理智值 —— `components/sanity.lua`（619 行）

### 5.1 速率方程

`Sanity:Recalc(dt)` 由 `OnUpdate(dt)` 调用（无敌 / `spawnprotection` / 睡眠 / 传送中跳过）。所有来源被加总进一个 `self.rate`，然后积分：

```lua
-- components/sanity.lua:590
self.rate = dapper_delta + moisture_delta + light_delta + aura_delta + ghost_delta + self.externalmodifiers:Get()

if self.custom_rate_fn ~= nil then
    --NOTE: dt param was added for wormwood's custom rate function
    self.rate = self.rate + self.custom_rate_fn(self.inst, dt)
end

self.rate = self.rate * self.rate_modifier
self.ratescale =
    (self.rate > .2 and RATE_SCALE.INCREASE_HIGH) or
    (self.rate > .1 and RATE_SCALE.INCREASE_MED) or
    (self.rate > .01 and RATE_SCALE.INCREASE_LOW) or
    (self.rate < -.3 and RATE_SCALE.DECREASE_HIGH) or
    (self.rate < -.1 and RATE_SCALE.DECREASE_MED) or
    (self.rate < -.02 and RATE_SCALE.DECREASE_LOW) or
    RATE_SCALE.NEUTRAL

self:DoDelta(self.rate * dt, true)
```

`ratescale` 驱动 HUD 箭头（7 个离散值，`constants.lua:2404`）。**阈值是刻意不对称的** —— 增益比损失更容易被注意到。

**1. 风度（服装）：**

```lua
local total_dapperness = self.dapperness
for k, v in pairs(self.inst.components.inventory.equipslots) do
    local equippable = v.components.equippable
    if equippable ~= nil then
        local item_dapperness = self.get_equippable_dappernessfn ~= nil
            and self.get_equippable_dappernessfn(self.inst, equippable)
            or equippable:GetDapperness(self.inst, self.no_moisture_penalty)
        total_dapperness = total_dapperness + item_dapperness
    end
end
total_dapperness = total_dapperness * self.dapperness_mult
dapper_delta = total_dapperness * TUNING.SANITY_DAPPERNESS    -- = 1
```

所以 `equippable.dapperness` *直接*就是每秒理智值。已核实的例子：冬帽 = `DAPPERNESS_TINY`（`prefabs/hats.lua:340`），tam o' shanter = `DAPPERNESS_LARGE`（`hats.lua:665`）。每件湿装备追加 `WET_ITEM_DAPPERNESS = -0.1`。

风度和光环用同样的"每天"修辞来调参（`tuning.lua:2204`）：

```lua
DAPPERNESS_TINY     = 100/(day_time*15),   -- 10.7 理智/天
DAPPERNESS_SMALL    = 100/(day_time*10),   -- 16.0 /天
DAPPERNESS_MED      = 100/(day_time*6),    -- 26.7 /天
DAPPERNESS_MED_LARGE= 100/(day_time*4.5),  -- 35.5 /天
DAPPERNESS_LARGE    = 100/(day_time*3),    -- 53.3 /天
DAPPERNESS_HUGE     = 100/(day_time),      -- 160  /天
```

作为尺度参照：完全黑暗的流失是 `100/(night_time*2)` = 0.833/s = 一个 8 分钟白天 400 点理智，所以一顶 `DAPPERNESS_LARGE` 的帽子只能抵消站在黑暗里约 13% 的流失——**服装是缓冲，不是答案**。

**2. 湿度：**

```lua
local moisture_delta = (self.inst.components.moisture == nil or self.no_moisture_penalty) and 0
    or easing.inSine(self.inst.components.moisture:GetMoisture(), 0, TUNING.MOISTURE_SANITY_PENALTY_MAX, self.inst.components.moisture:GetMaxMoisture())
```

`MOISTURE_SANITY_PENALTY_MAX = -100/(day_time*6)`，全身湿透时为 −0.0556/s（也就是说你要湿站 6 个白天才会掉光整条），用 `inSine` 缓动，所以微微潮湿时几乎不掉，彻底湿透时才崩塌。

**3. 光照：**

```lua
local LIGHT_SANITY_DRAINS = {
    [SANITY_MODE_INSANITY] = {
        DAY        = TUNING.SANITY_DAY_GAIN,      -- 0
        NIGHT_LIGHT= TUNING.SANITY_NIGHT_LIGHT,   -- -100/(night_time*20) = -0.083/s
        NIGHT_DIM  = TUNING.SANITY_NIGHT_MID,     -- -100/(night_time*20) = -0.083/s
        NIGHT_DARK = TUNING.SANITY_NIGHT_DARK,    -- -100/(night_time*2)  = -0.833/s
    },
    [SANITY_MODE_LUNACY] = { ... 反转 ... },
}
...
if TheWorld.state.isday and not TheWorld:HasTag("cave") then
    light_delta = light_sanity_drain.DAY
else
    local lightval = CanEntitySeeInDark(self.inst) and .9 or self.inst.LightWatcher:GetLightValue()
    light_delta = ((lightval > TUNING.SANITY_HIGH_LIGHT and light_sanity_drain.NIGHT_LIGHT) or  -- > 0.6
                   (lightval < TUNING.SANITY_LOW_LIGHT and light_sanity_drain.NIGHT_DARK) or     -- < 0.1
                   light_sanity_drain.NIGHT_DIM) * self.night_drain_mult
end
```

**黑暗的流失量是有光的 10 倍**（0.833 对 0.083 每秒）。日光精确地是中性（0），不是正向——你不会从太阳那里*获得*理智，只是停止流失。`CanEntitySeeInDark` 给夜视生物伪造 0.9 的 lightval。

**4. 光环** —— 见下文。

**5. 幽灵流失（多人专属）：**

```lua
function Sanity:RecalcGhostDrain()
    if GetGhostSanityDrain(TheNet:GetServerGameMode()) and not self.player_ghost_immune_sources:Get() then
        local num_ghosts = TheWorld.shard.components.shard_players:GetNumGhosts()
        local num_alive  = TheWorld.shard.components.shard_players:GetNumAlive()
        local group_resist = num_alive > num_ghosts and 1 - num_ghosts / num_alive or 0
        self.ghost_drain_mult = math.min(num_ghosts, TUNING.MAX_SANITY_GHOST_PLAYER_DRAIN_MULT) * (1 - group_resist * group_resist)
    else
        self.ghost_drain_mult = 0
    end
end
```

每个死亡的队友都在抽干活着的人，上限 3 倍，并被幽灵比例二次方地阻尼——一条"你队里一半人都死了，所以这很正常"的曲线。（`MAX_SANITY_GHOST_PLAYER_DRAIN_MULT = 3`，`tuning.lua:2194`。）

### 5.2 光环系统 —— `GetSanityAura` 模式

`components/sanityaura.lua` 总共 35 行：

```lua
local SanityAura = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.inst = inst
    self.aura = 0
    --self.max_distsq = nil
    --self.aurafn = nil
    --self.fallofffn = nil
    self.inst:AddTag("sanityaura")
end)

function SanityAura:GetBaseAura(observer) --No falloff.
    return self.aurafn == nil and self.aura or self.aurafn(self.inst, observer)
end

function SanityAura:SetBaseAuraName(baseauraname)
    -- Auras with the same source name will count as one. It is assumed all auras with the same name have the same magnitude.
    self.baseauraname = baseauraname
end

local SANITY_EFFECT_RANGE_SQ = TUNING.SANITY_EFFECT_RANGE * TUNING.SANITY_EFFECT_RANGE   -- 10*10 = 100
function SanityAura:GetAura(observer)
    local aura_val = 0
    local distsq = observer:GetDistanceSqToInst(self.inst)
    if distsq <= (self.max_distsq or SANITY_EFFECT_RANGE_SQ) then
        aura_val = (self.aurafn == nil and self.aura or self.aurafn(self.inst, observer))
                 / (self.fallofffn ~= nil and self.fallofffn(self.inst, observer, distsq) or math.max(1, distsq))
    end
    return aura_val
end
```

三个扩展点，全是可选的：`.aura`（常量）、`.aurafn(inst, observer)`（动态，可依赖观察者）、`.fallofffn(inst, observer, distsq)`（自定义除数；默认是平方反比 `max(1, distsq)`）。`.max_distsq` 覆盖 10 单位的截断。

消费侧（`sanity.lua:544`）：

```lua
local SANITYRECALC_MUST_TAGS = { "sanityaura" }
local SANITYRECALC_CANT_TAGS = { "FX", "NOCLICK", "DECOR","INLIMBO" }
-- NOTES(JBK): This recalculation happens so many times in one frame we will reuse this table
-- for all sanity components as a scratch pad to not stomp on garbage collection.
local SCRATCHPAD_AURANAMES = {}
...
SCRATCHPAD_AURANAMES_ENDPOS = 0
local aura_delta = 0
if not self.sanity_aura_immune_sources:Get() then
    local x, y, z = self.inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    local ents = TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, TUNING.SANITY_AURA_SEACH_RANGE, SANITYRECALC_MUST_TAGS, SANITYRECALC_CANT_TAGS)
    for i, v in ipairs(ents) do
        if v.components.sanityaura ~= nil and v ~= self.inst then
            local is_aura_immune = false
            if self.sanity_aura_immunities ~= nil then
                for tag, sources in pairs(self.sanity_aura_immunities) do ...
                    elseif v:HasTag(tag) then is_aura_immune = true break end
                end
            end
            if not is_aura_immune then
                if not SCRATCHPAD_AURANAMES_HasBaseAuraNameBeenUsed(v) then
                    local aura_val = v.components.sanityaura:GetAura(self.inst)
                    aura_val = (aura_val < 0 and (self.neg_aura_absorb > 0 and self.neg_aura_absorb * -aura_val or aura_val) * self:GetAuraMultipliers() or aura_val)
                    aura_delta = aura_delta + ((aura_val < 0 and self.neg_aura_immune_sources:Get()) and 0 or aura_val)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
```

关键规则：

- 搜索半径 20（`SANITY_AURA_SEACH_RANGE`，实测 `tuning.lua:2224`），但每个光环自身的默认截断是 10（`SANITY_EFFECT_RANGE = 10`，`tuning.lua:2223`）。
- **`baseauraname` 去重人群效应**：20 头猪共享一个光环，不是 20 个。用一个模块级 scratchpad 数组实现，以避免 GC 压力（注释说这个在"一帧内发生很多次"）。
- **正光环永不被修改；负光环会被乘。** `neg_aura_modifiers`（`SourceModifierList`）、`neg_aura_absorb`（把流失转成固定吸收）、`neg_aura_immune_sources`（布尔：忽略所有负光环）。这就是为什么 tam o' shanter 在人群中有效，但不会被"过度增益"。
- 骑乘状态在实体查询**之外**有一个额外的显式检查（`sanity.lua:578`），因为被骑的牛羊牛不会被 `FindEntities` 返回。
- 按标签免疫：`AddSanityAuraImmunity(tag, source)` —— Wendy 专门免疫带 ghost 标签的光环。

光环强度（`tuning.lua:2196`，全部为每秒）：

```lua
SANITYAURA_TINY      = 100/(seg_time*32),   -- 0.104/s  →   50/天
SANITYAURA_SMALL_TINY= 100/(seg_time*20),   -- 0.208/s  →   80/天
SANITYAURA_SMALL     = 100/(seg_time*8),    -- 0.417/s  →  200/天
SANITYAURA_MED       = 100/(seg_time*5),    -- 0.667/s  →  320/天
SANITYAURA_LARGE     = 100/(seg_time*2),    -- 1.667/s  →  800/天
SANITYAURA_HUGE      = 100/(seg_time*.5),   -- 6.667/s  → 3200/天
SANITYAURA_SUPERHUGE = 100/(seg_time*.25),  -- 13.33/s  → 6400/天
```

这个命名约定编码的是"**每天消耗/恢复多少秒的满理智**"——一种真正有用的数值表编写方式。

在预制体中找到的具体例子：

```lua
-- prefabs/flower_evil.lua:65        邪恶花：-SMALL（平方反比衰减）
inst.components.sanityaura.aura = -TUNING.SANITYAURA_SMALL

-- prefabs/beefalo.lua:96            已驯化的牛羊牛：+TINY
inst.components.sanityaura.aura = TUNING.SANITYAURA_TINY

-- prefabs/glommer.lua:133           Glommer：+TINY
inst.components.sanityaura.aura = TUNING.SANITYAURA_TINY

-- prefabs/deerclops.lua:99          依状态而定的动态光环
local function CalcSanityAura(inst)
    return inst.components.combat.target ~= nil and -TUNING.SANITYAURA_HUGE or -TUNING.SANITYAURA_LARGE
end
inst.components.sanityaura.aurafn = CalcSanityAura

-- prefabs/moon_fissure.lua:131      自定义衰减 + 自定义半径
local function aurafallofffn(inst, observer, distsq)
    distsq = aurafn(inst, observer) + math.sqrt(math.max(1, distsq))
    return distsq
end
inst.components.sanityaura.max_distsq = 1
inst.components.sanityaura.aurafn = aurafn
inst.components.sanityaura.fallofffn = aurafallofffn

-- prefabs/antlion.lua:308           -MED
-- prefabs/atrium_overgrowth.lua:72  -SUPERHUGE
-- prefabs/corpse_gestalt.lua:108    +MED
```

普通花**不是**光环 —— 摘一朵是一次性的：

```lua
-- prefabs/flower.lua:52
local function onpickedfn(inst, picker)
    if picker ~= nil then
        if picker.components.sanity ~= nil and not picker:HasTag("plantkin") then
            picker.components.sanity:DoDelta(TUNING.SANITY_TINY)     -- +5
```

（而玫瑰变种会以 `picker.components.combat:GetAttacked(inst, TUNING.ROSE_DAMAGE)` 反击。）

### 5.3 疯癫阈值 —— 迟滞，以及两个方向

```lua
-- components/sanity.lua:387
self.current = math.min(math.max(self.current + delta, 0), self.max - (self.max * self.penalty))

local percent_ignoresinduced = self.current / self.max
if self.mode == SANITY_MODE_INSANITY then
    if self.sane and percent_ignoresinduced <= TUNING.SANITY_BECOME_INSANE_THRESH then      -- 30/200 = 15%
        self.sane = false
    elseif not self.sane and percent_ignoresinduced >= TUNING.SANITY_BECOME_SANE_THRESH then -- 35/200 = 17.5%
        self.sane = true
    end
else
    if self.sane and percent_ignoresinduced >= TUNING.SANITY_BECOME_ENLIGHTENED_THRESH then  -- 170/200 = 85%
        self.sane = false
    elseif not self.sane and percent_ignoresinduced <= TUNING.SANITY_LOSE_ENLIGHTENMENT_THRESH then -- 165/200
        self.sane = true
    end
end
```

5% 的迟滞带防止边界处闪烁。穿越时 `DoDelta` 触发 `goinsane` / `gosane` / `goenlightened`，以及可选的 `onInsane` / `onSane` / `onEnlightened` 回调。`SANITY_MODE_LUNACY` 把整套反过来：**高**理智才是"疯"的状态。阈值实测：`SANITY_BECOME_INSANE_THRESH = 30/200`、`SANITY_BECOME_SANE_THRESH = 35/200`、`SANITY_BECOME_ENLIGHTENED_THRESH = 170/200`、`SANITY_LOSE_ENLIGHTENMENT_THRESH = 165/200`（`tuning.lua:2176-2180`）。

### 5.4 理智 → 影子生物刷出 —— `components/shadowcreaturespawner.lua`

这是一个**世界级组件**（挂在 `TheWorld` 上），不是每玩家的。它维护 `_players[player] = { ents = {}, targetpop = 0 }` 并运行一个种群控制器：

```lua
local sanity = is_insanity_mode and player.components.sanity:GetPercent() or 1

if sanity > 0.5 then
    maxpop = 0                                    -- 比较清醒：清理
elseif sanity > 0.1 then
    maxpop = TUNING.SANITYMONSTERS_MAXPOP[1]      -- 1
    if targetpop >= maxpop then dec_chance = TUNING.SANITYMONSTERS_CHANCES[1].dec   -- 0.3
    else inc_chance = TUNING.SANITYMONSTERS_CHANCES[1].inc end                       -- 0.1
else
    maxpop = TUNING.SANITYMONSTERS_MAXPOP[2]      -- 2
    ... CHANCES[2] = { inc = 0.3, dec = 0.2 }
end
```

每 `SANITYMONSTERS_POP_CHANGE_INTERVAL = 10` 秒 ±10 秒重掷一次种群目标。实际刷出每 `SANITYMONSTERS_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 5` ±10 秒：

```lua
local function SpawnLandShadowCreature(player)
    return SpawnPrefab(
        player.components.sanity:GetPercent() < .1 and
        TryLuckRoll(player, TUNING.TERRORBEAK_SPAWN_CHANCE, LuckFormulas.TerrorbeakSpawn) and
        "terrorbeak" or "crawlinghorror")
end
```

理智低于 10% 时，每次刷出有 **50% 概率**是 Terrorbeak（400 HP、50 伤害、攻击间隔 1.5 秒、速度 7），否则是 Crawling Horror（300 HP、20 伤害、间隔 2.5 秒、速度 3）。刷出位置：15 单位外、随机角度、只在可通行地面上；在船上且低于 10% 时会尝试海洋刷出（`oceanhorror`）最多 4 次，然后回退到陆地。

关键在于，这些生物是**短命且按玩家私有的**：

```lua
inst:ListenForEvent("entitysleep", function() inst:DoTaskInTime(0, function() ent:Remove() end) end, ent)
ent:ListenForEvent("onremove", function() ent.spawnedforplayer = nil; ent.persists = false; ent.wantstodespawn = true end, player)
```

它们离屏就消失、不存档、主人离开就消失。种群过密通过把随机实体标记 `wantstodespawn` 来剪枝。

另有一个 `inducedinsanity` 分支（通过 Maxwell/Wortox 机制自我施加），带 `SANITYMONSTERS_INDUCED_MAXPOP` 和更紧的 5 秒重掷。

---

## 6. 体温与湿度

### 6.1 `components/temperature.lua`（538 行）

状态与默认值：

```lua
self.current = TUNING.STARTING_TEMP          -- 35
self.maxtemp = TUNING.MAX_ENTITY_TEMP        -- 90
self.mintemp = TUNING.MIN_ENTITY_TEMP        -- -20
self.overheattemp = TUNING.OVERHEAT_TEMP     -- 70
self.hurtrate = TUNING.WILSON_HEALTH / TUNING.FREEZING_KILL_TIME   -- 150/120 = 1.25 hp/s
self.inherentinsulation = 0
self.inherentsummerinsulation = 0
self.shelterinsulation = TUNING.INSULATION_MED_LARGE   -- 180
self.maxmoisturepenalty = TUNING.MOISTURE_TEMP_PENALTY -- 30
```

（实测 `tuning.lua:2290-2297`：`STARTING_TEMP = 35`、`OVERHEAT_TEMP = 70`、`MIN_ENTITY_TEMP = -20`、`MAX_ENTITY_TEMP = 90`、`WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 1`、`THAW_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 5`；`FREEZING_KILL_TIME = 120` 在第 751 行；`MOISTURE_TEMP_PENALTY = 30` 在第 2915 行。）

### 6.2 保暖模型

```lua
-- components/temperature.lua:254
function Temperature:GetInsulation()
    local winterInsulation = self.inherentinsulation
    local summerInsulation = self.inherentsummerinsulation

    if self.inst.components.inventory ~= nil then
        for k, v in pairs(self.inst.components.inventory.equipslots) do
            if v.components.insulator ~= nil then
                local insulationValue, insulationType = v.components.insulator:GetInsulation()
                if insulationType == SEASONS.WINTER then
                    winterInsulation = winterInsulation + insulationValue
                elseif insulationType == SEASONS.SUMMER then
                    summerInsulation = summerInsulation + insulationValue
                else
                    print(v, " has invalid insulation type: ", insulationType)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if self.inst.components.beard ~= nil then
        --Beards help winterInsulation but hurt summerInsulation
        winterInsulation = winterInsulation + self.inst.components.beard:GetInsulation()
        summerInsulation = summerInsulation - self.inst.components.beard:GetInsulation()
    end

    if self.sheltered then summerInsulation = summerInsulation + self.shelterinsulation end

    if self.insulation_modifiers ~= nil then ... end

    if not TheWorld:HasTag("cave") then
        if TheWorld.state.isdusk then      summerInsulation = summerInsulation + TUNING.DUSK_INSULATION_BONUS   -- 60
        elseif TheWorld.state.isnight then summerInsulation = summerInsulation + TUNING.NIGHT_INSULATION_BONUS -- 120
        end
    end

    return math.max(0, winterInsulation), math.max(0, summerInsulation)
end
```

保暖是**季节分型**的（`components/insulator.lua` 只有 31 行：一个数值加上 `SEASONS.WINTER` / `SEASONS.SUMMER`，默认 WINTER；`SetSummer()` 翻转它）。冬装在夏天毫无作用，反之亦然。数值（`tuning.lua:2249`，单位是 `seg_time = 30`），附带已核实的穿戴者：

| 常量 | 数值 | 已核实装备（文件:行） |
|---|---|---|
| `INSULATION_TINY` | 30 | — |
| `INSULATION_SMALL` | 60 | 草帽（`prefabs/hats.lua:256`，`SetSummer()` 在 255）、耳罩（`hats.lua:319`）、雨衣（`prefabs/raincoat.lua:77`）、毛衣背心（`prefabs/sweatervest.lua:59`）、夏季木纹背心（`prefabs/trunkvest.lua:104`） |
| `INSULATION_MED` | 120 | 冬帽（`hats.lua:343`，同时有 `DAPPERNESS_TINY`）、tam o' shanter（`hats.lua:668`，同时有 `DAPPERNESS_LARGE`）、雨伞（`prefabs/umbrella.lua:147`，`SetSummer()` 在 125）、反光背心（`prefabs/reflectivevest.lua:75`）、西瓜帽（`hats.lua:1764`）、沙漠帽（`hats.lua:2235`，`SetSummer()`） |
| `INSULATION_MED_LARGE` | 180 | 树荫 `shelterinsulation`（`temperature.lua:35`） |
| `INSULATION_LARGE` | 240 | 牛角帽（`hats.lua:642`，同时有 `WATERPROOFNESS_SMALL`）、眼球伞帽（`hats.lua:1358`，`SetSummer()` 在 1359 + `WATERPROOFNESS_ABSOLUTE`）、冰帽（`hats.lua:1690`）、熊皮背心（`prefabs/beargervest.lua:76`）、夏威夷衬衫（`prefabs/hawaiianshirt.lua:63`）、冬季木纹背心（`prefabs/trunkvest.lua:121`）、虚空布伞（`prefabs/voidcloth_umbrella.lua:402`） |

黄昏给**夏季**保暖加 `DUSK_INSULATION_BONUS = 60`，夜晚加 `NIGHT_INSULATION_BONUS = 120`，且只在洞穴之外 —— 傍晚的凉爽被建模成了免费的夏季装备。

防水是另一条 0–1 的刻度（`tuning.lua:2935`）：`WATERPROOFNESS_SMALL = 0.2`、`_SMALLMED = 0.35`、`_MED = 0.5`（草伞，`prefabs/umbrella.lua:145`）、`_LARGE = 0.7`、`_HUGE = 0.9`（`umbrella.lua:180`）、`_ABSOLUTE = 1`（眼球伞帽，`hats.lua:1355`）。注意雨衣只是做了 `inst:AddComponent("waterproofer")` 而没有 `SetEffectiveness`（`prefabs/raincoat.lua:67`），所以它用的是组件默认值 **1.0，本身就完全防水**。由于 `moisture.lua` 会跨来源求和并在 `>= 1` 处短路，两件 `WATERPROOFNESS_MED` 的装备也能达到同样效果；**正是这种可加性让"部分防水装备"变得有意义地可叠加。**

**饱和公式** —— 这是巧妙之处：

```lua
-- components/temperature.lua:71
function Temperature:DoDelta(delta, skipinsulation)
    if not skipinsulation then
        local winterInsulation, summerInsulation = self:GetInsulation()
        if delta > 0 then
            delta = delta * (TUNING.SEG_TIME / (TUNING.SEG_TIME + summerInsulation))
        else
            delta = delta * (TUNING.SEG_TIME / (TUNING.SEG_TIME + winterInsulation))
        end
    end
    self:SetTemperature(self.current + delta)
end
```

`30/(30+I)` 是一个**渐近阻尼器**：I=0 → ×1.0，I=60 → ×0.33，I=120 → ×0.2，I=240 → ×0.111。叠保暖有严格的边际递减，且**永远不可能达到零**。这是"如何让服装可叠加又不破坏难度曲线"的一行答案。

### 6.3 完整的 `OnUpdate` 累加顺序

`components/temperature.lua:311`。`delta`（目标 − 当前）严格按顺序构建，源码里还留着 `print` 面包屑：

```lua
local ambient_temperature = inside_pocket_container and TheWorld.state.temperature or GetTemperatureAtXZ(x, z)
local ratemult = owner and owner.components.preserver and owner.components.preserver:GetTemperatureRateMultiplier(self.inst) or 1

if owner ~= nil and owner:HasTag("fridge") and not owner:HasTag("nocool") then
    -- Inside a fridge, excluding icepack ("nocool")
    mintemp = math.max(mintemp, math.min(0, ambient_temperature))
    self.rate = owner:HasTag("lowcool") and -.5 * TUNING.WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC or -TUNING.WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC
else
    ...
    self.delta = (ambient_temperature + self.totalmodifiers + self:GetMoisturePenalty()) - self.current
    --print(self.delta + self.current, "initial target")
```

1. **环境温度** —— `GetTemperatureAtXZ(x, z)`，来自 `components/temperatureoverrider.lua`：半径内最近注册的 overrider →  fumarole 场 → `TheWorld.state.temperature`。
2. **`totalmodifiers`** —— 一个手写的带键增删表（`SetModifier(name, value)` / `RemoveModifier(name)`），维护一个增量求和的总量。**不是** `SourceModifierList` —— 更便宜，是更老的代码。
3. **潮湿惩罚** —— `-Lerp(0, 30, moisturePercent)`。
4. **遮蔽** —— `sheltered_level > 1` 会把环境温度钳到 `overheattemp - 5`。
5. **已装备取暖物** → **手持（物品栏槽）取暖物** → **溢出容器（背包）取暖物** → **手持光标（active item）取暖物**：
```lua
for k, v in pairs(self.inst.components.inventory.equipslots) do
    if v.components.heater ~= nil then
        local heat, carriedmult = v.components.heater:GetEquippedHeat()
        if heat ~= nil and
            ((heat > self.current and v.components.heater:IsExothermic()) or
             (heat < self.current and v.components.heater:IsEndothermic())) then
            self.delta = self.delta + (heat - self.current) * carriedmult
        end
    end
end
```
一个取暖物只会把你往**它自己的温度方向**推 —— exo/endo 符号检查防止"热石"把你冷却到低于它自己的温度。`components/heater.lua` 暴露三个独立的温度值（`heat`、`equippedheat`、`carriedheat`），每个都有可选的 `*fn` 变体，外加 `SetThermics(exo, endo)`。
6. **腹中** —— 最近吃下的冷/热食物，受 `HOT_FOOD_WARMING_THRESHOLD` / `COLD_FOOD_CHILLING_THRESHOLD` 门控。
7. **树荫降温** —— 若 `sheltered` 且高于 `TREE_SHADE_COOLING_THRESHOLD`，减去 `(current - TREE_SHADE_COOLER)`。
8. **世界取暖物**（篝火等），用 `FindEntities(x,y,z, ZERO_DISTANCE=10, {"HASHEATER"}, ignoreheatertags)` 查找：
```lua
local heatfactor, dsqtoinst
if v.components.heater:ShouldFalloff() then
    dsqtoinst = self.inst:GetDistanceSqToInst(v)
    heatfactor = 1 - dsqtoinst / ZERO_DISTSQ        -- ZERO_DISTSQ = 100
else
    heatfactor = 1
end
local radius_cutoff = v.components.heater:GetHeatRadiusCutoff()
if radius_cutoff then ... if dsqtoinst > radius_cutoff*radius_cutoff then heatfactor = 0 end end

if heatfactor > 0 then
    if self.inst:GetIsWet() then
        if heat > 0 then heatfactor = heatfactor * heat_factor_penalty       -- 0.75
        elseif heat_factor_penalty ~= 0 then heatfactor = heatfactor / heat_factor_penalty end
    end
    if v.components.heater:IsExothermic() then
        -- heating heatfactor is relative to 0 (freezing)
        local warmingtemp = heat * heatfactor
        if warmingtemp > self.current then self.delta = self.delta + warmingtemp - self.current end
        self.externalheaterpower = self.externalheaterpower + heatfactor
    else
        -- cooling heatfactor is relative to overheattemp
        local coolingtemp = (heat - self.overheattemp) * heatfactor + self.overheattemp
        if coolingtemp < self.current then self.delta = self.delta + coolingtemp - self.current end
    end
end
```
注意**潮湿会让火更糟，同时让吸热（冷）火更好** —— 就是那个 `/ heat_factor_penalty` 分支。而 `externalheaterpower` 在这里被累加，纯粹是为了让 `moisture.lua` 能读它来算干燥速度。**一个干净的单向依赖。**
9. **保暖钳制** —— 让整个系统手感正确的不对称性：
```lua
-- Winter insulation only affects you when it's cold out, summer insulation only helps when it's warm
if ambient_temperature >= TUNING.STARTING_TEMP then
    -- it's warm out
    if self.delta > 0 then
        local winterInsulation, summerInsulation = self:GetInsulation()
        self.rate = math.min(self.delta, TUNING.SEG_TIME / (TUNING.SEG_TIME + summerInsulation))
    else
        -- If they are cooling, do it at full speed, and faster if they're overheated
        self.rate = math.max(self.delta, self.current >= self.overheattemp and -TUNING.THAW_DEGREES_PER_SEC or -TUNING.WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC)
    end
elseif self.delta < 0 then
    -- If the player is cooling, defend using insulation.
    local winterInsulation, summerInsulation = self:GetInsulation()
    self.rate = math.max(self.delta, -TUNING.SEG_TIME / (TUNING.SEG_TIME + winterInsulation))
else
    -- If they are heating up, do it at full speed, and faster if they're freezing
    self.rate = math.min(self.delta, self.current <= 0 and TUNING.THAW_DEGREES_PER_SEC or TUNING.WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC)
end
```
**保暖只抵抗危险的方向。** 从冻结状态回暖是*快速*的（`THAW_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 5`，对比 `WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 1`）。这就是"惩罚"与"挫败"之间的差别：一旦你找到火，5 °C/s 的速度足以让你脱离死亡螺旋。

10. **积分 + 扣血：**
```lua
self.rate = self.rate * ratemult
self:SetTemperature(math.clamp(self.current + self.rate * dt, mintemp, maxtemp))

--applyhealthdelta nil defaults to true
if applyhealthdelta ~= false and self.inst.components.health ~= nil then
    if self.current < 0 then
        self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-self.hurtrate * dt, true, "cold")
    elseif self.current > self.overheattemp then
        self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-(self.overheathurtrate or self.hurtrate) * dt, true, "hot")
    end
end
```

### 6.4 冻结/过热触发

```lua
-- components/temperature.lua:166
function Temperature:SetTemperature(value)
    local last = self.current
    self.current = math.clamp(value, self.mintemp, self.maxtemp)

    if (self.current < 0) ~= (last < 0) then
        self.inst:PushEvent(self.current < 0 and "startfreezing" or "stopfreezing")
    end
    if (self.current > self.overheattemp) ~= (last > self.overheattemp) then
        self.inst:PushEvent(self.current > self.overheattemp and "startoverheating" or "stopoverheating")
    end
    self.inst:PushEvent("temperaturedelta", { last = last, new = self.current, hasrate = self.rate ~= 0 })
end
```

边缘触发（`~= last`）—— 不会刷屏。通过 grep 找到的消费者：`components/wisecracker.lua`（角色台词）、`widgets/bloodover.lua`（红/蓝屏幕暗角）、`prefabs/player_classified.lua`（客户端镜像，让远端客户端也能看到特效）。

**两个不同的"冻结"概念 —— 不要混淆：**

- `temperature.current < 0` → 持续 HP 伤害（"你很冷"）。
- `components/freezable.lua` → 离散的"彻底冻住"眩晕（冰杖、巨鹿）。它累积 `coldness`，与 `resistance` 比较，然后要么 `Freeze(freezetime)`，要么 `StartWearingOff()`：
```lua
-- components/freezable.lua:137
function Freezable:AddColdness(coldness, freezetime, nofreeze)
    if self.redirectfn ~= nil and self.redirectfn(self.inst, coldness, freezetime, nofreeze) then return end
    self.coldness = math.max(0, self.coldness + coldness)
    if coldness > 0 then
        if self:IsFrozen() then self:Freeze(freezetime)
        else
            local resistance = self:ResolveResistance()
            if self.coldness < resistance then self:StartWearingOff()
            elseif self.inst.sg ~= nil and (nofreeze or self.inst.sg:HasStateTag("nofreeze")) then
                self.coldness = resistance; self:StartWearingOff()
            else self:Freeze(freezetime) end
        end
    end
    self:UpdateTint()
end
```

### 6.5 `components/moisture.lua`（397 行）

```lua
self.maxmoisture = 100
self.moisture = 0
self.numSegs = 5                       -- 5 格 UI "水滴"
self.baseDryingRate = 0
self.maxDryingRate = 0.1
self.maxPlayerTempDrying = 5
self.optimalPlayerTempDrying = 2
self.maxMoistureRate = .75
self.optimalDryingTemp = 50
self.inherentWaterproofness = 0        -- DEPRECATED, USE THE SourceModifierList BELOW
self.waterproofnessmodifiers = SourceModifierList(inst, 0, SourceModifierList.additive)
self.externalbonuses = SourceModifierList(inst, 0, SourceModifierList.additive)
```

**变湿：**

```lua
function Moisture:_GetMoistureRateAssumingRain()
    if self.inst.components.rainimmunity ~= nil then return 0 end
    local waterproofmult =
        (self.inst.components.sheltered ~= nil and self.inst.components.sheltered.sheltered
            and self.inst.components.sheltered.waterproofness or 0) +
        (self.inst.components.inventory ~= nil and self.inst.components.inventory:GetWaterproofness() or 0) +
        (self.inherentWaterproofness or 0) +
        (self.waterproofnessmodifiers:Get() or 0)
    if waterproofmult >= 1 then return 0 end
    local rate = easing.inSine(TheWorld.state.precipitationrate, self.minMoistureRate, self.maxMoistureRate, 1)
    return rate * (1 - waterproofmult)
end

function Moisture:GetMoistureRate()
    if self.inst.components.inventory and self.inst.components.inventory:IsFloaterHeld() or self:IsInBathingPool() then
        return self.maxMoistureRate                    -- 拿着泳圈 / 在泳池里 = 立刻湿透
    elseif not TheWorld.state.israining then return 0 end
    return self:_GetMoistureRateAssumingRain()
end
```

**防水跨来源可加，且在 1 处钳制**（`inventory:GetWaterproofness` 把每一件已装备的 `waterproofer:GetEffectiveness()` 求和；`moisture:GetWaterproofness` 钳到 0..1）。一件雨衣已经是 1.0；两件 0.5 的也能达到。这就是 `>= 1` 提前返回存在的原因。

**变干：**

```lua
function Moisture:GetDryingRate(moisturerate)
    if (moisturerate or self:GetMoistureRate()) > 0 then return 0 end     -- 下雨时永不变干

    local heaterPower = self.inst.components.temperature ~= nil and math.clamp(self.inst.components.temperature.externalheaterpower, 0, 1) or 0
    local playerTempDrying = self:GetSegs() < 3 and self.optimalPlayerTempDrying or self.maxPlayerTempDrying

    local rate = self.baseDryingRate
        + easing.linear(heaterPower, self.minPlayerTempDrying, playerTempDrying, 1)
        + easing.linear(GetLocalTemperature(self.inst), self.minDryingRate, self.maxDryingRate, self.optimalDryingTemp)
        + easing.inExpo(self:GetMoisture(), 0, 1, self.maxmoisture)

    return math.clamp(rate, 0, self.maxDryingRate + self.maxPlayerTempDrying)
end
```

站在火边最多 5/s 变干（如果你只是微湿则 2/s —— 一条**防闪烁规则**）。环境温度贡献最多 0.1/s，对照 50 °C 缩放。你越湿，干得越快（对湿度用 `inExpo`）—— 再次说明**逃离螺旋是容易的**。

**积分：**

```lua
function Moisture:OnUpdate(dt)
    if self:IsForceDry() then return end
    local sleepingbagdryingrate = self:GetSleepingBagDryingRate()
    if sleepingbagdryingrate ~= nil then
        self.rate = -sleepingbagdryingrate
    else
        local moisturerate = self:GetMoistureRate()
        local dryingrate = self:GetDryingRate(moisturerate)
        local equippedmoisturerate = self:GetEquippedMoistureRate(dryingrate)
        local externalbonuses = self:GetRateBonus()
        self.rate = moisturerate + equippedmoisturerate - dryingrate + externalbonuses
    end
    if self.moisture > 0 or self.rate > 0 then
        local drate = self:GetDesiccantBonus(self.rate, dt)   -- moistureabsorberuser（干燥剂物品）
        self.rate = self.rate - drate
    end
    self.ratescale = ...
    self:DoDelta(self.rate * dt, self:IsInBathingPool())
end
```

**"湿"是在 2/5 格处的一个离散状态：**

```lua
function Moisture:DoDelta(num, no_announce)
    if self:IsForceDry() then return end
    local oldLevel = self.moisture
    local oldSegs = self:GetSegs()
    self.moisture = math.clamp(self.moisture + num, 0, self.maxmoisture)
    local newSegs = self:GetSegs()
    self.wet = newSegs >= 2                      -- >= 40%
    if not no_announce then self:AnnounceMoisture(oldSegs, newSegs) end
    self.inst:PushEvent("moisturedelta", { old = oldLevel, new = self.moisture })
end
```

`AnnounceMoisture` 在每一次整格穿越时触发角色台词（DAMP / WET / WETTER / SOAKED）。`GetSegs()` 返回 `(full, fractional)`，好让 UI 能动画化一个部分填充的水滴。

`ForceDry(force, source)` 是一个带来源键的覆盖（带 `onremove` 清理），它把湿度归零并完全停止组件更新。

`components/waterproofer.lua`（30 行）：`effectiveness`（默认 1.0），加上 `waterproofer` 标签，并且**禁用该物品自身的湿度**（`inventoryitem:EnableMoisture(false)`）—— 雨衣自己不会变湿。

---

## 7. 战斗 —— `components/combat.lua`（1349 行）

### 7.1 核心状态

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:24
self.attackrange = 3
self.hitrange = 3
self.areahitrange = nil
self.hitarc = nil
self.areahitdamagepercent = nil
self.defaultdamage = 0
--use nil for defaults
--self.playerdamagepercent = 1  --modifier for NPC dmg on players, only works with NO WEAPON
--self.pvp_damagemod = 1
--self.damagemultiplier = 1
--self.damagebonus = 0
--self.ignorehitrange = false

self.externaldamagemultipliers = SourceModifierList(self.inst)      -- 对他人造成伤害的倍率
self.externaldamagetakenmultipliers = SourceModifierList(self.inst) -- 我承受伤害的倍率（在护甲减伤之后）

self.min_attack_period = 4
self.laststartattacktime = 0
self.canattack = true
self.panic_thresh = nil
self.forcefacing = true
self.bonusdamagefn = nil
```

那些 `--use nil for defaults` 注释是一种**内存优化纪律**：一个存在于游戏里每一个生物身上的组件，绝不能分配它不需要的字段。

### 7.2 距离：两个独立的数字

```lua
function Combat:GetAttackRange()
    local weapon = self:GetWeapon()
    return weapon ~= nil and weapon.components.weapon.attackrange ~= nil
        and math.max(0, self.attackrange + weapon.components.weapon.attackrange)
        or self.attackrange
end

function Combat:CalcAttackRangeSq(target)
    local range = (target or self.target):GetPhysicsRadius(0) + self:GetAttackRange()
    return range * range
end

function Combat:GetHitRange()
    local weapon = self:GetWeapon()
    return self.temprange or weapon ~= nil and weapon.components.weapon.hitrange ~= nil
        and self.hitrange + weapon.components.weapon.hitrange or self.hitrange
end
```

- **`attackrange`** = AI/运动控制器必须靠多近才会*开始*一次攻击。含目标的物理半径。
- **`hitrange`** = 挥击动画落地时实际能打到的距离。同样含物理半径，且可以通过 `self.temprange` 临时覆盖（由 `DoAttack(..., instrangeoverride, instpos)` 设置）。

两者都是平方距离比较（`distsq`，三维）。客户端副本会减去 **0.5 单位的预测误差容差**：`local range = math.max(0, target:GetPhysicsRadius(0) + self:GetAttackRangeWithWeapon() - .5)`。

### 7.3 冷却

```lua
function Combat:InCooldown()
    return self.laststartattacktime ~= nil and self.laststartattacktime + self.min_attack_period > GetTime()
end
function Combat:GetCooldown()
    return self.laststartattacktime ~= nil and math.max(0, self.min_attack_period - GetTime() + self.laststartattacktime) or 0
end
function Combat:StartAttack()
    if self.forcefacing and self.target ~= nil and self.target:IsValid() then
        self.inst:ForceFacePoint(self.target:GetPosition())
    end
    self:RestartCooldown()
end
Combat.CancelAttack = Combat.ResetCooldown
```

基于时间，不是基于任务。`OverrideCooldown(cd)` 会把 `laststartattacktime` **回拨**，让你能*设定*一个任意的剩余冷却时间 —— 一个实现"这次攻击让你力竭"的小花招。玩家得到 `wilson_attack_period = 0.4`；组件默认是 4 秒。

### 7.4 `CanAttack` —— 闸门

```lua
function Combat:CanAttack(target)
    if not self:IsValidTarget(target) then return false, true end
    return self.canattack
        and not self:InCooldown()
        and (self.inst.sg == nil or not self.inst.sg:HasStateTag("busy") or self.inst.sg:HasStateTag("hit"))
        and (self.ignorehitrange or distsq(target:GetPosition(), self.inst:GetPosition()) <= self:CalcAttackRangeSq(target))
end
```

两个返回值，让调用方能区分"目标无效，放弃"和"目标有效但没准备好，继续试"。`LocomotorCanAttack` 是 AI 寻路变体，额外做一次**虚空检测** —— 对射程 > 4 的近战武器，它拒绝跨越空隙寻路：

```lua
if attackrangesq > 4 and self.inst.isplayer then
    local weapon = self:GetWeapon()
    local is_ranged_weapon = weapon ~= nil and weapon:HasAnyTag("projectile", "rangedweapon")
    if not is_ranged_weapon then
        local currentpos = self.inst:GetPosition()
        local voidtest = currentpos + ((target:GetPosition() - currentpos):Normalize() * (self:GetAttackRange() / 2))
        if TheWorld.Map:IsNotValidGroundAtPoint(voidtest:Get()) and not TheWorld.Map:IsNotValidGroundAtPoint(target.Transform:GetWorldPosition()) then
            reached_dest = false
        end
    end
end
```

### 7.5 命中/落空/扇形

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:1034
function Combat:CanHitTarget(target, weapon)
    if ... and (self:CanExtinguishTarget(target, weapon) or self:CanLightTarget(target, weapon)
        or (target.components.combat ~= nil and target.components.combat:CanBeAttacked(self.inst))) then

        if self.ignorehitrange then return true end

        local x1, y1, z1 = target.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
        local x, y, z
        if self.temppos then x, y, z = self.temppos:Get() else x, y, z = self.inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition() end

        local dx, dy, dz = x1 - x, y1 - y, z1 - z
        local dsq3d = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz

        if dsq3d <= self:CalcHitRangeSq(target) then
            if self.hitarc == nil or (dx == 0 and dz == 0) then return true end
            local rot = self.inst.Transform:GetRotation()
            local rot1 = math.atan2(-dz, dx) * RADIANS
            if DiffAngle(rot, rot1) * 2 <= self.hitarc then return true end
        end

        --OOR, but check projectile as well
        if weapon and weapon.components.projectile then
            x, y, z = weapon.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
            ...
            local range = target:GetPhysicsRadius(0) + weapon.components.projectile.hitdist
            return dsq3d <= range * range
        end
    end
    return false
end
```

近战**没有闪避/命中判定骰子**。"落空"是几何意义上的：超出 `hitrange`，或落在 `hitarc` 锥形之外（按全锥宽度测量，所以是 `DiffAngle(...) * 2`）。真正的闪避是一个独立的可选组件 `attackdodger`，在 `GetAttacked` 内部检查。

### 7.6 `DoAttack` —— 攻击方

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:1095 (节选；完整顺序保留)
function Combat:DoAttack(targ, weapon, projectile, stimuli, instancemult, instrangeoverride, instpos)
    if instrangeoverride then self.temprange = instrangeoverride end
    if instpos then self.temppos = instpos end
    if targ == nil then targ = self.target end
    if weapon == nil then weapon = self:GetWeapon() end
    if stimuli == nil then
        if weapon and weapon.components.weapon then
            if weapon.components.weapon.overridestimulifn then stimuli = weapon.components.weapon.overridestimulifn(weapon, self.inst, targ) end
            if stimuli == nil and weapon.components.weapon.stimuli == "electric" then stimuli = "electric" end
        end
        if stimuli == nil and self.inst.components.electricattacks ~= nil then stimuli = "electric" end
    end

    if not self:CanHitTarget(targ, weapon) or self.AOEarc then
        self.inst:PushEvent("onmissother", { target = targ, weapon = weapon })
        if self.areahitrange ~= nil and not self.areahitdisabled then
            self:DoAreaAttack(projectile or self.inst, self.areahitrange, weapon, self.areahitcheck, stimuli, AREA_EXCLUDE_TAGS)
        end
        self:ClearAttackTemps(); return
    end

    if targ.components.combat then
        local recoil, damage = targ.components.combat:ShouldRecoil(self.inst, weapon)
        if recoil and self.inst.sg ~= nil and self.inst.sg.statemem.recoilstate ~= nil then
            self.inst:PushEventImmediate("recoil_off", { target = targ })
            if damage == 0 or damage == nil then self.inst:PushEvent("weapontooweak") end
        end
    end

    self.inst:PushEvent("onattackother", { target = targ, weapon = weapon, projectile = projectile, stimuli = stimuli })

    if weapon ~= nil and projectile == nil then
        if weapon.components.projectile ~= nil then                       -- 投掷物
            local projectile = self.inst.components.inventory:DropItem(weapon, false)
            if projectile ~= nil then projectile.components.projectile:Throw(self.inst, targ) end
            self:ClearAttackTemps(); return
        elseif weapon.components.complexprojectile ~= nil and not weapon.components.complexprojectile.ismeleeweapon then
            local projectile = self.inst.components.inventory:DropItem(weapon, false)
            if projectile ~= nil then projectile.components.complexprojectile:Launch(targ:GetPosition(), self.inst) end
            self:ClearAttackTemps(); return
        elseif weapon.components.weapon:CanRangedAttack() then            -- 生成一个投射物预制体
            weapon.components.weapon:LaunchProjectile(self.inst, targ)
            self:ClearAttackTemps(); return
        end
    end

    local reflected_dmg = 0
    local reflected_spdmg
    local reflect_list = {}
    if targ.components.combat ~= nil then
        local mult = 1
        local _weapon_cmp = weapon ~= nil and weapon.components.weapon or nil
        if (stimuli == "electric" or (_weapon_cmp ~= nil and _weapon_cmp.stimuli == "electric"))
           and not IsEntityElectricImmune(targ) then
            local electric_damage_mult     = _weapon_cmp ~= nil and _weapon_cmp.electric_damage_mult     or TUNING.ELECTRIC_DAMAGE_MULT       -- 1.5
            local electric_wet_damage_mult = _weapon_cmp ~= nil and _weapon_cmp.electric_wet_damage_mult or TUNING.ELECTRIC_WET_DAMAGE_MULT   -- 1
            mult = electric_damage_mult + electric_wet_damage_mult * targ:GetWetMultiplier()
        end

        local dmg, spdmg = self:CalcDamage(targ, weapon, mult)
        dmg = dmg * (instancemult or 1)
        --Calculate reflect first, before GetAttacked destroys armor etc.
        if projectile == nil then
            reflected_dmg, reflected_spdmg = self:CalcReflectedDamage(targ, dmg, weapon, stimuli, reflect_list, spdmg)
        end
        targ.components.combat:GetAttacked(self.inst, dmg, weapon, stimuli, spdmg)
    elseif projectile == nil then
        reflected_dmg, reflected_spdmg = self:CalcReflectedDamage(targ, 0, weapon, stimuli, reflect_list)
    end

    if weapon ~= nil then weapon.components.weapon:OnAttack(self.inst, targ, projectile) end   -- 耐久

    if self.areahitrange ~= nil and not self.areahitdisabled then
        self:DoAreaAttack(targ, self.areahitrange, weapon, self.areahitcheck, stimuli, AREA_EXCLUDE_TAGS)
    end
    self:ClearAttackTemps()
    self.lastdoattacktime = GetTime()

    --Apply reflected damage to self after our attack damage is completed
    if (reflected_dmg > 0 or reflected_spdmg ~= nil) and self.inst.components.health ~= nil and not self.inst.components.health:IsDead() then
        self:GetAttacked(targ, reflected_dmg, nil, nil, reflected_spdmg)
        for i, v in ipairs(reflect_list) do
            if v.inst:IsValid() then v.inst:PushEvent("onreflectdamage", v) end
        end
    end
end
```

**对湿目标的电击伤害：`1.5 + 1.0 × 湿度`** —— 一个湿透的目标吃晨星棒要承受 2.5 倍，干燥的只吃 1.5 倍。`GetWetMultiplier()`（`entityscript.lua:748`）对物品返回 0/1，对生物返回湿度分数。

注意那句顺序注释：**反射伤害在 `GetAttacked` 之前计算**，因为 `GetAttacked` 会消耗护甲耐久，会改变答案。

### 7.7 `CalcDamage` —— 伤害公式

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:846 (节选)
function Combat:CalcDamage(target, weapon, multiplier)
    if target:HasTag("alwaysblock") then return 0 end

    local basedamage
    local basemultiplier = self.damagemultiplier
    local externaldamagemultipliers = self.externaldamagemultipliers
    local damagetypemult = 1
    local bonus = self.damagebonus                    --not affected by multipliers
    local playermultiplier = CanApplyPlayerDamageMod(target)
    local pvpmultiplier = playermultiplier and self.inst.isplayer and self.pvp_damagemod or 1
    local mount = nil
    local spdamage

    --NOTE: playermultiplier is for damage towards players
    --      generally only applies for NPCs attacking players

    if weapon ~= nil then
        --No playermultiplier when using weapons
        basedamage, spdamage = weapon.components.weapon:GetDamage(self.inst, target)
        playermultiplier = 1
        if self.inst.components.damagetypebonus ~= nil then damagetypemult = self.inst.components.damagetypebonus:GetBonus(target) end
        spdamage = SpDamageUtil.CollectSpDamage(self.inst, spdamage)
    else
        basedamage = self.defaultdamage
        playermultiplier = playermultiplier and self.playerdamagepercent or 1
        if self.inst.components.rider ~= nil and self.inst.components.rider:IsRiding() then
            mount = self.inst.components.rider:GetMount()
            if mount ~= nil and mount.components.combat ~= nil then
                basedamage = mount.components.combat.defaultdamage
                basemultiplier = mount.components.combat.damagemultiplier
                externaldamagemultipliers = mount.components.combat.externaldamagemultipliers
                bonus = mount.components.combat.damagebonus
                ...
            end
            local saddle = self.inst.components.rider:GetSaddle()
            if saddle ~= nil and saddle.components.saddler ~= nil then
                basedamage = basedamage + saddle.components.saddler:GetBonusDamage()
                ...
            end
        else ... end
    end

    local damage = (basedamage or 0)
        * (basemultiplier or 1)
        * externaldamagemultipliers:Get()
        * damagetypemult
        * (multiplier or 1)
        * playermultiplier
        * pvpmultiplier
        * (self.customdamagemultfn ~= nil and self.customdamagemultfn(self.inst, target, weapon, multiplier, mount) or 1)
        + (bonus or 0)
    ...
    return damage, spdamage
end
```

**公式，用直白的话说：**

```
damage = 基础伤害
       × damagemultiplier            (攻击者自身的扁平缩放)
       × externaldamagemultipliers   (增益：酸液浸润、Wolfgang 强壮等)
       × damagetypemult              (对特定目标类型的加成)
       × multiplier                  (调用方提供：电击，或 AoE 的 areahitdamagepercent)
       × playermultiplier            (NPC→玩家 的难度缩放；**使用武器时强制为 1**)
       × pvpmultiplier               (玩家→玩家，来自 self.pvp_damagemod)
       × customdamagemultfn(...)     (逃生舱)
       + damagebonus                 (扁平值，最后加，永不参与乘法)
```

然后护甲在*之后*、在 `GetAttacked` 里应用。两个微妙之处：

- **`playerdamagepercent` 只对徒手的 NPC 攻击生效。** 使用武器会把它覆写为 1 —— 物品的平衡与"对玩家的 NPC 伤害"难度滑块保持独立。
- **骑乘战斗会把整个伤害来源换成坐骑的战斗组件**（牛羊牛冲撞用的是牛羊牛的数值），然后加上鞍具的扁平加成。

武器侧（`components/weapon.lua:94`）：

```lua
function Weapon:GetDamage(attacker, target)
    local dmg = FunctionOrValue(self.damage, self.inst, attacker, target)
    local spdmg = SpDamageUtil.CollectSpDamage(self.inst)
    if self.inst.components.damagetypebonus ~= nil then
        local damagetypemult = self.inst.components.damagetypebonus:GetBonus(target)
        dmg = dmg * damagetypemult
        spdmg = SpDamageUtil.ApplyMult(spdmg, damagetypemult)
    end
    return dmg, spdmg
end
```

`FunctionOrValue` 意味着 `weapon.damage` 可以是一个数字**或**一个函数 `(inst, attacker, target) → number` —— 伤害可以依赖谁在挥、砍的是谁，**零额外机制**。

命中时扣耐久（`weapon.lua:105`）：

```lua
function Weapon:OnAttack(attacker, target, projectile)
    if self.onattack ~= nil then self.onattack(self.inst, attacker, target) end
    self.inst:PushEvent("weapononattack", { attacker = attacker, target = target })
    if self.inst.components.finiteuses ~= nil and not self.inst.components.finiteuses:IgnoresCombatDurabilityLoss()
        and not (projectile ~= nil and projectile.components.projectile ~= nil and projectile.components.projectile:IsBounced()) then
        local uses = (self.attackwear or 1) * self.attackwearmultipliers:Get()
        if attacker ~= nil and attacker:IsValid() and attacker.components.efficientuser ~= nil then
            uses = uses * (attacker.components.efficientuser:GetMultiplier(ACTIONS.ATTACK) or 1)
        end
        self.inst.components.finiteuses:Use(uses)
    end
end
```

### 7.8 `GetAttacked` —— 防守方，完整顺序

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:568 (节选；每个阶段按原序保留)
function Combat:GetAttacked(attacker, damage, weapon, stimuli, spdamage)
    if self.inst.components.health and self.inst.components.health:IsDead() then return true end
    self.lastwasattackedtime = GetTime()

    local blocked = false
    local damageredirecttarget = self.redirectdamagefn ~= nil and self.redirectdamagefn(self.inst, attacker, damage, weapon, stimuli, spdamage) or nil
    local damageresolved = 0
    local original_damage = damage
    self.lastattacker = attacker

    --can add more attacktypes as needed; currently just "projectile" or nil, used for hit stun calculation
    if (damage or 0) > 0 and weapon and (weapon.components.projectile or (weapon.components.weapon and weapon.components.weapon.projectile)) then
        self.lastattacktype = "projectile"
    else self.lastattacktype = nil end
    self.laststimuli = stimuli

    local recoil
    recoil, damage = self:ShouldRecoil(attacker, weapon, damage)
    if recoil then blocked = true end

    if self.inst.components.health ~= nil and damage ~= nil and damageredirecttarget == nil then
        if self.inst.components.attackdodger ~= nil and self.inst.components.attackdodger:CanDodge(attacker) then
            self.inst.components.attackdodger:Dodge(attacker)
            damage, spdmg = 0, nil
        end

        if self.inst.components.inventory and not self.inst.components.inventory.ignorecombat then
            if attacker ~= nil and attacker.components.planarentity ~= nil and not self.inst.components.inventory:EquipHasSpDefenseForType("planar") then
                attacker.components.planarentity:OnPlanarAttackUndefended(self.inst)
            end
            damage, spdamage = self.inst.components.inventory:ApplyDamage(damage, attacker, weapon, spdamage)   -- ★ 护甲
        end
        if self.inst.components.rideable ~= nil then
            local saddle = self.inst.components.rideable.saddle
            if saddle ~= nil and saddle.components.saddler ~= nil then
                damage, spdamage = saddle.components.saddler:ApplyDamage(damage, attacker, weapon, spdamage)
            end
        end
        local damagetypemult = 1
        if self.inst.components.damagetyperesist ~= nil then damagetypemult = damagetypemult * self.inst.components.damagetyperesist:GetResist(attacker, weapon) end
        if self.conditionexternaldamagetakenmultipliers ~= nil then
            damage = damagetypemult * self:ApplyConditionExternalDamageTakenMultiplier(damage, attacker, weapon)
        end
        damage = damage * damagetypemult * self.externaldamagetakenmultipliers:Get()
        if (damage > 0 or spdamage ~= nil) and not self.inst.components.health:IsInvincible() then
            if damage > 0 then
                --Bonus damage only applies after unabsorbed damage gets through your armor
                if attacker ~= nil and attacker.components.combat ~= nil and attacker.components.combat.bonusdamagefn ~= nil then
                    damage = damage + damagetypemult * (attacker.components.combat.bonusdamagefn(attacker, self.inst, damage, weapon) or 0)
                end
                if self.inst.components.planarentity ~= nil then
                    damage, spdamage = self.inst.components.planarentity:AbsorbDamage(damage, attacker, weapon, spdamage)
                end
            end
            if spdamage ~= nil then
                spdamage = SpDamageUtil.ApplySpDefense(self.inst, spdamage)
                damage = damage + damagetypemult * SpDamageUtil.CalcTotalDamage(spdamage)
            end

            local cause = attacker == self.inst and weapon or attacker
            damageresolved = self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-damage, nil, cause ~= nil and (cause.nameoverride or cause.prefab) or "NIL", nil, cause)
            damageresolved = damageresolved ~= nil and -damageresolved or damage
            if self.inst.components.health:IsDead() then
                if attacker ~= nil then attacker:PushEvent("killed", { victim = self.inst, attacker = attacker }) end
                if self.onkilledbyother ~= nil then self.onkilledbyother(self.inst, attacker) end
            end
        else
            blocked = true
        end
    end

    local redirect_combat = damageredirecttarget ~= nil and damageredirecttarget.components.combat or nil
    if redirect_combat ~= nil then
```
