```lua
        -- Small hack for centipede
        redirect_combat.redirected_from = self.inst
        redirect_combat:GetAttacked(attacker, damage, weapon, stimuli, spdamage)
        redirect_combat.redirected_from = nil
    end
    ... 音效 ...
    if not blocked then
        self.inst:PushEvent("attacked", { attacker = attacker, damage = damage, damageresolved = damageresolved, original_damage = original_damage, weapon = weapon, stimuli = stimuli, spdamage = spdamage, redirected = damageredirecttarget, noimpactsound = self.noimpactsound })
        if self.onhitfn ~= nil then self.onhitfn(self.inst, attacker, damage, spdamage) end
        if attacker ~= nil then
            attacker:PushEvent("onhitother", { target = self.inst, damage = damage, damageresolved = damageresolved, stimuli = stimuli, spdamage = spdamage, weapon = weapon, redirected = damageredirecttarget })
            if attacker.components.combat ~= nil and attacker.components.combat.onhitotherfn ~= nil then
                attacker.components.combat.onhitotherfn(attacker, self.inst, damage, stimuli, weapon, damageresolved, spdamage, damageredirecttarget)
            end
        end
    else
        -- We blocked it, but we might still want to know how much they rattled us in damage value!
        self.inst:PushEvent("blocked", { attacker = attacker, damage = damage, spdamage = spdamage, original_damage = original_damage })
    end
    return not blocked
end
```

**完整的伤害管线，按顺序：**

```
攻击方                                        防守方
────────                                        ────────

weapon:GetDamage(attacker, target)
  × damagetypebonus (武器)
→ CalcDamage:
  × damagemultiplier
  × externaldamagemultipliers
  × damagetypebonus (攻击者)
  × stimuli 倍率 (电击 × 湿度)
  × playermultiplier / pvpmultiplier
  × customdamagemultfn
  + damagebonus
  × instancemult
→ DoAttack: CalcReflectedDamage（护甲前快照）
→ targ.combat:GetAttacked(dmg, ...)  ─────────▶  0. 已死的短路
                                                1. redirectdamagefn（招架 / 坐骑转移）
                                                2. ShouldRecoil → "tough" 护甲检查
                                                3. attackdodger:CanDodge → damage = 0
                                                4. inventory:ApplyDamage   ★ 护甲吸收 + 耐久
                                                5. saddler:ApplyDamage
                                                6. damagetyperesist
                                                7. conditionexternaldamagetakenmultipliers (fns)
                                                8. externaldamagetakenmultipliers
                                                9. attacker.bonusdamagefn  （护甲之后！）
                                               10. planarentity:AbsorbDamage
                                               11. SpDamageUtil.ApplySpDefense + CalcTotalDamage
                                               12. health:DoDelta(-damage)
                                                     → redirect / invincible / absorb / reduction
                                                     → deltamodifierfn / maxdamagetakenperhit
                                                     → SetVal → 钳制、"death"
                                               13. "attacked" 或 "blocked" 事件
                                               14. onhitfn / attacker "onhitother" / onhitotherfn
← 反射伤害通过 self:GetAttacked(...) 施加给攻击者
← weapon:OnAttack → finiteuses:Use（耐久）
← 若有 areahitrange 则 DoAreaAttack
```

### 7.9 反击 / 反射

```lua
-- components/combat.lua:941
local function _CalcReflectedDamage(inst, attacker, dmg, weapon, stimuli, reflect_list, spdmg)
    if inst == nil then return 0 end
    local reflected_dmg = 0
    local reflected_spdmg
    if inst.components.damagereflect ~= nil then
        local dmg1, spdmg1 = inst.components.damagereflect:GetReflectedDamage(attacker, dmg, weapon, stimuli, spdmg)
        if dmg1 > 0 or spdmg1 ~= nil then
            reflected_dmg = reflected_dmg + dmg1
            reflected_spdmg = SpDamageUtil.MergeSpDamage(reflected_spdmg, spdmg1)
            table.insert(reflect_list, { inst = inst, attacker = attacker, reflected_dmg = dmg1, reflected_spdmg = spdmg1 })
        end
    end
    if inst.components.inventory ~= nil then
        for k, v in pairs(EQUIPSLOTS) do
            local equip = inst.components.inventory:GetEquippedItem(v)
            if equip ~= nil and equip.components.damagereflect ~= nil then ... end
        end
    end
    return reflected_dmg, reflected_spdmg
end
```

荆棘式伤害来自实体*自身或其任一已装备物品*上的 `damagereflect` 组件，被收集进一个列表，好让每个来源在事后通过 `"onreflectdamage"` 各自被通知。被骑乘的目标从**坐骑 + 鞍具**反射，而不是从骑手。

另外，`ShouldRecoil` 是"你的武器被弹开"的机制：

```lua
function Combat:ShouldRecoil(attacker, weapon, damage)
    if self.shouldrecoilfn ~= nil then
        local recoil, remaining_damage = self.shouldrecoilfn(self.inst, attacker, weapon, damage)
        if recoil ~= nil then
            if recoil then return true, remaining_damage or nil end
            return false, remaining_damage or damage
        end
    end
    if self.tough and
        not (attacker ~= nil and attacker:HasTag("toughfighter")) and
        not (weapon ~= nil and weapon.components.weapon ~= nil and weapon.components.weapon:CanDoToughFight())
    then
        return true, nil           -- 被格挡，零伤害，攻击者收到 "weapontooweak"
    end
    return false, damage
end
```

### 7.10 范围伤害

```lua
local AREA_EXCLUDE_TAGS = { "INLIMBO", "notarget", "noattack", "flight", "invisible", "playerghost" }
local AREAATTACK_MUST_TAGS = { "_combat" }

function Combat:DoAreaAttack(target, range, weapon, validfn, stimuli, excludetags, onlyontarget)
    local hitcount = 0
    local x, y, z = target.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    ...
    local ents = TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, range, AREAATTACK_MUST_TAGS, excludetags)
    for i, ent in ipairs(ents) do
        if ent ~= target and ent ~= self.inst and self:IsValidTarget(ent) and (validfn == nil or validfn(ent, self.inst)) then
            self.inst:PushEvent("onareaattackother", { target = ent, weapon = weapon, stimuli = stimuli })
            local dmg, spdmg = self:CalcDamage(ent, weapon, self.areahitdamagepercent)
            ent.components.combat:GetAttacked(self.inst, dmg, weapon, stimuli, spdmg)
            hitcount = hitcount + 1
        end
    end
    return hitcount
end
```

AoE 以**目标**为圆心，不是攻击者，并对每个受害者以 `areahitdamagepercent` 作为倍率重跑完整的 `CalcDamage`→`GetAttacked` 管线。它在**落空时也会触发**（`DoAttack` 第 1124 行）—— 这就是"挥空了但依然溅到了"的实现方式。`_combat` 这个 must-tag 由战斗组件自动添加，提供了一个廉价的"这里有任何与战斗相关的东西吗"的空间查询。

### 7.11 目标选择与仇恨

```lua
function Combat:SetTarget(target)
    if target ~= self.target and
        (target == nil or (self:IsValidTarget(target) and self:ShouldAggro(target))) and
        not (target and target.isplayer and target.sg and target.sg:HasStateTag("hiding"))
    then
        local oldtarget = self.target
        self:DropTarget(target ~= nil)
        self:EngageTarget(target, oldtarget)
    end
end

function Combat:ShouldAggro(target, ignore_forbidden)
    if target ~= nil and
        (self.shouldaggrofn == nil or self.shouldaggrofn(self.inst, target)) and
        (self.shouldavoidaggro == nil or not self.shouldavoidaggro[target]) and
        (target.components.combat == nil or target.components.combat.shouldavoidaggrofn == nil
            or target.components.combat.shouldavoidaggrofn(self.inst, target))
    then
        if not ignore_forbidden and self.forbiddenaggrotags ~= nil then
            for _, tag in ipairs(self.forbiddenaggrotags) do
                if target:HasTag(tag) then return false end
            end
        end
        if target:HasTag("stealth") then return false end
        if target.components.health ~= nil and (target.components.health.minhealth or 0) > 0 and not target:HasTag("hostile") then
            target = target.components.follower ~= nil and target.components.follower:GetLeader() or target
            if not target.isplayer then
                --npc should not aggro on things that can't be killed (unless hostile!)
                return false
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    return false
end
```

`shouldavoidaggro` 是一个**引用计数**的表（`SetShouldAvoidAggro` / `RemoveShouldAvoidAggro` 增减计数）—— 两个重叠的"别攻击我"效果不会在其中一个到期时互相抵消。`ignore_forbidden = true` 只在通过 `ShareTarget` 继承他人仇恨时使用。

**目标的生命周期是事件驱动的，不是轮询的：**

```lua
function Combat:StartTrackingTarget(target)
    if target then
        self.inst:ListenForEvent("enterlimbo", self.losetargetcallback, target)
        self.inst:ListenForEvent("onremove", self.losetargetcallback, target)
        self.inst:ListenForEvent("transfercombattarget", self.transfertargetcallback, target)
        self.inst:ListenForEvent("leaderchanged", self.allycheckcallback, target)
    end
end
```

如果你的目标变成了你首领的跟随者，你掉仇恨。如果它被传送进了 limbo 或被移除，你也掉。

`keeptargetfn` **确实**是轮询的，1 Hz，且只在存在目标时：

```lua
function Combat:OnUpdate(dt)
    if self.target == nil then self.inst:StopUpdatingComponent(self); return end
    if self.keeptargetfn ~= nil then
        self.keeptargettimeout = self.keeptargettimeout - dt
        if self.keeptargettimeout < 0 then
            if self.inst:IsAsleep() then self.inst:StopUpdatingComponent(self); return end
            self.keeptargettimeout = 1
            local drop
            if not self.target:IsValid() or self.target:IsInLimbo() then drop = true
            else
                drop = not (self.target.components.combat and self.keeptargetfn(self.inst, self.target)
                            and self.target.components.combat:CanBeAttacked(self.inst))
            end
            if drop then self.inst:PushEvent("losttarget"); self:DropTarget() end
        end
    end
end
```

**重新选敌**是一个带随机初始延迟的周期任务，以避免所有怪物在同一帧重新评估（惊群效应）：

```lua
function Combat:SetRetargetFunction(period, fn)
    self.targetfn = fn
    self.retargetperiod = period
    ...
    if period and fn and not self.inst:IsAsleep() then
        self.retargettask = self.inst:DoPeriodicTask(period, dotryretarget, period*math.random(), self)
    end
end
```

重选任务在 `OnEntitySleep` 取消、在 `OnEntityWake` 重启 —— 离屏生物零开销。

`TryRetarget` 里有一条微妙的反"钻空子"规则：

```lua
elseif self.target ~= nil and self.target:HasTag("structure") and not newtarget:HasTag("structure") then
    self:SetTarget(newtarget)     -- 永远优先生物而不是墙
```

**目标共享**（群体仇恨）：

```lua
function Combat:ShareTarget(target, range, fn, maxnum, musttags)
    if not self:ShouldAggro(target, true) then return end
    if maxnum <= 0 then return end
    local x, y, z = self.inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    local ents = TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, SpringCombatMod(range), musttags or DEFAULT_SHARE_TARGET_MUST_TAGS)
    local num_helpers = 0
    for i, v in ipairs(ents) do
        if v ~= self.inst and not (v.components.health ~= nil and v.components.health:IsDead())
            and (fn == nil or fn(v, self.inst)) and v.components.combat:SuggestTarget(target) then
            num_helpers = num_helpers + 1
            if num_helpers >= maxnum then return end
        end
    end
end
```

`SuggestTarget` 只在助手*当前没有目标*时才接受 —— 你不能抢仇恨，只能填空位。

### 7.12 阵营系统 —— `components/combat_replica.lua`

所有"谁可以打谁"的逻辑都在**副本**里，好让客户端能预测。`components/combat.lua` 只是转发：`function Combat:CanBeAlly(guy) return self.inst.replica.combat:CanBeAlly(guy) end`。

```lua
-- components/combat_replica.lua:326
function Combat:CanBeAlly(guy)
    if guy:HasTag("alwayshostile") then return false end
    if guy == self.inst then return true end                       -- It's me.

    local follower = self.inst.replica.follower
    local myleader = follower and follower:GetLeader()
    if myleader and guy == myleader then return true end           -- It's my leader.

    local guy_follower = guy.replica.follower
    local theirleader = guy_follower and guy_follower:GetLeader()
    if self.inst == theirleader then return true end               -- It's my follower.
    if myleader and myleader == theirleader then return true end   -- Same leader.

    -- Are we player aligned?
    if self.inst.isplayer or self.inst.bedazzled or
        (myleader and (myleader.isplayer or myleader.replica.inventoryitem)) or
        self.inst:HasAnyTag("player_aligned", "domesticated", "saltlicker_salted")
    then
        if guy.bedazzled or guy:HasTag("companion") or (theirleader and theirleader.replica.inventoryitem) then
            return true                                            -- aligned to all players
        end
        if not TheNet:GetPVPEnabled() then
            if guy.isplayer or (theirleader and theirleader.isplayer) then return true end
            if guy:HasAnyTag("player_aligned", "domesticated", "saltlicker_salted") then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function Combat:IsAlly(guy)
    if not self:CanBeAlly(guy) then return false end
    local guy_combat = guy.replica.combat
    return guy_combat == nil or guy_combat:GetTarget() ~= self.inst
end
```

**没有阵营 ID，也没有声望数值。** "阵营"是从四个原语推导出来的：`follower.leader` 链接、`isplayer`、若干阵营标签（`player_aligned`、`domesticated`、`saltlicker_salted`、`companion`、`bedazzled`），以及服务器的 PVP 开关。`CanBeAlly` = "可能是友方的"；`IsAlly` = "是友方*并且*当前没有攻击我"。**用极少的机制产生了相当的行为。**

```lua
-- components/combat_replica.lua:292
function Combat:IsValidTarget(target)
    if target == nil or target == self.inst or not (target.entity:IsValid() and target.entity:IsVisible()) then return false end
    local weapon = self:GetWeapon()
    return self:CanExtinguishTarget(target, weapon)
        or self:CanLightTarget(target, weapon)
        or (target.replica.combat ~= nil and
            not IsEntityDead(target, true) and
            not target:HasTag("spawnprotection") and
            not (target:HasTag("shadow") and self.inst.replica.sanity == nil and not self.inst:HasTag("crazy")) and
            not (target:HasTag("playerghost") and (self.inst.replica.sanity == nil or self.inst.replica.sanity:IsSane()) and not self.inst:HasTag("crazy")) and
            (TheNet:GetPVPEnabled() or not (self.inst.isplayer and target.isplayer) or (weapon and weapon:HasTag("propweapon"))) and
            target:GetPosition().y <= self._attackrange:value())
end
```

注意两件**不属于战斗**的事：**"攻击"被重载成了"扑灭"和"点燃"**（火把/洒水壶的交互走的是同一条目标判定路径），以及 **PVP 有一个 `propweapon` 逃生口**，好让小游戏能在关闭 PVP 的服务器上开友伤。那个 `.y` 检查防止打到上下差距过大的东西。

```lua
-- components/combat_replica.lua:412 (节选)
function Combat:CanBeAttacked(attacker)
    if self.inst:HasAnyTag("playerghost", "flight") or
       (not self.temp_iframes_keep_aggro and self.inst:HasAnyTag("noattack", "invisible")) then
        return false                                       -- 谁都不能攻击
    end
    local sanity
    if attacker ~= nil then
        if attacker.isplayer and self.inst:HasTag("noplayertarget") then return false
        elseif attacker ~= self.inst and self.inst.isplayer then
            if attacker.isplayer and not TheNet:GetPVPEnabled() then
                local combat = attacker.replica.combat
                local weapon = combat ~= nil and combat:GetWeapon() or nil
                if weapon == nil or not weapon:HasTag("propweapon") then return false end   -- PVP 检查
            end
            if self._target:value() ~= attacker then
                local follower = attacker.replica.follower
                if follower ~= nil then
                    local leader = follower:GetLeader()
                    if leader ~= nil and leader ~= self._target:value() and leader.isplayer and not attacker:HasTag("alwayshostile") then
                        local combat = attacker.replica.combat
                        if combat ~= nil and combat:GetTarget() ~= self.inst then return false end   -- 跟随者检查
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        sanity = attacker.replica.sanity
        if sanity ~= nil and sanity:IsCrazy() or attacker:HasTag("crazy") then
            return true                                    -- 疯癫的攻击者几乎可以攻击任何东西
        end
    end
    if self.inst:HasAnyTag("shadowcreature", "nightmarecreature") and (...) then
        return false                                       -- 清醒的玩家打不到影子生物
    end
    return true
end
```

这就是**理智 → 战斗的闸门**：影子生物在你疯之前是字面意义上不可被选中的，而一个疯癫的攻击者会绕过其他所有限制。**理智不只是个掉血条，它改变了对你而言"存在什么"。**

### 7.13 PvP 处理小结

**没有独立的 PvP 代码路径。** 它就是两个函数里的三处检查：

1. `IsValidTarget`：`(TheNet:GetPVPEnabled() or not (self.inst.isplayer and target.isplayer) or weapon:HasTag("propweapon"))`
2. `CanBeAttacked`：同样的检查，从防守方视角，带 `propweapon` 例外。
3. `CalcDamage`：`pvpmultiplier = playermultiplier and self.inst.isplayer and self.pvp_damagemod or 1`。

### 7.14 `combat_damagemultiplier`

**在本代码库中不存在。** grep `combat_damagemultiplier` 无任何结果。最接近的真实东西是 `combat.damagemultiplier`（每实体标量）、`combat.externaldamagemultipliers`（`SourceModifierList`）、`combat.pvp_damagemod`、`combat.playerdamagepercent`，以及 `combat.externaldamagetakenmultipliers`。`components/mightiness.lua`（Wolfgang）是 `externaldamagemultipliers` 的典型使用者：`SetModifier(inst, 0.75)` 虚弱 / 移除 = 正常 / `SetModifier(inst, 2)` 强壮。

---

## 8. 物品栏与容器

### 8.1 槽位模型

`components/inventory.lua`（2754 行）持有三份不同的存储：

```lua
-- components/inventory.lua:46
self.itemslots = {}                                   -- [1..maxslots] = inst
self.maxslots = GetMaxItemSlots(TheNet:GetServerGameMode())   -- MAXITEMSLOTS = 15
self.equipslots = {}                                  -- [EQUIPSLOTS.HANDS|HEAD|BODY|BEARD] = inst
self.activeitem = nil                                 -- "光标" / 拿在手里的物品
self.acceptsstacks = true
self.opencontainers = {}                              -- 你当前打开的箱子
self.dropondeath = true
inst:ListenForEvent("death", OnDeath)                 -- → DropEverything(true)
```

外加第四份，*虚拟的*一份：`GetOverflowContainer()` —— BODY 槽里那个东西的 `container` 组件：

```lua
function Inventory:GetOverflowContainer()
    if self.ignoreoverflow then return end
    local item = self:GetEquippedItem(EQUIPSLOTS.BODY)
    return (item ~= nil and item.components.container ~= nil and item.components.container.canbeopened)
        and item.components.container or nil
end
```

**背包*就是*溢出容器。** 其他任何地方都没有"背包"这个概念 —— 它只是身体槽物品上的一个容器，而每个 `Has` / `FindItem` / `ForEachItem` / `CanAcceptCount` 都会递归进去。非常经济。

第五份，更窄的一份：`GetSpecializedContainers()` —— 那些*在你口袋里*且带有 `priorityfn` 的容器物品（弹药袋、种子袋）。匹配物品时会先搜索它们，再搜你的主槽。

### 8.2 `GetNextAvailableSlot` —— 放置优先级链

```lua
-- components/inventory.lua:813 (节选)
function Inventory:GetNextAvailableSlot(item)
    local overflow = self:GetOverflowContainer()
    local prioritize_overflow = overflow ~= nil and overflow:ShouldPrioritizeContainer(item)
    local useoverflow = overflow ~= nil and ValidateItemForOverflow(item, overflow)
    local specialized = self:GetSpecializedContainers()
    ...
    if item.components.stackable ~= nil then
        --check for stacks that aren't full
        for k, v in pairs(self.equipslots) do ... if CanStackWith(item) then return k, self.equipslots end end
        for k, v in pairs(self.itemslots)   do ... return k, self.itemslots end
        if useoverflow and (...) then for k, v in pairs(overflow.slots) do ... return k, overflow end end
        if specialized then ... return k, spoverflow end
        ...
    end
    if useoverflow and prioritize_overflow then ... 溢出容器的第一个空槽 ... end
    if specialized then ... 专用容器的第一个空槽 ... end
    --check for empty space in the container
    for k = 1, self.maxslots do
        if self:CanTakeItemInSlot(item, k) and not self.itemslots[k] then return k, self.itemslots end
    end
    return nil, self.itemslots
end
```

它返回**两个值：槽位索引，以及它住在哪张表里**（`self.itemslots`、`self.equipslots`、`overflow`，或某个专用容器）。每个调用方然后据此分派。优先级顺序：合并进未满的堆（装备 → 口袋 → 背包 → 专用）*优先于*占用一个新槽；并且被标记 `ShouldPrioritizeContainer` 的背包优先于你的口袋。

`ValidateItemForOverflow` 尊重 `canonlygoinpocket`（地图、蓝图），所以它们永远不会被塞进背包。

### 8.3 `GiveItem` —— 拾取流程

`components/inventory.lua:1029`。它很长，因为它要处理每一种边界情况：

```lua
function Inventory:GiveItem(inst, slot, src_pos)
    if inst.components.inventoryitem == nil or not inst:IsValid() then ... return end

    local eslot = self:IsItemEquipped(inst)
    if eslot then self:Unequip(eslot) end

    local new_item = inst ~= self.activeitem
    if new_item then for k, v in pairs(self.equipslots) do if v == inst then new_item = false break end end end

    if inst.components.inventoryitem.owner and inst.components.inventoryitem.owner ~= self.inst then
        inst.components.inventoryitem:RemoveFromOwner(true)
    end

    local objectDestroyed = inst.components.inventoryitem:OnPickup(self.inst, src_pos)
    if objectDestroyed then return end

    local can_use_suggested_slot = false

    if not slot and inst.prevslot and not inst.prevcontainer then slot = inst.prevslot end
    if not slot and inst.prevslot and inst.prevcontainer then
        if inst.prevcontainer.inst:IsValid() and inst.prevcontainer:IsOpenedBy(self.inst) then
            local item = inst.prevcontainer:GetItemInSlot(inst.prevslot)
            if item == nil then if inst.prevcontainer:GiveItem(inst, inst.prevslot) then return true end
            elseif ... stackable:Put(inst) == nil then return true end
        end
        inst.prevcontainer = nil; inst.prevslot = nil; slot = nil
    end
    ...
```

`prevslot` / `prevcontainer` 字段是**撤销记忆**：从箱子里拿出东西，它会记住它来自哪里；放回去时进同一个槽。约 20 行代码让箱子 juggling 变得顺手。

终止性兜底：

```lua
    --can't hold it!
    if self.activeitem == nil and self.maxslots > 0 and ValidateItemForOverflow(inst, self)
       and not (... isclientcontrollerattached) then
        inst.components.inventoryitem:OnPutInInventory(self.inst)
        self:SetActiveItem(inst)                     -- 放到光标上
        returnvalue = true
    elseif self.HandleLeftoversFn ~= nil then self.HandleLeftoversFn(self.inst, inst)
    else
        ... 叠进 activeitem，或 ...
        if self.HandleLeftoversShouldDropFn == nil or self.HandleLeftoversShouldDropFn(self.inst, inst) then
            self:DropItem(inst, true, true)          -- 弹回地面
        end
    end
    if shouldwisecrack and not (self.isloading or self.silentfull) and self.maxslots > 0 then
        self.inst:PushEvent("inventoryfull", { item = inst })
    end
```

`"inventoryfull"` 触发"我拿不下那个"的台词。`silentfull` 是一个重入保护位，在内部搬运周围设置，好让在容器之间移动物品不会刷屏台词。

### 8.4 `Equip`

`components/inventory.lua:1266`。有意思的规则：

```lua
    if item == nil or item.components.equippable == nil or not item:IsValid() or
        (not self.isloading and item.components.equippable:IsRestricted(self.inst) or self.isloading and item.components.equippable:IsRestricted_FromLoad(self.inst)) or
        (self.noheavylifting and item:HasTag("heavy"))
    then return end

    local eslot = item.components.equippable.equipslot
    local olditem = self.equipslots[eslot]
    if olditem ~= nil and olditem.components.equippable:ShouldPreventUnequipping() then return end

    -----
    --heavy lifting
    if item.components.equippable.equipslot == EQUIPSLOTS.HANDS then
        local heavyitem = self:GetEquippedItem(EQUIPSLOTS.BODY)
        if heavyitem ~= nil and heavyitem:HasTag("heavy") then self:DropItem(heavyitem, true, true) end
    elseif item.components.equippable.equipslot == EQUIPSLOTS.BODY and item:HasTag("heavy") then
        local handitem = self:GetEquippedItem(EQUIPSLOTS.HANDS)
        if handitem ~= nil then
            if handitem.components.inventoryitem.cangoincontainer then
                self.silentfull = true
                self:GiveItem(handitem, nil, force_ui_anim and self.inst:GetPosition() or nil)
                self.silentfull = false
            else self:DropItem(handitem, true, true) end
        end
    end
```

**要拿重物，双手必须是空的。** 捡起一块大理石会自动收好你的武器；装备武器会自动丢掉那块石头。然后：

```lua
        item.components.inventoryitem:OnPutInInventory(self.inst)
        item.components.equippable:Equip(self.inst, not old_to_active and item.prevslot == nil)
        self.equipslots[eslot] = item

        if eslot == EQUIPSLOTS.BODY then
            if item.components.container ~= nil then self.inst:PushEvent("setoverflow", { overflow = item }) end
            self.heavylifting = item:HasTag("heavy")
        elseif eslot == EQUIPSLOTS.HANDS then
            self.floaterheld = item.components.playerfloater and true
        end

        self.inst:PushEvent("equip", { item = item, eslot = eslot, no_animation = no_animation })
        if item.components.setbonus ~= nil then item.components.setbonus:UpdateSetBonus(self, true) end
```

`"setoverflow"` 就是让背包作为额外物品栏空间出现/消失的机制。`heavylifting` 和 `floaterheld` 会被镜像到副本并门控大量动作 —— grep 显示 `IsHeavyLifting()` 在 `actions.lua`、`componentactions.lua`，甚至 `brains/beefalobrain.lua`（牛羊牛不会跟随一个扛着重物的人）里被检查。**重量是能力门禁，不是速度乘数。**

### 8.5 `DropItem`

```lua
-- components/inventory.lua:721
function Inventory:DropItem(item, wholestack, randomdir, pos, keepoverstacked)
    if item == nil or item.components.inventoryitem == nil then return
    elseif item.components.inventoryitem.islockedinslot and (wholestack or not (item.components.stackable and item.components.stackable:IsStack())) then
        assert(BRANCH ~= "dev"); return
    end
    local dropped = item.components.inventoryitem:RemoveFromOwner(wholestack, keepoverstacked) or item
    if dropped ~= nil then
        if pos ~= nil then dropped.Transform:SetPosition(pos:Get())
        else dropped.Transform:SetPosition(self.inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()) end
        if dropped.components.inventoryitem ~= nil then dropped.components.inventoryitem:OnDropped(randomdir) end
        dropped.prevcontainer = nil
        dropped.prevslot = nil
        self.inst:PushEvent("dropitem", { item = dropped })
    end
    return dropped
end
```

`RemoveFromOwner(wholestack)` 返回的要么是物品本身，要么是一个新拆出来的堆成员 —— 调用方不关心是哪个。

### 8.6 堆叠 —— `components/stackable.lua`（213 行）

```lua
self.stacksize = 1
self.maxsize = TUNING.STACK_SIZE_MEDITEM     -- 20
```

`STACK_SIZE_LARGEITEM = 10`，`STACK_SIZE_MEDITEM = 20`，`STACK_SIZE_SMALLITEM = 40`。

```lua
function Stackable:CanStackWith(item)
    if self.inst.prefab ~= item.prefab then return false end
    if self.inst.skinname ~= item.skinname then return false end       -- 外观皮肤**不**堆叠
    if self.inst.stackable_CanStackWithFn and not self.inst.stackable_CanStackWithFn(self.inst, item) then return false end
    return true
end
```

`Put` 是每物品状态被合并的地方，也是大家经常忘记的部分：

```lua
function Stackable:Put(item, source_pos)
    if self:CanStackWith(item) then
        local num_to_add = item.components.stackable.stacksize
        local newtotal = self.stacksize + num_to_add
        local oldsize = self.stacksize
        local newsize = math.min(self.maxsize, newtotal)
        local numberadded = newsize - oldsize

        if self.inst.components.perishable ~= nil then
            self.inst.components.perishable:Dilute(numberadded, item.components.inventoryitem:GetPerishRemainingTime())
        end
        if self.inst.components.inventoryitem ~= nil then
            self.inst.components.inventoryitem:DiluteMoisture(item, numberadded)
            self.inst.components.inventoryitem:DiluteTemperature(item, numberadded)
        end
        if self.inst.components.edible ~= nil then self.inst.components.edible:DiluteChill(item, numberadded) end
        ...
        if self.maxsize >= newtotal then item:Remove()
        else item.components.stackable.stacksize = newtotal - self.maxsize; ret = item end
        self.stacksize = math.min(newsize, MAXUINT)
        self.inst:PushEvent("stacksizechange", {...})
    end
    return ret     -- 剩余部分，或 nil
end
```

加权平均稀释：

```lua
-- components/perishable.lua:168
function Perishable:Dilute(number, timeleft)
    if self.inst.components.stackable then
        if self.perishremainingtime or self.perishtime then
            local perishtime = self.perishremainingtime or self.perishtime
            self.perishremainingtime = (self.inst.components.stackable.stacksize * perishtime + number * timeleft) / (number + self.inst.components.stackable.stacksize)
```

把一颗新鲜的浆果并进一堆积体里，你得到的是平均值。`Stackable:Get(num)` 会拆分并把 `perishremainingtime`、湿度、温度、充能状态和诅咒状态复制到新实例上。

### 8.7 容器

`components/container.lua`（1651 行）结构上与 Inventory 相同但更简单：

```lua
self.slots = {}            -- [1..numslots] = inst
self.numslots = 0
self.canbeopened = true
self.acceptsstacks = true
self.usespecificslotsforitems = false
self.issidewidget = false
self.type = nil            -- "chest" | "pack" | "cooker" | ...
self.widget = nil          -- UI 布局
self.itemtestfn = nil      -- 每容器的接收过滤器
self.priorityfn = nil      -- 让它成为一个"专用容器"
self.openlist = {}
self.opencount = 0
--self.droponopen = false
--self.restrictedtag = nil  -- 只有带这个标签的实体能交互。
--self.thiefproof = nil
```

`WidgetSetup(prefab, data)` 从 `containers.lua` 复制每预制体的定义，然后**把属性锁成只读**（`makereadonly`），好让运行时没有任何东西能改布局：

```lua
-- components/container.lua:73
local widgetprops = { "numslots", "acceptsstacks", "usespecificslotsforitems", "issidewidget",
                      "type", "widget", "itemtestfn", "priorityfn", "openlimit" }

function Container:WidgetSetup(prefab, data)
    for i, v in ipairs(widgetprops) do removesetter(self, v) end
    containers.widgetsetup(self, prefab, data)
    self.inst.replica.container:WidgetSetup(prefab, data)
    for i, v in ipairs(widgetprops) do makereadonly(self, v) end
end
```

### 8.8 `containers.lua` —— 数据驱动的容器 DSL

2504 行、单一 `params` 表按预制体名索引，约 60 个容器定义：

```lua
-- containers.lua:9
function containers.widgetsetup(container, prefab, data)
    local t = data or params[prefab or container.inst.prefab]
    if t ~= nil then
        for k, v in pairs(t) do container[k] = v end
        container:SetNumSlots(container.widget.numslots or (container.widget.slotpos and #container.widget.slotpos or 0))
    end
end
```

**槽位数是从 UI 布局推导出来的**（`#widget.slotpos`）—— 你在物理上不可能让视觉网格和数据模型不一致。

```lua
-- containers.lua:1209  chest
params.treasurechest = {
    widget = { slotpos = {}, animbank = "ui_chest_3x3", animbuild = "ui_chest_3x3",
               animbank_upgraded = "ui_chest_upgraded_3x3", animbuild_upgraded = "ui_chest_upgraded_3x3",
               pos = Vector3(0, 200, 0), side_align_tip = 160 },
    type = "chest",
}
for y = 2, 0, -1 do
    for x = 0, 2 do
        table.insert(params.treasurechest.widget.slotpos, Vector3(80 * x - 80 * 2 + 80, 80 * y - 80 * 2 + 80, 0))
    end
end
params.pandoraschest = params.treasurechest     -- 按引用做别名（行为完全相同）
params.skullchest    = params.treasurechest
params.quagmire_safe = deepcopy(params.treasurechest)   -- 需要分叉时用 deepcopy
params.quagmire_safe.widget.animbank = "quagmire_ui_chest_3x3"
```

```lua
-- containers.lua:23  backpack
params.backpack = {
    widget = { slotpos = {}, animbank = "ui_backpack_2x4", animbuild = "ui_backpack_2x4", pos = Vector3(-5, -80, 0) },
    issidewidget = true,
    type = "pack",
    openlimit = 1,
}
for y = 0, 3 do
    table.insert(params.backpack.widget.slotpos, Vector3(-162, -75 * y + 114, 0))
    table.insert(params.backpack.widget.slotpos, Vector3(-162 + 75, -75 * y + 114, 0))
end
params.icepack = params.backpack
```

```lua
-- containers.lua:1000  icebox —— 3x3 带一个接收过滤器
params.icebox = { widget = { slotpos = {}, animbank = "ui_chest_3x3", ... }, type = "chest" }
...
function params.icebox.itemtestfn(container, item, slot)
    if item:HasAnyTag("icebox_valid", "inventoryitemtemperature") then return true end
    --Perishable
    if not item:HasAnyTag("fresh", "stale", "spoiled") then return false end
    if item:HasTag("smallcreature") then return false end
    --Edible
    for k, v in pairs(FOODTYPE) do
        if item:HasTag("edible_"..v) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
```

```lua
-- containers.lua:348  cookpot —— 一个同时也是机器的容器
params.cookpot = {
    widget = {
        slotpos = { Vector3(0, 64+32+8+4, 0), Vector3(0, 32+4, 0), Vector3(0, -(32+4), 0), Vector3(0, -(64+32+8+4), 0) },
        animbank = "ui_cookpot_1x4", animbuild = "ui_cookpot_1x4",
        pos = Vector3(200, 0, 0), side_align_tip = 100,
        buttoninfo = { text = STRINGS.ACTIONS.COOK, position = Vector3(0, -165, 0) },
    },
    acceptsstacks = false,          -- ★ 4 种不同的食材，不是 4 份相同的
    type = "cooker",
}
function params.cookpot.itemtestfn(container, item, slot)
    return cooking.IsCookingIngredient(item.prefab) and not container.inst:HasTag("burnt")
end
function params.cookpot.widget.buttoninfo.fn(inst, doer)
    if inst.components.container ~= nil then BufferedAction(doer, inst, ACTIONS.COOK):Do()
    elseif inst.replica.container ~= nil and not inst.replica.container:IsBusy() then
        SendRPCToServer(RPC.DoWidgetButtonAction, ACTIONS.COOK.code, inst, ACTIONS.COOK.mod_name)
    end
end
function params.cookpot.widget.buttoninfo.validfn(inst)
    return inst.replica.container ~= nil and inst.replica.container:IsFull()
end
```

五个正交的旋钮就能配出游戏里每一个容器：`numslots`/`slotpos`（容量 + 布局）、`itemtestfn`（什么能放）、`acceptsstacks`（重复品是否合并）、`type`/`issidewidget`（UI 摆放）、`priorityfn`（自动路由）。背包上的 `openlimit = 1` 防止两个玩家共用一个背包。

---

## 9. 制作

### 9.1 配方 DSL —— `recipe.lua`（244 行）

```lua
-- recipe.lua:5
Ingredient = Class(function(self, ingredienttype, amount, atlas, deconstruct, imageoverride)
    --Character ingredient multiples of 5 check only applies to health and sanity cost
    if ingredienttype == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.HEALTH or ingredienttype == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.SANITY then
        local x = tostring(amount)
        x = x:sub(x:find("^%-?%d+"))
        x = tonumber(x:sub(x:len()))
        assert(x == 0 or x == 5, "Character ingredients must be multiples of "..tostring(CHARACTER_INGREDIENT_SEG))
    end
    self.type = ingredienttype
    self.amount = amount
    ...
end)
```

一个 `Ingredient` 就是 `{type = prefabname, amount = n}`，附带可选的 UI 覆盖。构造时的那个 `assert` 是个很贴心的设计：**一条在加载时（而非运行时）执行的数据校验规则**。

```lua
-- recipe.lua:229 —— 现代 4 参数形式
Recipe2 = Class(Recipe, function(self, name, ingredients, tech, config)
    if config ~= nil then
        Recipe._ctor(self, name, ingredients, nil, tech, config, config.min_spacing, config.nounlock,
                     config.numtogive, config.builder_tag, config.atlas, config.image, config.testfn,
                     config.product, config.build_mode, config.build_distance)
    else
        Recipe._ctor(self, name, ingredients, nil, tech)
    end
    self.is_deconstruction_recipe = false
end)
```

材料会按类型**自动分进三个桶**：

```lua
-- recipe.lua:89
for k,v in pairs(ingredients) do
    table.insert(
        (IsCharacterIngredient(v.type) and self.character_ingredients) or
        (IsTechIngredient(v.type) and self.tech_ingredients) or
        self.ingredients,
        v
    )
end
```

- `ingredients` → 从物品栏消耗的物品
- `character_ingredients` → HEALTH / MAX_HEALTH / SANITY / MAX_SANITY，从**制作者自己的身体**里扣
- `tech_ingredients` → `*_material` 类型，检查你的科技等级而不是你的物品栏

其他值得注意的 `config` 字段（`recipe.lua:98-172`）：`product`（默认等于 `name`）、`numtogive`、`placer` + `min_spacing` + `testfn`（建筑类）、`builder_tag` / `no_builder_tag` / `builder_skill` / `no_builder_skill`（角色与技能树门禁）、`nounlock`（永不永久学会）、`canbuild`（自定义失败消息闸门）、`manufactured` + `station_tag`（由工作台生成物品而非制作者）、`forward_ingredients`（技能树配方替换）、`limitedamount` + `getlimitedrecipecount`、`dropitem`、`unlocks_from_skin`。

每个配方都会自我注册：`AllRecipes[name] = self`，并获得一个单调递增的 `sortkey`。`GetValidRecipe(recname)` 按游戏模式和当前特殊事件过滤。

### 9.2 真实配方（`recipes.lua`，1758 行）

```lua
Recipe2("axe",              {Ingredient("twigs", 1), Ingredient("flint", 1)},                        TECH.NONE)
Recipe2("torch",            {Ingredient("cutgrass", 2), Ingredient("twigs", 2)},                    TECH.NONE)
Recipe2("campfire",         {Ingredient("cutgrass", 3), Ingredient("log", 2)},                      TECH.NONE,
                            {placer="campfire_placer", min_spacing=2})
Recipe2("researchlab",      {Ingredient("goldnugget", 1), Ingredient("log", 4), Ingredient("rocks", 4)}, TECH.NONE,
                            {placer="researchlab_placer", min_spacing=2})
Recipe2("rope",             {Ingredient("cutgrass", 3)},                                            TECH.SCIENCE_ONE,
                            {no_builder_skill="walter_camp_rope", forward_ingredients={"walter_rope"}})
Recipe2("boards",           {Ingredient("log", 4)},                                                 TECH.SCIENCE_ONE,
                            {no_builder_tag="cancarveboards", forward_ingredients={"woodie_boards"}})
Recipe2("spear",            {Ingredient("twigs", 2), Ingredient("rope", 1), Ingredient("flint", 1)}, TECH.SCIENCE_ONE)

Recipe2("cookpot",          {Ingredient("cutstone", 3), Ingredient("charcoal", 6), Ingredient("twigs", 6)}, TECH.SCIENCE_ONE,
```
