
角色专属配方用 `builder_tag`：

```lua
Recipe2("lighter", {Ingredient("rope", 1), Ingredient("goldnugget", 1), Ingredient("petals", 3)}, TECH.NONE, {builder_tag="pyromaniac"})
```

### 9.3 科技树 —— 向量比较，不是图

`techtree.lua` 定义了约 40 条独立轴（`SCIENCE`、`MAGIC`、`ANCIENT`、`CELESTIAL`、`SHADOW`、`SEAFARING`、`CARTOGRAPHY`、`SCULPTING`、`ORPHANAGE`……）。`TechTree.Create(t)` 会把你没提到的每条轴补成 0：

```lua
local function Create(t)
    t = t or {}
    for i, v in ipairs(AVAILABLE_TECH) do t[v] = t[v] or 0 end
    return t
end
```

`constants.lua:1237`：

```lua
TECH = {
    NONE = TechTree.Create(),
    SCIENCE_ONE = { SCIENCE = 1 },
    SCIENCE_TWO = { SCIENCE = 2 },
    -- Magic starts at level 2 so it's not teased from the start.
    MAGIC_TWO = { MAGIC = 2 },
    MAGIC_THREE = { MAGIC = 3 },
    ANCIENT_TWO = { ANCIENT = 2 },
    ...
}
```

一台原型机机器就是一个科技向量（`tuning.lua:1256`）：

```lua
PROTOTYPER_TREES = {
    SCIENCEMACHINE   = TechTree.Create({ SCIENCE = 1, MAGIC = 1 }),
    ALCHEMYMACHINE   = TechTree.Create({ SCIENCE = 2, MAGIC = 1 }),
    PRESTIHATITATOR  = TechTree.Create({ MAGIC = 2 }),
    SHADOWMANIPULATOR= TechTree.Create({ MAGIC = 3 }),
    ANCIENTALTAR_LOW = TechTree.Create({ ANCIENT = 2 }),
    ...
}
```

在 `prefabs/scienceprototyper.lua:299` 接线：

```lua
return createmachine(1, "researchlab",  "lvl1", TUNING.PROTOTYPER_TREES.SCIENCEMACHINE, "science"),
       createmachine(2, "researchlab2", "lvl2", TUNING.PROTOTYPER_TREES.ALCHEMYMACHINE, "alchemy"), ...
```

而全部的解锁测试就是 8 行（`widgets/widgetutil.lua:16`）：

```lua
function CanPrototypeRecipe(recipetree, buildertree)
    for k, v in pairs(recipetree) do
        local v1 = buildertree[tostring(k)]
        if v ~= nil and v1 ~= nil and v > v1 then return false end
    end
    return true
end

function ShouldHintRecipe(recipetree, buildertree)
    if Profile:GetCraftingHintAllRecipesEnabled() then return true end
    for k, v in pairs(recipetree) do
        local v1 = buildertree[tostring(k)]
        if v ~= nil and v1 ~= nil and v > v1 + 1 then return false end   -- within +1 → tease it
    end
    return true
end
```

`ShouldHintRecipe` 就是"灰掉但可见、比你高一级"的预告规则 —— 一个 `+ 1`。

### 9.4 原型机邻近判定 —— `components/builder.lua:235`

```lua
local PROTOTYPER_TAGS = { "prototyper" }
...
function Builder:EvaluateTechTrees()
    local enabled = self.inst.player_classified == nil or self.inst.player_classified.iscraftingenabled:value()
    local pos = self.inst:GetPosition()
    ...
    if enabled and ents == nil then
        ents = TheSim:FindEntities(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, TUNING.RESEARCH_MACHINE_DIST, PROTOTYPER_TAGS, self.exclude_tags)
    end
    ...
    if ents then
        for i, v in ipairs(ents) do
            if v.components.prototyper and (v.components.prototyper.restrictedtag == nil or self.inst:HasTag(v.components.prototyper.restrictedtag)) then
                if not prototyper_active then
                    --activate the first machine in the list. This will be the one you're closest to.
                    v.components.prototyper:TurnOn(self.inst)
                    --prototyper:GetTrees() returns a deepcopy, which we no longer want
                    CopyTechTrees(v.components.prototyper.trees, self.accessible_tech_trees)
                    if v.components.craftingstation then
                        local recs = v.components.craftingstation:GetRecipes(self.inst)
                        for _, recname in ipairs(recs) do
                            local recipe = GetValidRecipe(recname)
                            if recipe and recipe.nounlock then
                                --only nounlock recipes can be unlocked via crafting station
                                ...
                                self.station_recipes[recname] = ...
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    prototyper_active = true
                    self.current_prototyper = v
                else
                    --you've already activated a machine. Turn all the other machines off.
                    v.components.prototyper:TurnOff(self.inst)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    ...
    --add any character specific bonuses to your current tech levels.
    CopyTechTrees(self.accessible_tech_trees, self.accessible_tech_trees_no_temp)
    if not prototyper_active then
        for i, v in ipairs(TechTree.AVAILABLE_TECH) do
            self.accessible_tech_trees_no_temp[v] = (self[TechTree.AVAILABLE_TECH_BONUS[v] ...] or 0)
            self.accessible_tech_trees[v] = (self[..._TEMPBONUS[v] ...] or 0) + (self[..._BONUS[v] ...] or 0)
        end
    else
        for i, v in ipairs(TechTree.BONUS_TECH) do ... end
    end
    ...
    if trees_changed then
        self.inst:PushEvent("techtreechange", { level = self.accessible_tech_trees })
        self.inst.replica.builder:SetTechTrees(self.accessible_tech_trees)
    end
end
```

`RESEARCH_MACHINE_DIST = 4`。**只有最近的机器算数** —— 在科学机器和炼金引擎之间走动**不会**把它们的向量取并集。`prototyper.lua` 维护一个引用计数的 `doers` 集合，好让机器的灯光/音效只在真的有人在用时才跑。

三个科技来源组合在一起：

1. **原型机向量** —— 位置性的，离开就没了。
2. **`recipes` 列表** —— 永久解锁（`UnlockRecipe`）。
3. **`*_bonus` / `*_tempbonus`** —— 角色永久加成与消耗品临时 Buff。`accessible_tech_trees_no_temp` 追踪"不带 buff"的版本，好让代码能判断"你只是因为临时 buff 才知道这个"。

### 9.5 `KnowsRecipe` 与 `HasIngredients`

```lua
-- components/builder.lua:841 (节选)
function Builder:KnowsRecipe(recipe, ignore_tempbonus, cached_tech_trees)
    if type(recipe) == "string" then recipe = GetValidRecipe(recipe) end
    if recipe == nil then return false
    elseif self.freebuildmode and not PREFAB_SKINS_SHOULD_NOT_SELECT[recipe.product] then return true end
    ...
    --the following builder_tag/skill checks are require due to character swapping
    if (recipe.builder_tag and not self.inst:HasTag(recipe.builder_tag)) or
       (recipe.no_builder_tag and self.inst:HasTag(recipe.no_builder_tag)) then return false end
    local skilltreeupdater = self.inst.components.skilltreeupdater
    if (recipe.builder_skill and not (skilltreeupdater and skilltreeupdater:IsActivated(recipe.builder_skill))) or
       (recipe.no_builder_skill and skilltreeupdater and skilltreeupdater:IsActivated(recipe.no_builder_skill)) then return false end

    if self.station_recipes[recipe.name] or table.contains(self.recipes, recipe.name) then ... return true end

    for i, v in ipairs(TechTree.AVAILABLE_TECH) do
        local bonusvalue = (self[TechTree.AVAILABLE_TECH_BONUS[v] ...] or 0)
        if not ignore_tempbonus then bonusvalue = bonusvalue + (self[..._TEMPBONUS[v] ...] or 0) end
        if recipe.level[v] > bonusvalue then ... return false end
    end
    return true
end

function Builder:HasIngredients(recipe)
    if type(recipe) == "string" then recipe = GetValidRecipe(recipe) end
    if recipe ~= nil then
        if self.freebuildmode then return true end
        if recipe.getlimitedrecipecount and recipe:getlimitedrecipecount(self.inst) <= 0 then return false end
        for i, v in ipairs(recipe.ingredients) do
            if not self.inst.components.inventory:Has(v.type, math.max(1, RoundBiasedUp(v.amount * self.ingredientmod)), true) then
                return false
            end
        end
        for i, v in ipairs(recipe.character_ingredients) do if not self:HasCharacterIngredient(v) then return false end end
        for i, v in ipairs(recipe.tech_ingredients)      do if not self:HasTechIngredient(v)      then return false end end
        return true
    end
    return false
end
```

**两个刻意分开的问题**："我能做这个吗？"（`KnowsRecipe`）与"我材料够吗？"（`HasIngredients`）。UI 在任一失败时把配方灰掉，并按原因显示不同的美术资源。

`ingredientmod` 是一个全局折扣乘数；`RoundBiasedUp` 意味着对 1 个物品打 0.9 折仍然是 1（永远不免费），但对 4 个打 0.9 折是 3.6 → 4。`inventory:Has(..., true)` 传 `checkallcontainers = true`，这就是为什么开着的箱子能算进制作：

```lua
-- components/inventory.lua:1507 (节选)
function Inventory:Has(item, amount, checkallcontainers)
    local iscrafting = checkallcontainers
    local num_found = 0
    for k, v in pairs(self.itemslots) do
        if v and v.prefab == item and not (iscrafting and v:HasTag("nocrafting")) then
            num_found = num_found + (v.components.stackable ~= nil and v.components.stackable:StackSize() or 1)
        end
    end
    ... activeitem, overflow container ...
    if checkallcontainers then
        for container_inst in pairs(self.opencontainers) do
            local container = container_inst.components.container or container_inst.components.inventory
            if container and container ~= overflow and not container.excludefromcrafting and not container.readonlycontainer then
                local _, container_found = container:Has(item, amount, iscrafting)
                num_found = num_found + container_found
            end
        end
    end
    return num_found >= amount, num_found
end
```

### 9.6 `MakeRecipe` → `BufferedAction` → `DoBuild`

制作**不是同步的**。`MakeRecipe` 排入一个移动+建造动作：

```lua
-- components/builder.lua:629
function Builder:MakeRecipe(recipe, pt, rot, skin, onsuccess)
    if recipe and not self.inst.sg:HasAnyStateTag("drowning", "falling", "floating", "nointerrupt", "nopredict", "pausepredict") then
        self.inst:PushEvent("makerecipe", { recipe = recipe })
        if self:IsBuildBuffered(recipe.name) or self:HasIngredients(recipe) then
            self.inst.components.locomotor:Stop()
            local buffaction = BufferedAction(self.inst, nil, ACTIONS.BUILD, nil, pt or self.inst:GetPosition(), recipe.name, recipe.build_distance, nil, rot)
            buffaction.skin = skin
            if onsuccess ~= nil then buffaction:AddSuccessAction(onsuccess) end
            self.inst.components.locomotor:PushAction(buffaction, true)
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
```

这就是制作有动画、可以边跑边排队、客户端能预测的原因。`onsuccess` 回调正是*解锁*发生的地方：

```lua
-- components/builder.lua:1041 (节选)
function Builder:MakeRecipeFromMenu(recipe, skin)
    if not self.inst.components.inventory:IsOpenedBy(self.inst) then return end
    if self:HasIngredients(recipe) then
        if recipe.placer == nil then
            local knows_no_temp = self:KnowsRecipe(recipe, true)
            local canproto_no_temp = CanPrototypeRecipe(recipe.level, self.accessible_tech_trees_no_temp)
            local canlearn = self:CanLearn(recipe.name)
            local usingtempbonus = not knows_no_temp and not canproto_no_temp

            if self:KnowsRecipe(recipe) then
                self:MakeRecipe(recipe, nil, nil, ValidateRecipeSkinRequest(self.inst.userid, recipe.product, skin),
                    function()
                        if usingtempbonus then self:ConsumeTempTechBonuses() end
                        if self.freebuildmode then
                            TryActivateCurrentResearchMachineOnFreeBuild(self, recipe)
                        elseif not knows_no_temp and canproto_no_temp and canlearn then
                            self:ActivateCurrentResearchMachine(recipe)
                            self:UnlockRecipe(recipe.name)          -- ★ 第一次制作 = 永久解锁
                        elseif not recipe.nounlock then
                            self:AddRecipe(recipe.name)
                        else
                            self:ActivateCurrentResearchMachine(recipe)
                        end
                    end)
            elseif canlearn and CanPrototypeRecipe(recipe.level, self.accessible_tech_trees) then
                self:MakeRecipe(recipe, nil, nil, ..., function() ... self:ActivateCurrentResearchMachine(recipe); self:UnlockRecipe(recipe.name) end)
            end
        end
    else
        -- recursive ingredient crafting
        for i, ing in ipairs(recipe.ingredients) do
            local ing_recipe = GetValidRecipe(ing.type)
            if ing_recipe and not self.inst.components.inventory:Has(ing.type, math.max(1, RoundBiasedUp(ing.amount * self.ingredientmod)), true) then
                local success, canaccess = _TryMakeIngredientRecipe(self, ing_recipe)
                if not canaccess and ing_recipe.forward_ingredients then
                    for i, v in ipairs(ing_recipe.forward_ingredients) do
                        ing_recipe = GetValidRecipe(v)
                        if ing_recipe then success, canaccess = _TryMakeIngredientRecipe(self, ing_recipe); if canaccess then break end end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
```

有两点值得偷：

- **在制作 `spear` 时自动先把 `rope` 做出来。** 点击一个做不出来的配方时，它会遍历材料树，把缺失的中间产物先造出来。
- `UnlockRecipe` 给出理智奖励：
```lua
function Builder:UnlockRecipe(recname, nosanity)
    local recipe = GetValidRecipe(recname)
    if recipe ~= nil and not recipe.nounlock then
        if self.inst.components.sanity ~= nil and not nosanity then
            self.inst.components.sanity:DoDelta(TUNING.SANITY_MED)     -- +15
        end
        self:AddRecipe(recname)
        self.inst:PushEvent("unlockrecipe", { recipe = recname })
    end
end
```
**原型制造本身就是一次理智奖励。** "科学"循环字面意义上就是：探索 → 找资源 → 做出新东西 → +15 理智。这让理智经济与黑暗流失形成闭环。

### 9.7 `DoBuild` —— 真正的建造

```lua
-- components/builder.lua:657 (节选)
function Builder:DoBuild(recname, pt, rotation, skin)
    local recipe = GetValidRecipe(recname)
    if recipe ~= nil and (self:IsBuildBuffered(recname) or self:HasIngredients(recipe)) and not PREFAB_SKINS_SHOULD_NOT_SELECT[skin] then
        if recipe.placer ~= nil and self.inst.components.rider ~= nil and self.inst.components.rider:IsRiding() then
            return false, "MOUNTED"
        elseif recipe.level.ORPHANAGE > 0 and (...) then return false, "HASPET"
        elseif recipe.manufactured and (self.current_prototyper == nil or ...) then return false end
        ...
        if recipe.canbuild ~= nil then
            local success, msg = recipe.canbuild(recipe, self.inst, pt, rotation, self.current_prototyper, skin)
            if not success then return false, msg end
        end
        ...
        local materials, discounted = self:GetIngredients(recname)
        if self:CheckIngredientsForMimic(materials) or (discounted and self:CheckDiscountEquipsForMimic()) then
            return false, "ITEMMIMIC"
        end
        if recipe.manufactured then
            self:RemoveIngredients(materials, recname, discounted)
            -- its up to the prototyper to implement onactivate and handle spawning the prefab
            return true
        end

        local prod = SpawnPrefab(recipe.product, recipe.chooseskin or skin, nil, self.inst.userid) or nil
        if prod ~= nil then
            pt = pt or self.inst:GetPosition()
            if prod.components.inventoryitem ~= nil then
                if self.inst.components.inventory ~= nil then
                    local wetlevel = self:GetIngredientWetness(materials)
                    if wetlevel > 0 then prod.components.inventoryitem:InheritMoisture(wetlevel, self.inst:GetIsWet()) end
                    if prod.onPreBuilt ~= nil then prod:onPreBuilt(self.inst, materials, recipe) end
                    self:RemoveIngredients(materials, recname, discounted)
                    self.inst:PushEvent("builditem", { item = prod, recipe = recipe, skin = skin, prototyper = self.current_prototyper })
                    ...
                    local numtogive = recipe.override_numtogive_fn and recipe.override_numtogive_fn(recipe, self.inst) or recipe.numtogive
                    if prod.components.equippable ~= nil and not recipe.dropitem
                       and self.inst.components.inventory:GetEquippedItem(prod.components.equippable.equipslot) == nil
                       and not prod.components.equippable:IsRestricted(self.inst) then
                        if numtogive <= 1 then self.inst.components.inventory:Equip(prod)     -- ★ 自动装备
                        elseif prod.components.stackable ~= nil then prod.components.stackable:SetStackSize(numtogive); self.inst.components.inventory:Equip(prod)
                        else self.inst.components.inventory:Equip(prod); for i = 2, numtogive do ... GiveItem(addt_prod, nil, pt) end end
                    elseif numtogive <= 1 then GiveOrDropItem(self, recipe, prod, pt)
                    elseif prod.components.stackable ~= nil then prod.components.stackable:SetStackSize(numtogive); GiveOrDropItem(...)
                    else GiveOrDropItem(...); for i = 2, numtogive do ... end end

                    prod:OnBuilt(self.inst)
                    return true
                else prod:Remove(); prod = nil end
            else
                -- structures
                if not is_buffered_build then self:RemoveIngredients(materials, recname, discounted) end
                ...
                prod.Transform:SetPosition(spawn_pos:Get())
                prod.Transform:SetRotation(rotation or 0)
                self.inst:PushEvent("buildstructure", { item = prod, recipe = recipe, skin = skin })
                prod:PushEvent("onbuilt", { builder = self.inst, pos = pt })
                prod:OnBuilt(self.inst)
                return true
            end
        end
    end
end
```

值得抄的细节：**做出来的物品继承其材料的湿度**（`GetIngredientWetness` 对所有材料求 `湿度 × 数量` 之和）；**一个可装备的产物在对应槽位空着时会自动装备**；建筑和物品走的是完全不同的路径（有没有 `inventoryitem` 组件）。

角色代价材料在这里被扣除：

```lua
-- components/builder.lua:546 (节选)
function Builder:RemoveIngredients(ingredients, recname, discounted)
    if self.freebuildmode then return end
    for item, ents in pairs(ingredients) do
        for k,v in pairs(ents) do
            for i = 1, v do
                local item = self.inst.components.inventory:RemoveItem(k, false, true)
                -- If the item we're crafting with is a container, drop the contained items onto the ground.
                if item.components.container ~= nil then item.components.container:DropEverything(self.inst:GetPosition()) end
                item:Remove()
            end
        end
    end
    local recipe = AllRecipes[recname]
    if recipe then
        for k,v in pairs(recipe.character_ingredients) do
            if v.type == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.HEALTH then
                self.inst:PushEvent("consumehealthcost")
                self.inst.components.health:DoDelta(-v.amount, false, "builder", true, nil, true)   -- ignore_invincible AND ignore_absorb
            elseif v.type == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.MAX_HEALTH then
                self.inst:PushEvent("consumehealthcost")
                self.inst.components.health:DeltaPenalty(v.amount)
            elseif v.type == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.SANITY then
                self.inst.components.sanity:DoDelta(-v.amount)
            elseif v.type == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.MAX_SANITY then
                --[[ Removal of sanity is actually managed by the entity that is created.
                     See maxwell's pet leash on spawn and pet on death functions for examples. ]]
            end
        end
    end
    self.inst:PushEvent("consumeingredients", { discounted = discounted })
end
```

`HasCharacterIngredient` 拒绝让制作杀死你：

```lua
if ingredient.type == CHARACTER_INGREDIENT.HEALTH then
    local amount_required = self.inst:HasTag("health_as_oldage") and math.ceil(ingredient.amount * TUNING.OLDAGE_HEALTH_SCALE) or ingredient.amount
    local current = math.ceil(self.inst.components.health.currenthealth)
    return current > amount_required, current     --Don't die from crafting!
```

### 9.8 制作菜单 UI

`recipes_filter.lua` 把筛选标签声明为纯数据（`CRAFTING_FILTER_DEFS`）：`FAVORITES`、`CRAFTING_STATION`、`SPECIAL_EVENT`、`MODS`、`CHARACTER`、`TOOLS`、`LIGHT`、`PROTOTYPERS`、`REFINE`、`WEAPONS`、`ARMOUR`、`CLOTHING`、`RESTORATION`、`MAGIC`、`DECOR`、`STRUCTURES`、`CONTAINERS`、`COOKING`、`GARDENING`、`FISHING`、`SEAFARING`、`RIDING`、`WINTER`、`SUMMER`、`RAIN`、`EVERYTHING`。每个都有显式的配方名列表（`CRAFTING_FILTERS.TOOLS.recipes = {...}`）。

`crafting_sorting.lua` 在其上叠加用户排序/收藏：它为每个筛选构建一个 `sorted` 数组 + `unsorted` 集合，好让新配方（包括模组的）自动追加到末尾而不是破坏用户的自定义顺序。

控件：`widgets/crafting.lua`（可滚动的 HUD 条 —— `CraftSlots`、上下按钮、`UpdateRecipes`）、`widgets/craftslots.lua`、`widgets/craftslot.lua`、`widgets/crafttabs.lua`、`widgets/mousecrafting.lua`、`widgets/controllercrafting.lua`，以及全屏的 redux 套件 `widgets/craftingmenu_widget.lua` / `_details.lua` / `_ingredients.lua` / `_pinbar.lua` / `_pinslot.lua` / `_skinselector.lua`。`recipes.lua` 里的 `PROTOTYPER_DEFS` 提供每个工作台的图标和动词（`action_str = "USE"`、`"STUDY"`、`"FORGE"`、`"TRADE"`、`"SCULPTING"`……）。

---

## 10. 烹饪

### 10.1 材料标签数据库 —— `cooking.lua`（324 行）

```lua
-- cooking.lua:46
local ingredients = {}
function AddIngredientValues(names, tags, cancook, candry)
    for _,name in pairs(names) do
        ingredients[name] = { tags = {} }
        if cancook then ingredients[name.."_cooked"] = {tags={}} end
        if candry  then ingredients[name.."_dried"]  = {tags={}} end
        for tagname,tagval in pairs(tags) do
            ingredients[name].tags[tagname] = tagval
            if cancook then
                ingredients[name.."_cooked"].tags.precook = 1
                ingredients[name.."_cooked"].tags[tagname] = tagval
            end
            if candry then
                ingredients[name.."_dried"].tags.dried = 1
                ingredients[name.."_dried"].tags[tagname] = tagval
            end
        end
    end
end
```

一次调用就同时声明了原材料*并*自动派生出它的 `_cooked` / `_dried` 变体，带相同标签外加 `precook = 1` / `dried = 1`。完整标签词汇表在注释里：**`fruit, monster, sweetener, veggie, meat, fish, egg, decoration, fat, dairy, inedible, seed, magic`**（外加 `frozen`、`precook`、`dried`）。

标签是**分数权重，不是布尔**：

```lua
AddIngredientValues({"berries"}, {fruit=.5}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"pomegranate", "dragonfruit", "cave_banana"}, {fruit=1}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"meat"}, {meat=1}, true, true)
AddIngredientValues({"monstermeat"}, {meat=1, monster=1}, true, true)
AddIngredientValues({"froglegs", "drumstick", "batwing"}, {meat=.5}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"eel"}, {meat=.5, fish=1}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"honey", "honeycomb"}, {sweetener=1}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"royal_jelly"}, {sweetener=3}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"tallbirdegg"}, {egg=4}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"butterflywings"}, {decoration=2})
AddIngredientValues({"twigs"}, {inedible=1})
AddIngredientValues({"ice"}, {frozen=1})
AddIngredientValues({"nightmarefuel"}, {inedible=1, magic=1})
AddIngredientValues({"carrot", "corn", "pumpkin", "eggplant", "cutlichen", "asparagus", "onion", "garlic", "tomato", "potato", "pepper"}, {veggie=1}, true)
AddIngredientValues({"red_cap", "green_cap", "blue_cap", "moon_cap"}, {veggie=.5}, true)
```

所以"1 块肉"和"2 条蛙腿"是*同样多的肉量*。这就是配方测试读起来自然的原因。

一个别名表处理命名不一致：

```lua
local aliases = { cookedsmallmeat = "smallmeat_cooked", cookedmonstermeat = "monstermeat_cooked", cookedmeat = "meat_cooked" }
local function IsCookingIngredient(prefabname)
    return ingredients[aliases[prefabname] or prefabname] ~= nil
end
```

### 10.2 匹配算法

```lua
-- cooking.lua:217
local function GetIngredientValues(prefablist)
    local prefabs = {}
    local tags = {}
    for k,v in pairs(prefablist) do
        local name = aliases[v] or v
        prefabs[name] = (prefabs[name] or 0) + 1                 -- 按 prefab 计数
        local data = ingredients[name]
        if data ~= nil then
            for kk, vv in pairs(data.tags) do tags[kk] = (tags[kk] or 0) + vv end   -- 按标签求和
        end
    end
    return { tags = tags, names = prefabs }
end

function GetCandidateRecipes(cooker, ingdata)
    local recipes = cookerrecipes[cooker] or {}
    local candidates = {}
    --find all potentially valid recipes
    for k,v in pairs(recipes) do
        if v.test(cooker, ingdata.names, ingdata.tags) then table.insert(candidates, v) end
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(a,b) return (a.priority or 0) > (b.priority or 0) end)
    if #candidates > 0 then
        --find the set of highest priority recipes
        local top_candidates = {}
        local val = candidates[1].priority or 0
        for k,v in ipairs(candidates) do
            if k > 1 and (v.priority or 0) < val then break end
            table.insert(top_candidates, v)
        end
        return top_candidates
    end
    return candidates
end

local function CalculateRecipe(cooker, names)
    local ingdata = GetIngredientValues(names)
    local candidates = GetCandidateRecipes(cooker, ingdata)
    table.sort(candidates, function(a,b) return (a.weight or 1) > (b.weight or 1) end)
    local total = 0
    for k,v in pairs(candidates) do total = total + (v.weight or 1) end
    local val = math.random()*total
    local idx = 1
    while idx <= #candidates do
        val = val - candidates[idx].weight
        if val <= 0 then return candidates[idx].name, candidates[idx].cooktime or 1 end
        idx = idx+1
    end
end
```

**四个阶段：谓词过滤 → 优先级去重 → 加权随机 → 返回 `(name, cooktime)`。**

优先级是一个*层级*，不是一个分数：所有处于最高优先级的配方被保留下来，然后按权重随机挑一个。这就是同样的 4 种材料能产出不同菜肴、同时"招牌菜"（`priority = 10` 击败通用的 `priority = 1`）仍然感觉确定的原因。

### 10.3 配方形态（`preparedfoods.lua`，1106 行）

```lua
butterflymuffin = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return (names.butterflywings or names.moonbutterflywings) and not tags.meat and tags.veggie and tags.veggie >= 0.5 end,
    priority = 1,
    weight = 1,
    foodtype = FOODTYPE.VEGGIE,
    health = TUNING.HEALING_MED,          -- 20
    hunger = TUNING.CALORIES_LARGE,       -- 37.5
    perishtime = TUNING.PERISH_SLOW,      -- 15 天
    sanity = TUNING.SANITY_TINY,          -- 5
    cooktime = 2,
    floater = {"small", 0.05, 0.7},
    card_def = {ingredients = {{"butterflywings", 1}, {"carrot", 2}, {"berries", 1}} },
},

taffy = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return tags.sweetener and tags.sweetener >= 3 and not tags.meat end,
    priority = 10, foodtype = FOODTYPE.GOODIES,
    health = -TUNING.HEALING_SMALL, hunger = TUNING.CALORIES_SMALL*2,
    perishtime = TUNING.PERISH_SLOW, sanity = TUNING.SANITY_MED,
    cooktime = 2, tags = {"honeyed"},
},

fishsticks = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return tags.fish and names.twigs and (tags.inedible and tags.inedible <= 1) end,
    priority = 10, ... health = TUNING.HEALING_LARGE, hunger = TUNING.CALORIES_LARGE, potlevel = "high", tags = {"catfood"},
},

honeynuggets = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return names.honey and tags.meat and tags.meat <= 1.5 and not tags.inedible end,
    priority = 2, ...
},
honeyham = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return names.honey and tags.meat and tags.meat > 1.5 and not tags.inedible end,
    priority = 2, ... health = TUNING.HEALING_MEDLARGE, hunger = TUNING.CALORIES_HUGE, temperature = TUNING.HOT_FOOD_BONUS_TEMP, ...
},
```

以及那个保证的兜底项：

```lua
wetgoop = {
    test = function(cooker, names, tags) return true end,
    priority = -10,
    health=0, hunger=0, perishtime = TUNING.PERISH_FAST, sanity = 0, cooktime = .25,
    wet_prefix = STRINGS.WET_PREFIX.WETGOOP,
},
```

`priority = -10` 意味着它只在没有其他任何东西匹配时才获胜。**一个全函数（total function）配方系统，在优先级阶梯的底部恰好需要一条恒真条目。**

配方按炊具类型注册（`cooking.lua:189`）：

```lua
local foods = require("preparedfoods")
for k,recipe in pairs(foods) do
    AddCookerRecipe("cookpot", recipe)
    AddCookerRecipe("portablecookpot", recipe)
    AddCookerRecipe("archive_cookpot", recipe)
    if recipe.card_def then AddRecipeCard("cookpot", recipe) end
end
AddCookerRecipe("portablecookpot", ...) -- preparedfoods_warly（Warly 专属菜肴）
AddCookerRecipe("portablespicer", ...)  -- spicedfoods
AddCookerRecipe("cookpot", ...)         -- preparednonfoods
```

`cookerrecipes[cooker][recipename] = recipe` —— 炊具预制体名就是命名空间，所以模组只需调用 `AddCookerRecipe("cookpot", myrecipe)` 就能加菜。

### 10.4 锅机制 —— `components/stewer.lua`（361 行）

```lua
-- components/stewer.lua:130
function Stewer:StartCooking(doer)
    if self.targettime == nil and self.inst.components.container ~= nil then
        self.chef_id = (doer ~= nil and doer.player_classified ~= nil) and doer.userid
        self.ingredient_prefabs = {}
        self.done = nil
        self.spoiltime = nil
        if self.onstartcooking ~= nil then self.onstartcooking(self.inst) end

        for k, v in pairs(self.inst.components.container.slots) do
            table.insert(self.ingredient_prefabs, v.prefab)
        end

        local cooktime = 1
        self.product, cooktime = cooking.CalculateRecipe(self.inst.prefab, self.ingredient_prefabs)
        local productperishtime = cooking.GetRecipe(self.inst.prefab, self.product).perishtime or 0

        if productperishtime > 0 then
            local spoilage_total, spoilage_n = 0, 0
            for k, v in pairs(self.inst.components.container.slots) do
                if v.components.perishable ~= nil then
                    spoilage_n = spoilage_n + 1
                    spoilage_total = spoilage_total + v.components.perishable:GetPercent()
                end
            end
            self.product_spoilage =
                (spoilage_n <= 0 and 1) or
                (self.keepspoilage and spoilage_total / spoilage_n) or
                1 - (1 - spoilage_total / spoilage_n) * .5
        else self.product_spoilage = nil end

        cooktime = TUNING.BASE_COOK_TIME * cooktime * self.cooktimemult
        self.targettime = GetTime() + cooktime
        if self.task ~= nil then self.task:Cancel() end
        self.task = self.inst:DoTaskInTime(cooktime, dostew, self)

        self.inst.components.container:Close()
        self.inst.components.container:DestroyContents()
        self.inst.components.container.canbeopened = false
    end
end
```

关键机制：

- `cooktime = BASE_COOK_TIME（night_time*.3333 = 20 秒）× recipe.cooktime × cooktimemult`（实测 `tuning.lua:2087`）。一道 `cooktime = 2` 的菜要 40 秒；`wetgoop` 的 0.25 只要 5 秒（即时的失败反馈）。
- **成品新鲜度 = 材料平均新鲜度的部分刷新**：`1 - (1 - 平均值) * 0.5`。烹饪半腐的肉给你 75% 新鲜的食物，而不是 50%。`keepspoilage = true` 则是直接取平均。
- 材料被**立即销毁**（`DestroyContents`），容器被锁（`canbeopened = false`）—— 你不能在烹饪中途偷看或作弊。
- `chef_id` + `ingredient_prefabs` 被保存，纯粹是为了让食谱书能归属"发现"。

```lua
-- components/stewer.lua:75
local function dostew(inst, self)
    self.task = nil; self.targettime = nil; self.spoiltime = nil
    if self.ondonecooking ~= nil then self.ondonecooking(inst) end
    if self.product == self.spoiledproduct then
        if self.onspoil ~= nil then self.onspoil(inst) end
    elseif self.product ~= nil then
        local recipe = cooking.GetRecipe(inst.prefab, self.product)
        local prep_perishtime = (recipe ~= nil and (recipe.cookpot_perishtime or recipe.perishtime)) or 0
        if prep_perishtime > 0 then
            local prod_spoil = self.product_spoilage or 1
            self.spoiltime = prep_perishtime * prod_spoil
            self.targettime = GetTime() + self.spoiltime
            self.task = self.inst:DoTaskInTime(self.spoiltime, dospoil, self)
        end
    end
    self.done = true
end
```

然后第二个计时器启动：**饭菜留在锅里会腐坏**。把晚饭在锅里放一整夜是一个真实的代价。

```lua
-- components/stewer.lua:276
function Stewer:Harvest(harvester)
    if self.done then
        if self.onharvest ~= nil then self.onharvest(self.inst) end
        if self.product ~= nil then
            local loot = SpawnPrefab(self.product)
            if loot ~= nil then
                local recipe = cooking.GetRecipe(self.inst.prefab, self.product)
                if harvester ~= nil and self.chef_id == harvester.userid and recipe ~= nil and recipe.cookbook_category ~= nil
                   and cooking.cookbook_recipes[recipe.cookbook_category] ~= nil
                   and cooking.cookbook_recipes[recipe.cookbook_category][self.product] ~= nil then
                    harvester:PushEvent("learncookbookrecipe", {product = self.product, ingredients = self.ingredient_prefabs})
                end
                local stacksize = recipe and recipe.stacksize or 1
                if stacksize > 1 then loot.components.stackable:SetStackSize(stacksize) end
                if self.spoiltime ~= nil and loot.components.perishable ~= nil then
                    local spoilpercent = self:GetTimeToSpoil() / self.spoiltime
                    loot.components.perishable:SetPercent(self.product_spoilage * spoilpercent)
                    loot.components.perishable:StartPerishing()
                end
                if harvester ~= nil and harvester.components.inventory ~= nil then
                    harvester.components.inventory:GiveItem(loot, nil, self.inst:GetPosition())
                else LaunchAt(loot, self.inst, nil, 1, 1) end
            end
            self.product = nil
        end
        ...
        if self.inst.components.container ~= nil then self.inst.components.container.canbeopened = true end
        return true
    end
end
```

**只有做饭的那个人学会配方**（`self.chef_id == harvester.userid`）—— 多人的食谱发现循环全在这一行里。`cookbookdata.lua` 通过 `TheSim:SetPersistentString("cookbook", ...)` 把 `preparedfoods` 持久化为 JSON，`EncodeCookbookEntry` 存 `"ing1,ing2,ing3,ing4|has_eaten"` 字符串，另有 `ApplyOnlineProfileData()` 兜底以应对本地文件损坏。

直接明火烹饪是另一个简单得多的组件（`components/cooker.lua`，52 行）+ `components/cookable.lua`（40 行）：`Cooker:CookItem(item, chef)` → `item.components.cookable:Cook(cooker, chef)` → 生成 `product` 预制体，移除原件。由 `CanCook` 门控：有燃料、不在燃烧中、不在被投掷中，且 `dangerouscooker` 需要 `expertchef`。

### 10.5 腐败 —— `components/perishable.lua`（353 行）

```lua
-- perishable.lua:1
local function onpercent(self)
    if self.perishremainingtime ~= nil then
        local percent = self:GetPercent()
        if percent >= TUNING.PERISH_FRESH then          -- 0.5
            if not self.inst:HasTag("fresh") then ...AddTag("fresh")... end
        elseif percent > TUNING.PERISH_STALE then       -- 0.2
            if not self.inst:HasTag("stale") then ...AddTag("stale")... end
        elseif not self.inst:HasTag("spoiled") then ...AddTag("spoiled")... end
    end
end
```

倍率堆叠（`perishable.lua:60`，`Update`）：

```lua
local ambient_temperature = inside_pocket_container and TheWorld.state.temperature or GetTemperatureAtXZ(pos.x, pos.z)
if owner then
    if owner.components.preserver ~= nil then modifier = owner.components.preserver:GetPerishRateMultiplier(inst) or modifier
    elseif owner:HasTag("fridge") then
        if inst:HasTag("frozen") and not owner:HasAnyTag("nocool", "lowcool") then modifier = TUNING.PERISH_COLD_FROZEN_MULT   -- 0
        else modifier = TUNING.PERISH_FRIDGE_MULT end                                                                          -- 0.5
    elseif owner:HasTag("foodpreserver") then modifier = TUNING.PERISH_FOOD_PRESERVER_MULT                                     -- 0.75
    elseif owner:HasTag("cage") and inst:HasTag("small_livestock") then modifier = TUNING.PERISH_CAGE_MULT end                 -- 0.25
    if owner:HasTag("spoiler") then modifier = modifier * TUNING.PERISH_GROUND_MULT end                                        -- 1.5
else
    modifier = TUNING.PERISH_GROUND_MULT     -- 地面上是 1.5
    ... 酸雨额外衰减 ...
end
if inst:GetIsWet() and not self.ignorewentness then modifier = modifier * TUNING.PERISH_WET_MULT end                            -- 1.3
if ambient_temperature < 0 then
    if inst:HasTag("frozen") and not self.frozenfiremult then modifier = TUNING.PERISH_COLD_FROZEN_MULT                          -- 0
    else modifier = modifier * TUNING.PERISH_WINTER_MULT end                                                                     -- 0.75
end
if ambient_temperature > TUNING.OVERHEAT_TEMP then modifier = modifier * TUNING.PERISH_SUMMER_MULT end                           -- 1.25
modifier = modifier * self.localPerishMultiplyer
modifier = modifier * TUNING.PERISH_GLOBAL_MULT                                                                                  -- 1
...
self.perishremainingtime = math.min(self.perishtime, self.perishremainingtime - dt * modifier - additional_decay)
```

它同时驱动热食物的冷却：

```lua
if inst.components.edible ~= nil and inst.components.edible.temperaturedelta > 0 and not (owner ~= nil and owner:HasTag("nocool")) then
    if owner ~= nil and owner:HasTag("fridge") then inst.components.edible:AddChill(1)
    elseif ambient_temperature < TUNING.OVERHEAT_TEMP - 5 then inst.components.edible:AddChill(.25) end
end
```

地面 1.5×、冰箱 0.5×、冰柜冷冻 0×、冬季空气 0.75×、夏季空气 1.25×、潮湿 1.3× —— 全是乘法的，所以一颗冻湿的浆果在冬天是 `1.3 × 0.75`。简单、可组合，而且每个因子对一个学到"冰箱和冬天有用"的玩家来说都是可读的。

---

## 11. 农业

### 11.1 旧系统：`components/crop.lua`（290 行）+ `components/grower.lua`（204 行）

连续 0→1 进度，每 2 秒 tick 一次：

```lua
-- components/crop.lua:13
self.product_prefab = nil
self.growthpercent = 0
self.rate = 1 / 120           -- 默认：120 秒成熟
self.matured = false

function Crop:StartGrowing(prod, grow_time, grower, percent)
    self.product_prefab = prod
    self.rate = 1 / grow_time
    self.growthpercent = math.clamp(percent or 0, 0, 1)
    self.inst.AnimState:SetPercent("grow", self.growthpercent)
    self.grower = grower
    local dt = 2
    self.task = self.inst:DoPeriodicTask(dt, _DoGrow, nil, self, dt)
end
```

```lua
function Crop:DoGrow(dt, nowither)
    if not self.inst:HasTag("withered") and self.growthpercent < 1 then
        local shouldgrow = nowither or not TheWorld.state.isnight
        if not shouldgrow then
            -- night: only grow if within 70% of a light source's radius
            local x, y, z = self.inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
            for i, v in ipairs(TheSim:FindEntities(x, 0, z, TUNING.DAYLIGHT_SEARCH_RANGE, CANGROW_TAGS)) do
                local lightrad = v.Light:GetCalculatedRadius() * .7
                if v:GetDistanceSqToPoint(x, y, z) < lightrad * lightrad then shouldgrow = true break end
            end
        end
        if shouldgrow then
            local temp_rate = self:GetWorldGrowthRateMultiplier()
            self.growthpercent = math.clamp(self.growthpercent + dt * self.rate * temp_rate, 0, 1)
            self.cantgrowtime = 0
        else
            self.cantgrowtime = self.cantgrowtime + dt
            if self.cantgrowtime > TUNING.CROP_DARK_WITHER_TIME and self.inst.components.witherable ~= nil then
                self.inst.components.witherable:ForceWither()
                if self.inst:HasTag("withered") then return false end
            end
        end
        if self.growthpercent < 1 then self.inst.AnimState:SetPercent("grow", self.growthpercent)
        else self.inst.AnimState:PlayAnimation("grow_pst"); self:Mature(); ... end
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function Crop:GetWorldGrowthRateMultiplier()
    if TheWorld.state.temperature < TUNING.MIN_CROP_GROW_TEMP then return 0 end            -- 5 °C
    if TheWorld.state.israining and self.inst.components.rainimmunity == nil then
        return 1 + TUNING.CROP_RAIN_BONUS * TheWorld.state.precipitationrate                -- 最多 4×
    end
    if TheWorld.state.isspring then return 1 + TUNING.SPRING_GROWTH_MODIFIER / 3 end        -- 1.25×
    return 1
end
```

三道环境闸门：**太冷 = 不生长（0×），下雨 = 最多快 4×，春天 = 1.25×，黑暗 = 在 `CROP_DARK_WITHER_TIME`（1.5 天）后枯萎。** 生长百分比被直接喂给 `AnimState:SetPercent("grow", ...)`，所以精灵图就是一条活的进度条。

肥料转化为进度：

```lua
function Crop:Fertilize(fertilizer, doer)
    if not (TheWorld.state.iswinter and TheWorld.state.temperature <= 0) then
        if fertilizer.components.fertilizer ~= nil then
            self.growthpercent = math.clamp(self.growthpercent + fertilizer.components.fertilizer.fertilizervalue * self.rate, 0, 1)
        end
        ...
    end
end
```

注意：**在冰冻的冬天禁止施肥**。`fertilizervalue` 以*生长秒数*为单位计量，所以要乘 `self.rate`。

收获：

```lua
function Crop:Harvest(harvester)
    if self.matured or self.inst:HasTag("withered") then
        local product = nil
        if (self.grower ~= nil and self.grower.components.burnable ~= nil and self.grower.components.burnable:IsBurning())
           or (self.inst.components.burnable ~= nil and self.inst.components.burnable:IsBurning()) then
            local temp = SpawnPrefab(self.product_prefab)
            product = SpawnPrefab(temp.components.cookable ~= nil and temp.components.cookable.product or "seeds_cooked")
            temp:Remove()          -- ★ 收获一株燃烧中的作物会产出**熟**版本
        else product = SpawnPrefab(self.product_prefab) end
        ...
        product.components.inventoryitem:InheritWorldWetnessAtTarget(self.inst)
        harvester.components.inventory:GiveItem(product, nil, self.inst:GetPosition())
        ...
    end
end
```

`MakeWithered()` 会设 `product_prefab = "cutgrass"` —— 枯萎的作物收获出来就是草。**没有什么东西是死胡同。**

`grower.lua`（农田）追踪 `crops`、`croppoints`，以及作为 `cycles_left` / `max_cycles_left = 6` 的**土壤肥力**，用属性设置器驱动 `infertile`/`fertile`/`fullfertile` 标签。`Grower:Fertilize(obj, doer)` 增加 `obj.components.fertilizer.soil_cycles`。

### 11.2 新系统：`components/growable.lua`（369 行）—— 一个通用的阶段机

```lua
local Growable = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.stages = nil
    self.stage = 1
    self.pausereasons = {}
    --self.loopstages = false
    --self.loopstages_start = 1
    --self.growonly = false
    --self.springgrowth = false
    --self.growoffscreen = false
    --self.magicgrowable = false
end)

function Growable:StartGrowing(time)
    self.usetimemultiplier = false
    if #self.stages == 0 then print("... Trying to grow without setting the stages table...") return end
    if self.stage > #self.stages then return end
    self:StopGrowing()
    if time == nil then
        local stagedata = self:GetCurrentStageData()
        local timefn = stagedata ~= nil and stagedata.time or nil
        if timefn ~= nil then time = timefn(self.inst, self.stage, stagedata)
        else time = FALLBACK_GROWTH_TIME end           -- 10
        self.usetimemultiplier = stagedata ~= nil and stagedata.multiplier
    end
    if time == nil then return end
    if self.springgrowth then time = SpringGrowthMod(math.max(0, time)) end
    self:StartGrowingTask(time)
end

function Growable:DoGrowth(skipgrownfn)
    if self.targettime == nil and self.pausedremaining == nil then return false end
    local stage = self:GetNextStage()
    local stagedata = self.stages[stage]
    if stagedata ~= nil and stagedata.pregrowfn ~= nil then stagedata.pregrowfn(self.inst, stage, stagedata) end
    if not self.growonly then self:SetStage(stage) end
    if self.inst:IsValid() then
        if not skipgrownfn and stagedata ~= nil and stagedata.growfn ~= nil then stagedata.growfn(self.inst, stage, stagedata) end
        if (self.stage < #self.stages) or self.loopstages then self:StartGrowing() else self:StopGrowing() end
    end
    return true
end
```

一个阶段是一张表 `{ name, time = fn(inst, stage, stagedata), fn, pregrowfn, growfn, multiplier, ... }`。`time` 返回 `nil` 会**完全停止生长** —— 一个阶段可以声明自己是条件性的。`Pause(reason)` / `Resume(reason)` 按 reason 引用计数，所以"因为冬天暂停"和"因为没人照看暂停"不会互相踩踏。`OnEntitySleep` 取消任务并记录 `sleeptime`；`OnEntityWake` 调用 `LongUpdate(dt)` 来追赶 —— **离屏的植物仍然生长，但不消耗任何计时器槽。**（实测 `components/growable.lua`：第 155 行 `Pause`、第 177 行 `Resume`、第 273 行 `LongUpdate`、第 313 行 `OnEntitySleep`、第 324 行 `OnEntityWake`、第 6 行 `FALLBACK_GROWTH_TIME = 10`。）

### 11.3 农场植物 —— `prefabs/farm_plants.lua`（937）+ `prefabs/farm_plant_defs.lua`（360）

生长时间被写成 min/max 对，然后分摊到各阶段：

```lua
-- prefabs/farm_plant_defs.lua:20
local function MakeGrowTimes(germination_min, germination_max, full_grow_min, full_grow_max)
    local grow_time = {}
    grow_time.seed   = {germination_min, germination_max}              -- germination
    grow_time.sprout = {full_grow_min * 0.5, full_grow_max * 0.5}      -- 总时长的 50%
    grow_time.small  = {full_grow_min * 0.3, full_grow_max * 0.3}      -- 30%
    grow_time.med    = {full_grow_min * 0.2, full_grow_max * 0.2}      -- 20%
    grow_time.full     = 4 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME                     -- 可收获窗口
    grow_time.oversized= 6 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME
    grow_time.regrow    = {4 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME, 5 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME}
    return grow_time
end

PLANT_DEFS.carrot.grow_time = MakeGrowTimes(12 * TUNING.SEG_TIME, 16 * TUNING.SEG_TIME, 4 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME, 7 * TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME)
```

所以一根胡萝卜在 6–8 分钟内发芽，然后用 4–7 *天*生长，按 50/30/20 分到 sprout/small/med，然后保持可收获 4 天，再进入 oversized 阶段 6 天。

每种植物的数据是声明式的：

```lua
PLANT_DEFS.carrot.moisture    = {drink_rate = drink_low,  min_percent = TUNING.FARM_PLANT_DROUGHT_TOLERANCE}   -- 0.10
PLANT_DEFS.tomato.moisture    = {drink_rate = drink_high, min_percent = TUNING.FARM_PLANT_DROUGHT_TOLERANCE}
PLANT_DEFS.carrot.good_seasons = {autumn = true, winter = true, spring = true}
PLANT_DEFS.watermelon.good_seasons = {spring = true, summer = true}
```

配套 `FARM_PLANT_DRINK_LOW = -0.0075`、`_MED = -0.0200`、`_HIGH = -0.0350`（每秒土壤湿度），`FARM_PLANT_KILLJOY_RADIUS = 6`。

### 11.4 压力系统 —— `components/farmplantstress.lua`（149 行）

七个独立评估的压力源，按名字注册：

```lua
-- prefabs/farm_plants.lua:855
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("nutrients",    NutrientsStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("moisture",     MoistureStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("killjoys",     KillJoyStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("family",       FamilyStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("overcrowding", OvercrowdingStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("season",       SeasonStressTest)
inst.components.farmplantstress:AddStressCategory("happiness",    HappinessStressTest, on_happiness_changed)
```

```lua
-- prefabs/farm_plants.lua:173
local function KillJoyStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    local x, y, z = inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    return #TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, TUNING.FARM_PLANT_KILLJOY_RADIUS, {"farm_plant_killjoy"}) > inst.plant_def.max_killjoys_tolerance
end
local function FamilyStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    local x, y, z = inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    local num_plants = inst.plant_def.family_min_count > 0
        and #TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, inst.plant_def.family_check_dist, {inst.plant_def.plant_type_tag}, POLLEN_SOURCE_NOT_TAGS) or 0
    return num_plants < inst.plant_def.family_min_count          -- 需要同类邻居
end
local function OvercrowdingStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    local ents = TheWorld.Map:GetEntitiesOnTileAtPoint(inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition())
    local count = 0
    for i = 1, #ents do
        if ents[i]:HasTag("farm_plant") then
            count = count + 1
            if count > TUNING.FARM_PANT_OVERCROWDING_MAX_PLANTS then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end
local function SeasonStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    return not inst.plant_def.good_seasons[TheWorld.state.season]
end
local function MoistureStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    local moisture = inst.components.farmsoildrinker
    if moisture then return moisture:CalcPercentTimeHydrated() < inst.plant_def.moisture.min_percent end
    return false
end
local function NutrientsStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply)
    local x, y, z = inst.Transform:GetWorldPosition()
    if TheWorld.components.farming_manager then
        return TheWorld.components.farming_manager:CycleNutrientsAtPoint(x, y, z, inst.plant_def.nutrient_consumption, inst.plant_def.nutrient_restoration, not apply)
    end
    return true
end
local function HappinessStressTest(inst, currentstress, apply) return currentstress end   -- 由玩家交互设置
```

**在检查点打分，而不是连续评估** —— 每个阶段切换上挂 `pregrowfn = function(inst) MakeStressCheckpoint(inst) end`：

```lua
-- prefabs/farm_plants.lua:223
local function MakeStressCheckpoint(inst, is_final_stage)
    inst.components.farmplantstress:SetStressed("killjoys",     KillJoyStressTest(inst, nil, true))
    inst.components.farmplantstress:SetStressed("family",       FamilyStressTest(inst, nil, true))
    inst.components.farmplantstress:SetStressed("overcrowding", OvercrowdingStressTest(inst, nil, true))
    inst.components.farmplantstress:SetStressed("season",       SeasonStressTest(inst, nil, true))
    ... moisture:Reset() 在读取之后 ...
    inst.components.farmplantstress:SetStressed("nutrients",    NutrientsStressTest(inst, nil, true))
    -- happiness is update via an action callback
    inst.components.farmplantstress:MakeCheckpoint()
    if is_final_stage then inst.components.farmplantstress:CalcFinalStressState() end
    inst.scale = math.random() * 0.07 + (inst.components.farmplantstress.checkpoint_stress_points <= 0.5 * inst.components.farmplantstress.num_stressors and 0.93 or 0.86)
end
```

```lua
-- components/farmplantstress.lua:72
function FarmPlantStress:CalcFinalStressState()
    local stress = self.stress_points
    self.final_stress_state = stress <= 1  and FARM_PLANT_STRESS.NONE       -- allow one mistake
                            or stress <= 6  and FARM_PLANT_STRESS.LOW        -- one and half categories can fail
                            or stress <= 11 and FARM_PLANT_STRESS.MODERATE    -- almost 3 categories can fail
                            or FARM_PLANT_STRESS.HIGH                         -- you aren't even trying now, are you?
    return self.final_stress_state
end
```

压力点跨约 5 个检查点 × 7 个类别 = 最多 35 个累积。每个压力源在**每个**检查点读完后都会被**重置为有压力**（`MakeCheckpoint` 在读取后执行 `self.stressors[stressor] = true`），所以你必须**反复**满足它，而不是一次。`CalcFinalStressState` 里的注释就是设计文档。

注意**检查里内建的信息不对称**：

```lua
-- components/farmplantstress.lua:90
function FarmPlantStress:GetStressDescription(viewer)
    ...
    local stressors = {}
    for stressor, testfn in pairs(self.stressors_testfns) do
        if testfn(self.inst, self.stressors[stressor], false) then table.insert(stressors, stressor) end
    end
    if #stressors == 0 then return GetString(viewer, "DESCRIBE_PLANTHAPPY")
    elseif viewer:HasTag("plantkin") or (viewer.replica.inventory and viewer.replica.inventory:EquipHasTag("detailedplanthappiness")) then
        local stressor = shuffleArray(stressors)[1]
        return GetString(viewer, "DESCRIBE_PLANTSTRESSOR"..string.upper(stressor))   -- 只点名一个压力源
    else
        if #stressors >= 5 then return GetString(viewer, "DESCRIBE_PLANTVERYSTRESSED")
        else return GetString(viewer, "DESCRIBE_PLANTSTRESSED") end                  -- 含糊其辞
    end
end
```

Wormwood（`plantkin`）或一顶特定的帽子会告诉你它*具体*有什么问题；其他人只知道"有压力"或"压力很大"。**知识就是装备。**

配套组件：`farmplantable.lua`（32 行，种子 → 植物）、`farmplanttendable.lua`（43 行，"照料/让它开心"的交互）、`farmsoildrinker.lua`（土壤湿度）、`farmtiller.lua`，以及 `components/farming_manager.lua`（一个*世界*组件，持有共享的按地块养分池 —— `CycleNutrientsAtPoint(x,y,z, consumption, restoration, dryRun)`，`not apply` 标志让测试运行可以模拟而不改动实际状态）。

## 12. 驯服 —— `components/domesticatable.lua`（264 行）

### 12.1 三个独立的计量

```lua
-- components/domesticatable.lua:12
local Domesticatable = Class(function(self, inst)
    -- I feel like it would be much cleaner to break domestication and obedience into two components, but they
    -- use a lot of the same hooks so I'm keeping them together for now.
    self.domesticated = false
    inst:AddTag("domesticatable")
    self.domestication = 0            -- 0..1，趋向驯化的半永久进度
    self.domestication_latch = false
    self.lastdomesticationgain = 0
    self.domestication_triggerfn = nil
    self.obedience = 0                -- 0..maxobedience，衰减很快，门控骑乘/上鞍
    self.minobedience = 0
    self.maxobedience = 1
    self.domesticationdecaypaused = false
    self.tendencies = {}              -- ORNERY / RIDER / PUDGY 人格累积
    self.decaytask = nil
end, nil, { domesticated = ondomesticated })
```

- **`domestication`**（0–1）：缓慢、基本永久的进度。到 1.0 时设 `domestication_latch`；真正的 `domesticated` 标志在下一次喂食（`TryBecomeDomesticated`）时才翻转。
- **`obedience`**（0–1）：快速衰减，门控可上鞍性与甩人。
- **`tendencies`**：决定成年体型的三个人格累积器。

### 12.2 衰减/增长 tick

```lua
-- components/domesticatable.lua:3
local DECAY_TASK_PERIOD = 10
local OBEDIENCE_DECAY_RATE = -1/(TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME * 2)      -- 2 天掉满服从度
local FEEDBACK_DECAY_RATE  = -1/(TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME * 45)     -- 人格倾向 45 天淡出

local function CalculateLoss(currenttime, lastgaintime)
    -- you don't lose full domestication right away, only after ignoring the critter for a while
    local delta = currenttime-lastgaintime
    local ratio = math.min(delta/(TUNING.BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_MAX_LOSS_DAYS*TUNING.TOTAL_DAY_TIME), 1.0)
    return TUNING.BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_LOSE_DOMESTICATION * ratio
end

local function UpdateDomestication(inst, self)
    if not self.domesticationdecaypaused then
        for k,v in pairs(self.tendencies) do
            self.tendencies[k] = math.max(v + FEEDBACK_DECAY_RATE * DECAY_TASK_PERIOD, 0)
        end
    end
    -- obedience still decays even if domestication decay is paused
    DoDeltaObedience(self, OBEDIENCE_DECAY_RATE * DECAY_TASK_PERIOD)

    if self.domestication_triggerfn(inst) then
        self.lastdomesticationgain = GetTime()
        DoDeltaDomestication(self, TUNING.BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_GAIN_DOMESTICATION * DECAY_TASK_PERIOD)
    elseif self.domesticationdecaypaused then
        self.lastdomesticationgain = self.lastdomesticationgain + DECAY_TASK_PERIOD   -- 把时钟往前推
    else
        DoDeltaDomestication(self, CalculateLoss(GetTime(), self.lastdomesticationgain))
    end

    self:CheckForChanges()
    self:Validate()
end
```

每 10 秒一次。**衰减斜坡**是人道化的部分：`BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_LOSE_DOMESTICATION = -1/(480*4)`（即满速 4 天掉光），但速率会被 `min(忽略天数 / BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_MAX_LOSS_DAYS(10), 1)` 缩放。**忽略一天几乎不掉什么；忽略 10 天以上就满速衰减。** 不会惩罚下线玩家。

`domesticationdecaypaused` 在牛羊牛被舔盐或睡眠时设置（`prefabs/beefalo.lua:923`），暂停时代码会*推进* `lastdomesticationgain`，所以暂停不算"忽视"。

闸门：

```lua
-- prefabs/beefalo.lua:724
local function DomesticationTriggerFn(inst)
    return (inst.components.hunger:GetPercent() > 0 and not inst.components.hunger:IsPaused())
        or inst.components.rideable:IsBeingRidden() == true
end
```

增长率 `BEEFALO_DOMESTICATION_GAIN_DOMESTICATION = 1/(480*20)` → **喂养/骑乘 20 天**才能完全驯服。这是一个长周期目标，不是一个会话目标。

### 12.3 玩家交互（`prefabs/beefalo.lua`、`tuning.lua:898`）

| 行为 | 服从度 | 驯化度 | 倾向 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 喂食 | `+0.1` | — | — |
| 喂过头 | `−0.3` | `−0.01` | PUDGY `+0.02` |
| 刷毛 | `+0.4` | `+(1−15/20)/15` | — |
| 被玩家攻击 | `−1`（立即归零） | `−0.3` | ORNERY `+0.004` |
| 被任何东西攻击 | `−0.01` | `0` | ORNERY `+0.004` |
| 被剃毛 | `−1` | — | — |
| 饥饿 | `−1/天` | — | — |
| 喂得很饱 | — | — | PUDGY `+1/(480*5)` |
| 被骑乘 | — | — | RIDER `+1/(480*5)` |
| 攻击了某物 | — | — | ORNERY `+0.004` |

`Validate()` 是"彻底放弃"的检查：

```lua
function Domesticatable:Validate()
    if self.obedience <= self.minobedience and self.inst.components.hunger:GetPercent() <= 0 and self.domestication <= 0 then
        self:CancelTask(); return false
    end
    return true
end
```

而 `CheckForChanges()` 处理"变野"：

```lua
if self.inst.components.hunger:GetPercent() <= 0 and self.domestication <= 0 then
    self.tendencies = {}
    self.inst:PushEvent("goneferal", {domesticated = self.domesticated})
    if self.domesticated then self:SetDomesticated(false) end
end
```

### 12.4 倾向 → 体型

```lua
-- prefabs/beefalo.lua:565
local function SetTendency(inst, changedomestication)
    -- tendency is locked in after we become domesticated
    if not inst.components.domesticatable:IsDomesticated() then
        local tendencysum, maxtendency, maxtendencyval = 0, nil, 0
        for k, v in pairs(inst.components.domesticatable.tendencies) do
            tendencysum = tendencysum + v
            if v > maxtendencyval then maxtendencyval = v; maxtendency = k end
        end
        inst.tendency = (tendencysum < .1 or maxtendencyval * 2 < tendencysum) and TENDENCY.DEFAULT or maxtendency
    end
    ...
    if inst.components.domesticatable:IsDomesticated() then
        inst.components.domesticatable:SetMinObedience(TUNING.BEEFALO_MIN_DOMESTICATED_OBEDIENCE[inst.tendency])
        inst.components.combat:SetDefaultDamage(TUNING.BEEFALO_DAMAGE[inst.tendency])
        inst.components.locomotor.runspeed = TUNING.BEEFALO_RUN_SPEED[inst.tendency]
    else
        inst.components.domesticatable:SetMinObedience(0)
        inst.components.combat:SetDefaultDamage(TUNING.BEEFALO_DAMAGE.DEFAULT)
        inst.components.locomotor.runspeed = TUNING.BEEFALO_RUN_SPEED.DEFAULT
    end
end
```

`TENDENCY = {DEFAULT, ORNERY, RIDER, PUDGY}`（`constants.lua:2467`）。赢家必须持有**严格多数**（`maxtendencyval * 2 >= tendencysum`）*且*总和超过 0.1，否则为 DEFAULT。在驯化时永久锁定。每个倾向索引三张数值表 —— 伤害、跑速、最低服从度 —— 所以人格纯粹是数据。

### 12.5 骑乘门禁

```lua
inst.components.rideable:SetSaddleable(data.new >= TUNING.BEEFALO_SADDLEABLE_OBEDIENCE)   -- 0.1
inst.components.rideable:SetRequiredObedience(TUNING.BEEFALO_MIN_BUCK_OBEDIENCE)           -- 0.5

local function CalculateBuckDelay(inst)
    local domestication = inst.components.domesticatable:GetDomestication() or 0
    local moodmult      = fns.GetIsInMood(inst) and TUNING.BEEFALO_BUCK_TIME_MOOD_MULT or 1
    local beardmult     = (inst.components.beard ~= nil and inst.components.beard.bits == 0) and TUNING.BEEFALO_BUCK_TIME_NUDE_MULT or 1
    local domesticmult  = inst.components.domesticatable:IsDomesticated() and 1 or TUNING.BEEFALO_BUCK_TIME_UNDOMESTICATED_MULT
    local skillmult     = rider ~= nil and ... and TUNING.SKILLS.WATHGRITHR.WATHGRITHR_BEEFALO_BUCK_TIME_MOD or 1
    local basedelay = Remap(domestication, 0, 1, TUNING.BEEFALO_MIN_BUCK_TIME, TUNING.BEEFALO_MAX_BUCK_TIME)
    return basedelay * moodmult * beardmult * domesticmult * skillmult
end
```

进度阶梯：**服从度 ≥ 0.1 → 可上鞍；≥ 0.5 → 不会把你甩下来；驯化度 → 更长的甩人延迟。** 而一只被剃光的牛羊牛（`beard.bits == 0`）更容易甩人 —— 一个"薅羊毛惩罚"，而不是硬编码的"别这么干"规则。

`DeltaObedience`/`DeltaDomestication` 触发 `obediencedelta`/`domesticationdelta` 事件，`beefalo.lua` 监听 `obediencedelta` 来重跑 `SetSaddleable`。标准事件管线。

**`beefalo_clothing.lua`（469 行）纯粹是外观数据** —— `BEEFALO_CLOTHING = { beefalo_body_beast = { type = "beef_body", skin_tags = {...}, symbol_overrides = {...}, rarity = "Complimentary", release_group = 112 }, ... }`。功能性的鞍具代码在 `components/saddler.lua` / `components/rideable.lua` 以及娃娃/装饰物预制体里。

---

## 13. 服装与装备

### 13.1 槽位系统

```lua
-- constants.lua:652
EQUIPSLOTS = { HANDS = "hands", HEAD = "head", BODY = "body", BEARD = "beard" }
```

`equipslotutil.lua`（50 行）把它变成一个稳定的、用于网络同步的数字 ID 空间：

```lua
local function InitializeSlots()
    assert(EQUIPSLOT_NAMES == nil and EQUIPSLOT_IDS == nil, "Equip slots already initialized")
    EQUIPSLOT_NAMES = {}
    for k, v in orderedPairs(EQUIPSLOTS) do table.insert(EQUIPSLOT_NAMES, v) end
    EQUIPSLOT_COUNT = #EQUIPSLOT_NAMES
    assert(EQUIPSLOT_COUNT <= 63, "Too many equip slots!")
    -- NOTES(JBK): The orderedPairs iterator above forces this sort so it is deterministic for all platforms.
    -- I am reversing the sort so that coincidentally the names will be good for priorities when using deterministic checks.
    -- {"head", "hands", "body", "beard"} is the expected output.
    table.reverse_inplace(EQUIPSLOT_NAMES)
    EQUIPSLOT_IDS = table.invert(EQUIPSLOT_NAMES)
end
```

四点值得注意：初始化**只发生一次，在模组加载之后**（这样模组可以加槽位）；`orderedPairs` 保证跨平台确定性；63 槽上限是位掩码限制；而 `ApplyDamage` 里 `for slotid = 1, EquipSlot.Count()` 意味着护甲是按一个**确定性的优先级顺序**（头 → 手 → 身体 → 胡须）被检查的，这很重要，因为 `resistance` 的短路在第一个匹配处就返回。

### 13.2 `components/equippable.lua`（218 行）

```lua
local Equippable = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.isequipped = false
    self.equipslot = EQUIPSLOTS.HANDS
    self.onequipfn = nil
    self.onunequipfn = nil
    self.onpocketfn = nil
    self.onequiptomodelfn = nil
    self.equipstack = false
    self.walkspeedmult = nil
    --self.retrictedtag = nil --only entities with this tag can equip
    self.dapperness = 0
    self.dapperfn = nil
    self.insulated = false           -- electricity, not temperature
    self.equippedmoisture = 0
    self.maxequippedmoisture = 0
    -- self.preventunequipping = nil
    -- self.is_magic_dapperness
end, nil, { equipslot = onequipslot, walkspeedmult = onwalkspeedmult,
            restrictedtag = onrestrictedtag, preventunequipping = onpreventunequipping })
```

**没有"服装"组件。** 一个物品是不是服装是由*组合*决定的：`equippable`（槽位 + 回调）加上任意子集 `insulator`、`waterproofer`、`armor`、`heater`、`finiteuses`、`planardefense`、`damagereflect`、`setbonus`…… 装备系统本身只认识槽位和回调：

```lua
function Equippable:Equip(owner, from_ground)
    self.isequipped = true
    if self.inst.components.burnable ~= nil then self.inst.components.burnable:StopSmoldering() end
    if self.onequipfn ~= nil then self.onequipfn(self.inst, owner, from_ground) end
    self.inst:PushEvent("equipped", { owner = owner })
    if self.onequiptomodelfn ~= nil and owner:HasTag("equipmentmodel") then
        self.onequiptomodelfn(self.inst, owner, from_ground)
    end
end
```

`onequipfn` 是预制体接线一切的地方：`inst.components.insulator:SetInsulation(...)`、`owner.components.combat.externaldamagemultipliers:SetModifier(...)`、`owner.components.locomotor:SetExternalSpeedMultiplier(...)` 等等。**装备中的物品把它的效果推到拥有者身上；卸下时反转。** 任何地方都没有一个集中的"重算所有装备属性"的流程。

风度：

```lua
function Equippable:GetDapperness(owner, ignore_wetness)
    local dapperness = self.dapperness
    if self.flipdapperonmerms and owner and owner:HasTag("merm") then dapperness = -dapperness end
    if self.dapperfn ~= nil then dapperness = self.dapperfn(self.inst, owner) end
    if not ignore_wetness and self.inst:GetIsWet() then dapperness = dapperness + TUNING.WET_ITEM_DAPPERNESS end  -- -0.1
    return dapperness
end
```

速度：

```lua
function Equippable:GetWalkSpeedMult()
    local speed = self.walkspeedmult or 1.0
    local owner = self.inst.components.inventoryitem and self.inst.components.inventoryitem.owner
    if owner and self.isequipped then
        if speed < 1 and owner:HasTag("vigorbuff") then speed = math.min(1, speed + 0.25) end   -- 惩罚可被软化
        local speedmodifierfn = owner.inventory_EquippableWalkSpeedMultModifier
        if speedmodifierfn ~= nil then speed = speedmodifierfn(owner, speed, self.inst) end
    end
    return speed
end
```

大理石甲的 0.7 可以被活力 Buff 恢复到 0.95 —— **惩罚永远不是绝对的。**

限制（角色专属装备）：

```lua
function Equippable:IsRestricted(target)
    if not target:HasAnyTag("player", "possessedbody") then return false end
    local linkeditem = self.inst.components.linkeditem
    if linkeditem and linkeditem:IsEquippableRestrictedToOwner() then
        local owneruserid = linkeditem:GetOwnerUserID()
        if owneruserid and owneruserid ~= target.userid then return true end
    end
    return self.restrictedtag ~= nil and self.restrictedtag:len() > 0 and not target:HasTag(self.restrictedtag)
end
```

`IsRestricted_FromLoad` 是一个独立变体，它把技能树授予的标签加入白名单，好让加载 snapshot 时不会剥掉角色合法拥有的装备。

### 13.3 保暖叠加

`components/insulator.lua` 只有 31 行 —— 一个数值加一个季节类型：

```lua
local Insulator = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.insulation = 0
    self.type = SEASONS.WINTER
end)
function Insulator:SetSummer() self.type = SEASONS.SUMMER end
function Insulator:SetWinter() self.type = SEASONS.WINTER end
function Insulator:GetInsulation() return self.insulation, self:GetType() end
```

叠加完全发生在 `temperature.lua:GetInsulation()` 里：**对所有装备槽、按季节类型做纯加法求和**，然后钳制 `math.max(0, ...)`。胡须加到冬季保暖上，并从夏季保暖上*减*。遮蔽给夏季加 `INSULATION_MED_LARGE = 180`。黄昏加 60，夜晚加 120，只在洞穴外且只对夏季。

然后渐近阻尼器 `SEG_TIME/(SEG_TIME + I)` 把这个总和转成一个速率上限。具体来说，在冬天、环境 −20 °C、体温 35 °C（所以 `delta = -55`，始终受速率限制）的情况下，只用已核实的装备算：

| 装备 | 冬季保暖 | 速率上限 `30/(30+I)`（°C/s） | 35→0 耗时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 赤手空拳 | 0 | 1.000 | 35 秒 |
| 耳罩（HEAD，`SMALL`） | 60 | 0.333 | 105 秒 |
| 冬帽（HEAD，`MED`） | 120 | 0.200 | 175 秒 |
| 牛角帽（HEAD，`LARGE`） | 240 | 0.111 | 315 秒 |
| 牛角帽 + 冬季木纹背心（BODY，`LARGE`） | 480 | 0.059 | ~595 秒 ≈ 10 分钟 |
| …再加固有/胡须保暖 | 480+ | <0.059 | 更久 |

这张表就是**整个冬季难度曲线**，由两行算术生成。注意 HEAD 位的装备是互斥的 —— 你选耳罩*或*冬帽*或*牛角帽 —— 所以分级是按槽位的，叠加只发生在跨槽位时。（遮蔽/黄昏/夜晚加成只作用于**夏季**保暖，所以在这里帮不上忙。）

### 13.4 防水叠加

`components/waterproofer.lua`（30 行）：

```lua
local WaterProofer = Class(function(self, inst)
    self.effectiveness = 1
    inst:AddTag("waterproofer")
    if inst.components.inventoryitem ~= nil then inst.components.inventoryitem:EnableMoisture(false) end
end)
function WaterProofer:GetEffectiveness() return self.effectiveness end
```

由 `inventory:GetWaterproofness()` 求和，而它本身是 `moisture:_GetMoistureRateAssumingRain()` 里四项可加项之一（遮蔽 + 物品栏 + 固有 + `SourceModifierList`），钳制到 `[0,1]`，然后 `速率 × (1 − 防水度)`。**恰好达到 1.0 意味着完全免疫雨天**并短路。

注意与保暖的不对称：防水度是一个**硬顶在 1 的线性求和**，而保暖是一个**喂给饱和曲线的线性求和**。不同的手感对应不同的玩法目标 —— 雨具是二元的"我干了吗？"，寒具是渐变的"我还剩多久？"。

### 13.5 其他装备派生属性

其余一切都是**在使用点**上对 `equipslots` 的扫描，不是缓存的聚合值：

```lua
-- inventory.lua:368
function Inventory:IsWearingArmor()
    for k, v in pairs(self.equipslots) do if v.components.armor ~= nil then return true end end
end
function Inventory:ArmorHasTag(tag) ...
function Inventory:EquipHasTag(tag) ...
function Inventory:EquipHasSpDefenseForType(sptype) ...
function Inventory:IsInsulated()      -- from electricity, not temperature
    if self.force_no_insulation then return false end
    for k,v in pairs(self.equipslots) do if v and v.components.equippable:IsInsulated() then return true end end
    return self.isexternallyinsulated:Get()
end
function Inventory:GetEquippedMoistureRate(slot)   -- 求和 equippable:GetEquippedMoisture()
function Inventory:GetWaterproofness(slot)         -- 求和 waterproofer:GetEffectiveness()
```

只有 4 个槽位时，遍历比维护一个脏聚合值**更便宜** —— 而且这意味着装备效果永远正确，不需要任何失效逻辑。理智对风度、体温对保暖和取暖物做同样的事。**这是一个刻意且一致的架构选择：读时推导，绝不缓存。**

---

## 14. 生存游戏的关键模式

十二条值得抄的模式，附上它们各自值得存在的理由。

**1. `SourceModifierList` —— 带键、自清理的修饰器袋。**
`util/sourcemodifierlist.lua`，185 行（实测 `wc -l` = 184 行，最后一行为 `return SourceModifierList`）。`SetModifier(source, value, key)` / `RemoveModifier(source, key)` / `Get()`，用 `multiply`、`additive` 或 `boolean` 折叠。若 `source` 是一个实体，它会自动订阅 `onremove` 并自我清理。
*为什么：* 生存游戏里 buff/debuff bug 的第一大来源就是"buff 过期了但倍率还留着"。按来源打键让泄漏在结构上变得**不可能**。被生命吸收、饥饿 burnrate、理智光环、战斗伤害、体温保暖、湿度防水、易腐品、护甲耐久损耗、走速共用。一个类，约 15 个系统。

**2. `Class(...)` 的属性设置器表 = 免费的网络同步。**
每个组件都传第三个参数，把字段名映射到 `on*` 处理器：`{ currenthealth = oncurrenthealth, maxhealth = onmaxhealth, penalty = onpenalty }`。
*为什么：* **设置器本身就是网络同步。** 你不可能忘记给一个字段打脏标记，因为赋值是改变它的唯一方式。它也是挂载标签更新的天然位置（`onmatured` 加/移除 `readyforharvest`），所以实体标签永远不会与组件状态脱节。

**3. 基于速率的积分 + `LongUpdate(dt)` 追赶钩子。**
每个计量表都从世界算出 `self.rate`，然后做 `DoDelta(rate * dt)`。每个组件还实现了 `LongUpdate(dt)` 用于离线/快进，以及 `OnEntitySleep`/`OnEntityWake` 用于离屏追赶。
*为什么：* 从一种形状里免费获得暂停、时间缩放、睡眠、存读档、以及多人 desync 恢复。`hunger.lua:LongUpdate` 调用 `DoDec(dt, true)` —— `ignore_damage` 标志意味着**离线时间能消耗你但永远杀不死你**，这是一条用一个布尔量实现的仁慈规则。

**4. 边缘触发事件 + 基于电平的状态。**
`temperature.lua:SetTemperature` 在推 `startfreezing`/`stopfreezing` 之前比较 `(self.current < 0) ~= (last < 0)`。`hunger.lua:SetCurrent` 对 `startstarving` 做同样的事。`armor.lua` 监听 `percentusedchange` 并在 0 时推 `armorbroke`。
*为什么：* 消费者（屏幕特效、台词、音效）要的是**转变**；模拟要的是**电平**。从设置器同时发出两者，意味着没有消费者需要跟踪前值，也没有监听者被刷屏。

**5. `healthtrigger.lua` —— 41 行的通用阈值响应器。**
`AddTrigger(percent, fn)`，在任何发生穿越的 `healthdelta` 上触发，排序后保证多阈值暴跌时按高→低触发。
*为什么：* "Boss 在 50% 狂暴"、"角色低于 25% 跛行"、"屏幕在 15% 变红"都是同一个问题。把它做成一个小型事件驱动组件，而不是散落的 `if health < X` 检查，能把这些检查挪出热路径并把阈值变成数据。

**6. 护甲：减伤取 MAX，耐久按比例分摊。**
`inventory:ApplyDamage` —— `absorbed_percent = math.max(...)` 但 `armor_damage[armor] = absorbed_damage * amt / total_absorption`。
*为什么：* 它以玩家直觉期望的方式回答了"护甲叠加吗？" —— 穿两件让它们*都比一件单独穿时消耗得慢*，但你不会变得无敌。它还天然地让廉价护甲成为昂贵护甲的**耐久电池**。

**7. 渐近属性饱和：`SEG_TIME / (SEG_TIME + stat)`。**
`temperature.lua:DoDelta` 以及 `self.rate` 钳制。
*为什么：* 保暖可以无限叠加却永远达不到免疫，所以装备分级始终有意义，而且不存在"打到顶就不在乎了"的临界点。同样的形状适用于护甲、抗性、冷却缩减、移速。**一个公式，顶端无特例。**

**8. 不对称的恢复速率。**
`THAW_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 5` 对比 `WARM_DEGREES_PER_SEC = 1`；保暖只抵抗*危险*方向；干燥速率随 `inExpo(moisture)` 缩放；驯化度衰减有 10 天宽限期。
*为什么：* 这是生存游戏里最重要的手感决定。**严厉下滑 + 快速恢复 = 有张力而不挫败。** 这每一条都是"陷入麻烦很容易，但一旦你采取行动，脱离很快" —— 而且它永远是一个你能调的常数，不是你需要构建的系统。

**9. 用标签而非类型继承来做空间查询玩法。**
`TheSim:FindEntities(x, y, z, radius, mustTags, cantTags)` 配合 `{ "HASHEATER" }`、`{ "sanityaura" }`、`{ "prototyper" }`、`{ "_combat" }`、`{ "farm_plant_killjoy" }`。规则是 `inst:HasTag("playerghost")`、`item:HasTag("edible_MEAT")`、`target:HasTag("alwaysblock")`。
*为什么：* 加一个新的热源 = `inst:AddTag("HASHEATER")` 加一个 `heater` 组件 —— 对 `temperature.lua` **零改动**。没有 `isFood()` 函数要更新，没有基类要继承。这就是为什么一个十年历史的代码库、800+ 预制体，依然可扩展。（全库 `TheSim:FindEntities` 在 Lua 侧被调用 **955** 次，实测 `grep -rn | wc -l`。）

**10. 物品用组合而非继承 —— `equippable` + 可选的属性组件。**
没有 `Clothing` 类。一顶冬帽是 `equippable(HEAD)` + `insulator(240, WINTER)` + `finiteuses`，你愿意的话再加 `armor`。一件雨衣是 `equippable(BODY)` + `waterproofer(0.7)` + `insulator(60, WINTER)`。
*为什么：* 跨切面装备（保暖的护甲、反射伤害的帽子、同时是容器的背包）在生存游戏里是常态，而类继承处理得**很糟**。DST 通过在使用点扫描 4 个装备槽（`IsWearingArmor`、`GetInsulation`、`GetWaterproofness`、风度）来推导一切 —— **从不缓存，从不失效**，而这很便宜因为只有 4 个槽。

**11. 数据驱动的表 + `test`/`priority`/`weight` 处理组合系统。**
烹饪：`preparedfoods.lua` 是一张扁平表 `{ test = fn(cooker, names, tags), priority, weight, health, hunger, sanity, perishtime, cooktime }`，由 `GetCandidateRecipes` → 优先级去重 → 加权随机匹配，以 `wetgoop` 作为 `return true` / `priority = -10` 的兜底。制作：`Recipe2(name, {Ingredient(...)}, TECH.X, config)`，配按轴的科技向量由 `CanPrototypeRecipe` 比较。
*为什么：* 两个系统都需要"N 种输入 → M 种输出之一，且可被模组扩展"。谓词 + 优先级 + 权重这个三元组优雅地处理歧义（多个有效配方 → 最高层内加权随机）并保证全函数性（`priority = -10` 兜底意味着锅永远不会失败）。加一道菜是一张表条目；加一个可制作物是一行。

**12. 分数/加权标签而非布尔分类。**
`AddIngredientValues({"berries"}, {fruit=.5})`、`({"meat"}, {meat=1})`、`({"royal_jelly"}, {sweetener=3})`、`({"tallbirdegg"}, {egg=4})`。理智光环按"每天多少秒的满理智"缩放。热量按 `calories_per_day = 75` 的分数表达。
*为什么：* 布尔标签强迫你在每个配方测试里写 `names.berries or names.berries_juicy or names.fig or ...`，并导致组合爆炸。加权标签让一个配方可以写 `tags.fruit >= 2` 并且无论是 2 个石榴还是 4 颗浆果都表示同一件事。而把调参常数表达成*人类单位*（"这个光环每天抽 320 理智"、"这份食物是半天热量"）让数字**自文档化**且容易平衡得多。

**附加 —— 三个小但性价比极高的技巧：**

- **物品上的 `prevslot`/`prevcontainer` 撤销记忆**（`inventory.lua:1062`）：约 20 行让容器 juggling 变得**顺手**。
- **引用计数的"别碰我"集合**：`combat.shouldavoidaggro`（`Set`/`Remove` 增减计数）、`moisture.forcedrysources`、`growable.pausereasons`、`sanity.inducedinsanity_sources`、`prototyper.doers`。**任何时候两个独立系统都可能抑制同一个行为，就引用计数它** —— 否则"一个过期了把另一个的也取消了"这个 bug 是必然发生的。
- **`baseauraname` 人群去重**（`sanityaura.lua:20`）："20 头猪 = 1 头猪的光环"。没有它，在任何兽群旁边理智都变得不可玩。实现上复用一个模块级 scratchpad 数组，因为它每帧跑很多次 —— 一个罕见的、优化被写进注释里的例子。

---

## 15. 文件索引

| 文件 | 行数 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `components/health.lua` | 665 | 生命值计量。`DoDelta` 伤害管线（非致死地板 → redirect → 无敌 → % 吸收 → 扁平减免 → `deltamodifierfn` → 单次上限）、`SetVal` 死亡检测、惩罚（上限生命缩水，上限 0.75）、回血（`StartRegen` / 带键的 `AddRegenSource`）、带速率上限的火焰伤害、月相燃烧标志。 |
| `components/healthtrigger.lua` | 41 | 通用双向百分比阈值响应器，纯由 `healthdelta` 驱动。带排序的触发顺序。 |
| `components/armor.lua` | 177 | 护甲物品：`condition`/`maxcondition`、`absorb_percent`、`InitIndestructible`、带标签门控的 `CanResist`、`AddWeakness` 额外耐久伤害、带 `conditionlossmultipliers` 的 `TakeDamage`、`armorbroke` 事件。 |
| `components/hunger.lua` | 210 | 饥饿度计量。1 秒 `DoPeriodicTask`；消耗 = `hungerrate × burnrate × burnratemodifiers`；到 0 后通过 `hurtrate` 抽生命；`Pause`/`Resume`；`overridestarvefn`；`LongUpdate` 忽略伤害。 |
| `components/eater.lua` | 371 | 饮食（`caneat`/`preferseating` 食物组 → `edible_*` 标签）、`Eat()`（每属性吸收 × foodmemory × 堆叠乘数）、`DoFoodEffects` 负面效果门控、`PrefersToEat` 与 `CanEat` 的分离、强胃 / 生肉 / 耐腐容差。 |
| `components/edible.lua` | 261 | 食物数值（生命/饥饿/理智）、foodtype + secondaryfoodtype、腐败乘数（`stale_*`、`spoiled_*`）、香料乘数、`temperaturedelta`/`duration`/`chill`（食物即隐式取暖器）、`badfood` 标签、`HandleEatRemove`。 |
| `components/foodmemory.lua` | ~90 | Warly 式递减收益：在 `TOTAL_DAY_TIME` 内记住每种食物，返回 `mults[min(#mults, count)]`。 |
| `components/sanity.lua` | 619 | 理智值计量。`Recalc(dt)` 把风度 + 湿度 + 光照 + 光环 + 幽灵流失 + 外部修饰器加总成一个 `rate`；`RATE_SCALE` 分桶；迟滞阈值（15% / 17.5%）；INSANITY 与 LUNACY 模式；光环免疫/吸收/乘数 `SourceModifierList`。 |
| `components/sanityaura.lua` | 35 | 光环来源：`.aura` 常量 / `.aurafn(inst, observer)` / `.fallofffn`（默认平方反比）/ `.max_distsq`（默认 10²）/ `.baseauraname` 人群去重。加上 `sanityaura` 标签。 |
| `components/sanityauraadjuster.lua` | 28 | 周期性（1 秒）推送给一个被追踪玩家列表的理智调整 —— 用于那些不是距离光环的区域效果。 |
